# v2026.9.6

OpenClaw 2026.9.6 adds background tasks from the command palette and more installed coding agents you can use in chat, giving you more ways to start work without leaving OpenClaw. You can ask your agent to create a personal theme, follow notes that update during a meeting, and return to Telegram group history after a restart or chat reset. Update recovery and task status also give clearer answers about what finished and what still needs attention.

**Release scale:** 1,699 pull requests, 45 direct commits, and 243 contributors.

## Installation and Onboarding

Setup has clearer recovery paths when an installation cannot continue, and new custom agents keep the job you approved for them.

<AccordionGroup>

<Accordion title="Install and find command-line tools on Windows">

The Windows installer can continue after a failed Node package-manager attempt, trying the remaining options before downloading an official portable runtime into your user account. That portable recovery needs no administrator access, while setup still stops if it cannot validate a supported runtime. OpenClaw also recognizes supported JavaScript command-line launchers configured by their full path without requiring changes to Windows' executable-extension settings. See the [installer guide](/install/installer) for setup options.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Repair stale Winget Node registrations during Windows setup. [76cf13b](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/76cf13b65b5040c79ac94294f8569af120253eef) Thanks @ooiuuii and @fuller-stack-dev.
- Continue Windows setup after failed Node package-manager attempts. [#134386](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/134386) Thanks @ly85206559 and @fuller-stack-dev.
- Verify supported explicit Windows JavaScript CLI paths without PATHEXT changes, while bare command names retain normal PATH/PATHEXT lookup. [#152862](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152862)

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Choose a supported FreeBSD installation">

On FreeBSD, an unsupported source installation now stops before changing your existing installation and points you to the [package installation route](/install/installer#install-clish). Use a published npm version or compatible built `.tgz` package with the same installation prefix. If pkg or Ports manages OpenClaw, continue using that package manager. Source installation remains unsupported, and this change does not make FreeBSD a Tier 1 platform.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Limits and compatibility**

- Reject unsupported FreeBSD source installs before setup and explain the supported package route. [#151227](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151227) Thanks @vincentkoc and @lucarinaorg.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Check Node under restricted worker permissions">

If Node explicitly denies permission to start worker threads, OpenClaw can check SQLite in the current process instead of incorrectly reporting that SQLite is unavailable. This helps setup and diagnostics in restricted environments while leaving permissions and the Gateway's other worker requirements in place.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Check SQLite capabilities when Node denies workers. Other worker failures still report an error. [#152075](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152075)

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Find missing Podman setup requirements">

When Podman cannot create a sandbox because its host-side init helper is missing, the error now explains what to repair. Install `catatonit` or correct its configured path on the computer running the Podman engine, including inside Podman Machine when you use it. The [Podman setup guide](/install/podman) covers this requirement, which you must supply before retrying with the existing sandbox protections enabled.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Explain repairs for missing Podman host init helpers without automatically installing them or disabling `--init`. [#152308](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152308)

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Continue setup after configuration and storage problems">

If invalid configuration blocks setup, the error now directs you to run `openclaw doctor --fix` before retrying. [Doctor](/cli/doctor) applies supported repairs when you explicitly request them. Setup also stays responsive while saving recommendations and waits for progress to be saved before continuing, with storage failures still reported as errors.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Skip Matrix saved-auth storage probes when the backing database is definitely absent. Existing or uncertain state still receives normal credential validation. [#152576](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152576)

**Bug fixes**

- Point invalid-configuration recovery to explicit Doctor repair. [#130035](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/130035)
- Keep setup responsive while storing recommendations and wait for saved progress before continuing. [#152445](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152445)

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Keep the purpose of a new custom agent">

A new custom agent created through the [Control UI](/web/control-ui) now keeps its approved purpose as saved instructions for later conversations. This applies when creating a custom agent in a local workspace. Existing instructions stay intact, and conflicting instructions prompt you to choose another workspace. Changing the proposed purpose requires fresh approval.

Reopening **New agent** brings back the creation choices without discarding your Ask OpenClaw conversation, once pending questions, setup steps and approvals are finished. New agents also retain the display name you requested separately from their identifier, including when you choose a bundled role.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Preserve requested names when creating custom and bundled-role agents, while keeping role instructions and existing agents unchanged. [#151449](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151449)
- Save a custom agent's approved purpose at creation without overwriting existing workspace instructions. Custom purpose cannot be combined with bundled-role templates or remote workspaces. [#154137](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/154137)
- Restore creation choices when reopening New agent while preserving the conversation and pending-interaction priority. [#154167](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/154167)

</details>

</Accordion>

</AccordionGroup>

## Web UI

The Web UI makes it easier to start independent work while keeping the conversation you are already in, then follow its progress and return to it later. Reading, attachments, collaboration and appearance controls also receive changes that help keep your draft and place intact.

<AccordionGroup>

<Accordion title="Start background work from the command palette">

Use the [command palette](/web/control-ui/sessions-and-sidebar#command-palette) to start an independent task from text or a pasted screenshot while keeping your current conversation and draft in place. Press Cmd/Ctrl+K to open it and Cmd/Ctrl+Enter or **New session** to start the work, while ordinary Enter still selects a search result. Failed creation keeps images for another try, but closing the palette normally discards unsent images.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Start background sessions directly from the command palette [#152370](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152370) — thanks @vyctorbrzezowski, @jalehman.
- Start image tasks from the command palette [#153238](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153238) — thanks @vyctorbrzezowski.
- Pause command-palette search while composing long or multiline prompts [#153100](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153100) — thanks @vyctorbrzezowski.
- Keep background attention updates out of global popups [#151446](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151446).

**Bug fixes**

- Preserve command-palette prompt scroll during background updates [#153091](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153091) — thanks @vyctorbrzezowski.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Start suggested tasks without interrupting chat">

Suggested tasks keep their full summaries and Start controls reachable in shorter windows, and launching one in a new session or worktree leaves your current conversation and draft selected. If the selected repository cannot start the task, you can correct it without retyping the prompt, then explicitly retry with a usable repository that has a commit.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Show full suggested-task summaries with reachable controls [#153304](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153304).
- Launch suggested tasks without interrupting the current chat [#151611](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151611).
- Choose a valid repository when starting suggested tasks [#152522](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152522).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Choose where a new conversation runs">

New-session placement makes the cloud profile, operating system, machine size and starting branch easier to confirm, and submits the choices shown in the picker. Your prompt and attachments remain visible beside startup progress while the first message waits for the destination to become active. **Open session** returns to a created conversation without sending the prompt again. Configured size defaults still apply and existing instances are not resized. Administrators can also [register an existing repository](/web/control-ui/sessions-and-sidebar#register-an-existing-repository) using an accessible absolute path with a valid Git HEAD. Interrupted Incognito prompts remain only in the current tab's memory.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Show cloud operating system and machine size in the selector [#151947](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151947).
- Show the starting branch for cloud sessions [#152010](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152010).
- Show cloud backend marks in placement pickers [#153288](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153288) — thanks @vyctorbrzezowski.

- Load session catalog choices without history enumeration [#150831](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/150831).

**Bug fixes**

- Keep cloud-session prompts and setup progress visible together [#152024](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152024).

**Documentation**

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Choose the starting branch for a worktree">

Leaving the worktree **From** choice untouched now starts from the fetched remote default branch, while an explicit or saved local branch keeps its meaning. Branch editing stays usable and an accepted start clears the custom name it consumed, so it does not accidentally name the next session. Rejected starts retain the name, and failed remote lookup retains the source-HEAD fallback. If name cleanup is unconfirmed, check **Name** before starting another session.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Keep worktree editing stable and clear accepted custom names [#150426](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/150426) — thanks @romneyda.
- Start default worktrees from the remote default branch [#154234](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/154234).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Group, archive and reopen conversations">

Conversations can be archived or restored directly from the sidebar with Undo, and keyboard shortcuts open a New Session draft or archive the current eligible chat. Archiving keeps the conversation and its work, while group moves appear immediately and retain confirmed placement if the next refresh fails. New tabs keep the original draft in place and open with navigation expanded, and generated links retain the intended conversation. On desktop, right-click or Shift+F10 opens the full row menu, while touch keeps a menu button. If a group save is uncertain, refresh and check its placement before retrying.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Open New Session and archive the current chat from the keyboard [#153537](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153537) — thanks @vyctorbrzezowski, @jalehman.
- Reduce unnecessary work when filtering owned conversations [#152097](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152097).
- Archive and restore sessions directly from the sidebar [#154144](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/154144).
- Open new desktop session tabs with the sidebar expanded [#153106](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153106) — thanks @vyctorbrzezowski.
- Register new session categories off the Gateway thread [e2eae6ab](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/e2eae6abdd06ca13e795018c6d75a68cf963bbca).

**Bug fixes**

- Keep generated session links bound to the selected conversation [#150242](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/150242).
- Restore header organization actions for Home-linked conversations [#152439](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152439).
- Show session group moves immediately and preserve saved placement [#151689](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151689) — thanks @jalehman.
- Keep ordinary Claude CLI conversations in chosen groups [#150537](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/150537) — thanks @marvinthebored, @peetiegonzalez, @davidofflol12, @bitbalancer.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Recognize conversations in the sidebar">

The sidebar keeps loaded Codex conversations available while paired computers refresh, identifies linked messaging conversations from their recorded details, and keeps grouped agent-created chats searchable and reopenable. More room goes to conversation names by removing redundant owner avatars, duplicate Home entries and Worktree or Checkout row labels, with repository details still available on hover. Session lists and previews also reuse unchanged information more often, reducing repeated preparation as activity arrives.

<Accordion title="Follow background activity from its parent conversation">

Temporary child runs now belong in transcript activity and **Tasks**, leaving ordinary navigation for persistent conversations. Their parent conversation continues to show an activity ring during follow-up work, and the icon keeps its resting size when that ring starts. Use Tasks or transcript activity to inspect the work itself, since a failure tooltip is not a link to the child conversation.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Update subagent trees from complete session events [#152510](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152510).
- Move temporary subagent runs out of conversation navigation [#151470](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151470).
- Keep sidebar icons steady when activity rings start [526477da](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/526477da9bc6152fe0eeb1ae72de8147d2401d58) — thanks @fuller-stack-dev, @vyctorbrzezowski.

**Bug fixes**

- Show parent activity during subagent follow-ups [#153118](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153118).
- Remove duplicate Home rows and move session icons left [#152498](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152498).
- Avoid history-wide scans during session refreshes [#154163](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/154163).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Find older conversations and search within chat">

Search can find authorized conversations beyond the currently loaded sidebar, with avatars, owner badges, highlighted matches and All, Sessions or Messages filters to help recognize the result. Results remain a bounded set of matches, and cold archived history must first be restored by opening its conversation. Within a chat, Escape closes focused transcript search and returns focus without clearing your draft or pending reply.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Make search results recognizable with avatars and filters [#151968](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151968).
- Reduce repeated session-search matching work [#151708](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151708) — thanks @vincentkoc.
- Batch visibility metadata for restricted session searches [#152503](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152503) — thanks @bsniznd.

**Bug fixes**

- Find older conversations and preserve completed empty external-auth discovery [#152314](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152314).
- Close transcript search with Escape without clearing replies [#151759](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151759).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Keep conversation names usable">

Automatic naming can finish and update the sidebar after worktree setup stops waiting, while failed naming uses a neutral two-word name for a new session or worktree instead of copying the opening prompt. Setup can wait up to 30 seconds for a name, and a later title leaves an already-created branch unchanged. Manual names remain intact, and composing characters in the title editor no longer saves or discards a rename before composition ends.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Recover session names after slow or failed generation [#153221](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153221).
- Preserve session-name drafts during text composition [#129341](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/129341).
- Use neutral fallback names for sessions and worktrees [#153455](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153455).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Read long chats without losing your place">

Long chats load older history in fewer batches and keep navigation markers and reading position steadier as replies stream, the composer changes size or images finish loading. Expanded details and code-reading controls stay in place while the current part of a reply is still being written, but they may reset as it finishes. Code examples stay literal, reduced-motion preferences apply from startup, and opening a chat no longer slides the whole conversation into place. The initial history load stays small, while older-history batches can be larger.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Return to the intended conversation branch">

After rewinding and resending, the branch menu reveals the preserved conversations without a reload, and loaded history follows the branch you selected. Repeated branch listings and history reads also avoid some unnecessary transcript work. These are conversation branches, and returning to one does not undo earlier tool actions or file changes.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Retrieve branched conversation history with less database work [#153470](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153470).
- Reduce redundant work in session history reads [#153161](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153161).
- Keep more conversation branch summaries ready for reuse [#152724](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152724).

**Bug fixes**

- Keep session history aligned with branches and transcript ownership [#151640](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151640).
- Refresh preserved chat branches after rewind and resend [#151598](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151598).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Compaction checkpoint controls are retired">

Dedicated compaction checkpoint links, history cards and branch or restore controls have been removed, along with `sessions.compaction.list`, `sessions.compaction.branch` and `sessions.compaction.restore`. Ordinary Fork, rewind, conversation history, compaction summaries and available token-savings reporting remain. There is no equivalent replacement for checkpoint restore, and the retirement does not require a data purge or configuration migration.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Preserve queued work after failed checkpoint restores before checkpoint retirement [#148877](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/148877) — thanks @sunnyshu0925, @obviyus.

**Limits and compatibility**

- Retire compaction checkpoint controls while preserving history [#154131](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/154131).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Keep your place while collaborators write">

Reading older messages in a shared chat no longer gets interrupted by another person's message or typing, and the active reply stays visible through queued sends and saved history. A message from your own account in another browser also counts as remote activity. Sending from the current pane or selecting **Latest** resumes following the conversation, while typing produces fewer repeated presence updates.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Reduce repeated presence broadcasts while typing [#152042](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152042).

**Bug fixes**

- Preserve chat position and active replies during collaborator messages [#153061](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153061) — thanks @vyctorbrzezowski.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Keep parallel chats and drafts in place">

Parallel conversations keep more of their individual working state. A Side chat that finishes loading late does not pull typing away from your current input, archiving a neighboring pane preserves your unsaved comments and draft, and each conversation retains its own Chat or Dashboard view. Archiving the conversation you are editing still dismisses its controls.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Keep typing in the intended input while Side chat loads [#150217](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/150217).
- Preserve unsaved comments when another split pane archives [#150198](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/150198).
- Preserve each split conversation's Chat or Dashboard preference [#150228](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/150228).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Keep message controls within reach">

Chat notices, reply context, Goal previews and selected recipients sit above a reachable message field in a bounded footer, keeping controls usable in narrow or overlapping panes. Shared pickers continue to dismiss correctly as panes close, and moving between menu rows closes the previous submenu. Removing a selected recipient leaves the mention text in your message.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Integrate reply, Goal and recipient composer previews [#151246](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151246) — thanks @vyctorbrzezowski.

**Bug fixes**

- Keep the editor reachable and retry stale authentication reads after matching OAuth refresh [#152608](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152608).
- Align composer alerts with the message field [#151422](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151422) — thanks @vyctorbrzezowski.
- Close stuck submenus when moving between menu rows [#152317](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152317).
- Keep picker dismissal working across overlapping chat composers [23b9b82d](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/23b9b82d72c540da116e79340eefd842209a9ee8).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Keep attached context separate from your words">

Long pasted excerpts now appear as compact chips, and newly attached Home context appears separately from the words you wrote. You can inspect the source, copy the original text or return to editing without filling the transcript with reference material, while the text sent to the model stays unchanged. Pasted-text previews cover up to 256 KiB and downloads retain the full content. The Home treatment applies to new messages with recorded context metadata, and attached context grants no additional permissions.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Show long pasted text as compact excerpt chips [#151618](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151618) — thanks @vyctorbrzezowski.

**Bug fixes**

- Separate attached Home context from message text [#152539](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152539).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Prepare attachments and understand delivery failures">

Files appear immediately in stable attachment slots with individual reading progress and removable errors, so you can see which file needs attention before sending. That progress measures the browser reading each file, and sending waits for active reads while excluding unreadable files. Replies retain failed attachment cards beside successful media, selected local workspace files can reach supported progress and final replies, and progress attachments survive the repaired cleanup race. Older failed deliveries may need a resend, and previously deleted files cannot be recovered by this fix.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Show stable attachment tiles with individual read progress and errors [#152163](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152163) — thanks @vyctorbrzezowski.
- Translate attachment preparation announcements in twenty locales [#152196](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152196).

**Bug fixes**

- Keep attachment previews inside narrow chat composers [#153080](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153080) — thanks @vyctorbrzezowski.
- Preserve progress attachments during managed-media cleanup [#152645](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152645) — thanks @sahilsatralkar, @yetval, @takhoffman.
- Explain why an attachment failed in the current chat [#151913](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151913).
- Keep session Actions accessible beside attachments [#151975](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151975).
- Deliver attachments from the selected local session workspace [#151888](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151888).
- Remove empty gaps around unavailable image cards [#150573](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/150573) — thanks @vyctorbrzezowski.
- Preserve mixed attachment failures and selected-worktree image, document and audio delivery [#152079](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152079).

**Documentation**

- Clarify committed progress-message attachment guidance [#152225](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152225).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Browse a message's images without closing the viewer">

Browse the images attached to one message with buttons, arrow keys or swipes without closing the viewer. Sharper previews can open while originals load, then upgrade in place without resetting zoom, and a failed original leaves the preview visible. The gallery stays within one message and covers raster images, with SVG handled separately. Protected images still require **Allow**.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Browse a message’s images without closing the viewer [#152027](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152027) — thanks @vyctorbrzezowski.
- Open sharper image previews while originals load [#153265](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153265).

**Bug fixes**

- Navigate attached images in the chat gallery [#153198](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153198) — thanks @vyctorbrzezowski.
- Keep image loading and retry states current [#153269](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153269) — thanks @vyctorbrzezowski.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Preview larger HTML attachments beside chat">

Self-contained HTML attachments can fill the available height beside chat, making larger reports and interactive pages easier to inspect. Same-origin UTF-8 HTML previews now support up to 2 MiB, while text, Markdown and workspace reading or editing retain their 256 KiB limits. Cross-origin HTML remains download-only and the preview's sandbox permissions are unchanged.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Preview HTML attachments up to 2 MiB [#153197](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153197) — thanks @vyctorbrzezowski.

**Bug fixes**

- Let HTML previews fill the attachment side panel [#152031](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152031).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Open and read the intended file">

Explicit chat file links open the intended reader, including CSV files and authored Markdown links with spaces, emoji or punctuation in their names. Website paths keep their hostname instead of becoming local-file buttons, and local directories that resemble hostnames need an explicit path such as `./`. Background link renewal preserves reader focus and controls, while Escape from in-file search returns focus to its search button.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Open CSV chat links in the conversation file preview [#152900](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152900).
- Preserve website paths in chat file-link rendering [#152630](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152630).
- Restore keyboard focus after closing file search [#154221](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/154221).
- Keep attachment keyboard navigation during link refresh [#151858](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151858).
- Open workspace links containing spaces, emoji and punctuation [#152179](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152179).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Download conversation Markdown">

Use `/export` or `/export-session` without a path to download conversation Markdown from the browser. The guidance now matches that workflow, and an invalid path or a late export completion preserves your current draft and attachments. HTML and workspace exports in other clients keep their separate behavior.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Correct browser export guidance and preserve composer drafts [#152836](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152836).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Preview public links before opening them">

Hover or keyboard-focus an ordinary public link to see page details before opening it, with an explicit **Open in your browser** action and a text fallback when images are unavailable. This sends the eligible URL, including its path and query, to the Gateway for an anonymous public-page fetch without your browser's site credentials. The existing automatic-favicon opt-out also disables this enrichment, and touch taps keep normal navigation.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Preview ordinary external links on hover or keyboard focus [#152758](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152758).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Explore structured answers in Tree and Raw views">

Structured assistant answers can be explored in expandable **Tree** and literal **Raw** views, with copying that preserves the original source and expansion choices that stay in place. Complete JSON objects and arrays use the inspector, while unfinished, invalid or oversized content keeps a readable fallback. Your own code fences and passive previews remain ordinary code.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Explore assistant JSON in Tree and Raw views [#153272](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153272) — thanks @vyctorbrzezowski.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Manage comments on selected passages">

Pending comments on selected passages can be removed individually while the remaining preview stays open, or cleared together with focus returning to the composer. **Remove all comments cannot be undone** and no longer shows an Undo notification. Your message draft, ordinary files, already-sent comments and comments in other conversations stay intact, and reopened sent-comment previews remain visible after an interrupted hide animation.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Add bulk pending-comment removal and keep individual deletion previews open [#151409](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151409) — thanks @vyctorbrzezowski.
- Clear pending comments without toast or Undo and avoid duplicate image retries [#153057](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153057) — thanks @vyctorbrzezowski.

**Bug fixes**

- Keep reopened tooltips and comment previews visible [#152240](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152240).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Answer questions without losing their context">

Agent questions keep entered answers and selected options while you move between them, retain the original wording after completion, and stay with the conversation they came from. Optional questions remain reachable above the composer or in a minimized strip, then move to history after later successful work and can still be reopened. Skip sends nothing, archival supplies no answer or permission, and secret answers remain masked. Unfinished answers are not made durable across reloads or reconnects.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Preserve typed question answers and pending submission state [#152096](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152096).
- Keep questions and approval cards in their originating conversation [#151412](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151412).
- Keep full agent questions visible after answering [#151623](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151623).
- Archive outdated optional reminders while keeping them answerable [#153849](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153849) — thanks @vincentkoc.
- Keep unanswered questions visible above the composer [#153418](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153418).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Read and refresh task progress">

Task-progress cards stay steadier when you send a message, make overflowing checklists easier to notice and keep keyboard focus visible. **Refresh task progress** asks for a current status update without adding a chat message, retains the previous card while waiting and confirms an update only when a newer saved version arrives. The refresh does not resume work or answer pending questions. You can also hide the cards as a browser-local display preference without stopping the underlying task. See [progress-card refresh](/tools/progress-card#refresh-current-work-status) for its controls.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Hide progress cards, simplify settings hints and preserve queued foreground text and files [#152429](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152429).
- Improve task progress handles, overflow cues and checklist alignment [#150881](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/150881) — thanks @vyctorbrzezowski.
- Refresh task-progress cards without adding a chat message [#154044](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/154044) — thanks @shakkernerd.

**Bug fixes**

- Keep task progress steady when sending messages [#151388](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151388) — thanks @vyctorbrzezowski.
- Remove obstructing tool-summary hover text [#152456](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152456).
- Keep tool-summary keyboard focus rings visible [#149002](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/149002) — thanks @romneyda.
- Repair progress refresh startup and retries after cancellation [#154148](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/154148) — thanks @shakkernerd.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Inspect active and completed tasks in Tasks">

Open a background task or inline subagent activity to inspect its status and transcript inside **Tasks**, then use **Back to tasks** to return to the list. Review keeps its diff and Files keeps its selection, while reload can restore the selected task or show that it is unavailable. Compact activity summaries retain expandable details, and completed or canceled tasks update their labels correctly. Operation counts include repeated attempts and do not mean distinct files or successful results.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Keep task activity summaries compact with full tool details available [#152255](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152255).

**Bug fixes**

- Restore selected task details after reload, subsequently moved into Tasks [#153185](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153185).
- Show task completion after clock rollback [#153329](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153329) — thanks @gabrielnkl, @obviyus.
- Inspect subagent status and transcripts in Tasks [#153260](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153260).
- Refresh canceled task delivery labels immediately [#153226](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153226).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Read delegated results as work finishes">

Expanded Swarm results remain open through stale list refreshes, keep text clear of keyboard focus outlines and expose each child's status to screen readers. Completed work also keeps its newer status when conversation compaction or another pane refreshes history. Removing or replacing a result still clears its row.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Expose individual Swarm child statuses to screen readers [#150350](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/150350).
- Keep expanded Swarm results clear of focus outlines [#153168](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153168).
- Keep expanded Swarm results open during stale refreshes [#154170](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/154170).
- Preserve completed Swarm status during history refreshes [#153840](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153840).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Check an unconfirmed Goal action">

If Pause, Resume, Clear or editing a Goal returns an uncertain result, **Check outcome** lets you check the original action without repeating completed work, even after reconnecting or reloading a normal chat. Recovery is explicit and requires browser tab storage for normal chats, while Incognito recovery remains in memory. Saved requests expire after 24 hours, after which **Review current goal** refreshes the current state before your next decision. The [Goal controls guide](/tools/goal#control-ui) explains the recovery flow.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Recover unconfirmed Goal actions without repeating completed work [#153910](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153910) — thanks @vincentkoc, @vacinc.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Inspect tools available to the current conversation">

**Available Right Now** includes tools bound to the current conversation and refreshes after a session reset, so the inventory better reflects the tools that conversation can use. Opening Tools also avoids refreshing the entire model catalog unnecessarily. Inspecting this list does not add tools or grant permission to run them.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Show session-bound tools and refresh availability after reset [#153553](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153553).
- List session tools without refreshing the full model catalog [#153310](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153310).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Find people and keep linked accounts together">

Explicitly linked GitHub accounts can keep one person's avatar, preferences and mention Inbox, and people search supports full names and verified handles. Selected mentions show avatars and open full person cards using information you are allowed to see, while assignment and sharing pickers remain searchable and current. Typing or pasting a name alone does not notify someone or grant membership. Before downgrading, keep a backup because older writers can discard secondary account links, and upgrading again will not restore them without explicitly relinking.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Keep linked accounts together and make selected mentions open person cards [#147568](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147568).
- Search session people pickers and show current profile details [#153798](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153798) — thanks @vincentkoc.
- Simplify selected mention badges while preserving copied text [#152423](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152423) — thanks @vyctorbrzezowski, @fuller-stack-dev.
- Open full person cards from selected mentions [#153231](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153231).
- Show profile avatars in selected human mentions [#152037](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152037).
- Avoid full history scans in the session member picker [#152497](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152497).
- Move people-list and mention reads off the Gateway thread [#152005](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152005).

**Bug fixes**

- Preserve mention selection and profile photos with keyboard and retry controls [#152194](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152194) — thanks @vyctorbrzezowski.
- Reuse recent people searches in mention pickers [#153988](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153988).
- Keep concurrent Gravatar photo requests independent [#127172](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/127172).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Find conversations that mention you">

An explicit mention can bring an accessible conversation into **Involving me** without requiring you to reply first. Personal Hide and Show controls follow your profile across browsers and restarts, and a fresh mention can bring a hidden conversation back. These controls appear when more than one identity is available and remain separate from Inbox dismissal, shared membership and archive state. Older conversations are not scanned for missed mentions. See [finding sessions by owner](/concepts/multi-user#finding-sessions-by-owner).

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Find mentioned sessions in Involving me with personal Hide and Show [#152083](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152083).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Understand Inbox alerts and saved Outbox messages">

Inbox snoozes stay separate for each account and Gateway in a shared browser, and **Dismiss** or **Dismiss all shown** makes the scope of clearing notifications explicit. The account menu also explains saved Outbox messages and distinguishes queued, failed and unconfirmed states. Dismissal does not answer approvals or stop work, and an unconfirmed message may already have arrived. Older snoozes without a known owner may let an alert reappear once.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Clarify Inbox dismissal and saved Outbox message status [#153810](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153810) — thanks @vincentkoc.
- Reduce duplicate reads for connected Mentions Inbox viewers [#153565](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153565).
- Keep notification recovery text and actions readable [#151575](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151575).

**Bug fixes**

- Keep Inbox snoozes separate across accounts [#153862](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153862) — thanks @vincentkoc.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Recover browser sign-in and reconnecting chats">

**Forget this browser** in Settings → Connections → Gateway → Browser resets the current Gateway's browser sign-in without clearing unrelated preferences or other Gateways' sign-ins. It signs out the current tab and may require signing in or approval again, but does not revoke server-side devices or start a stopped Gateway. During ordinary reconnects, chat stays usable while its session list loads, with clearer account identity, connection status and unresolved Outbox counts.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Forget the current Gateway's browser sign-in without clearing other data [#120319](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/120319) — thanks @nehanth, @sallyom, @theangrypit.

**Bug fixes**

- Keep reconnect status and unresolved outbox messages visible [#153321](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153321).
- Prevent global chat freezes during reconnect [#151567](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151567).
- Clarify expired pairing-code notices [#153921](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153921) — thanks @vyctorbrzezowski.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Recover failed chats and queued follow-ups">

Failed or timed-out chats can show their saved error and **Copy error** even when the live notification was missed, while temporary history-index rebuilding returns a retryable error. Failed pending messages can be discarded locally and stay removed after reload, and queued follow-ups can proceed once completion of their exact run is confirmed. Discarding does not undo accepted work or history. An outdated guarded Stop request also leaves a replacement conversation and its newer queued work alone.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

- Show saved run errors when chat notifications are missed [#153568](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153568) — thanks @vincentkoc.
- Keep session history current after rollback or database replacement [#153010](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153010).
- Discard failed pending chat messages from the transcript [#152188](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152188) — thanks @mertbasar0, @obviyus, @laurencebrown.
- Release queued messages after their conversation finishes [#152299](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152299).
- Clear resolved queued-message edit conflicts [#150220](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/150220) — thanks @vyctorbrzezowski.
- Keep outdated Stop requests from interrupting replacement conversations [#152116](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152116).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Create and apply personal themes through an agent">

Ask an agent to discover, choose or create a [personal theme](/tools/theme) and save it to your authenticated profile for use across signed-in browsers. Appearance uses the same catalog, including enabled plugin themes, and connected browsers update as choices change. This requires an updated UI and Gateway together, while older tweakcn imports remain browser-local. If a selected theme becomes unavailable, Claw renders temporarily and the selection is retained for its return.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Create and apply themes through an agent and refresh skills after rapid symlink replacement [#152460](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152460) — thanks @xingzihai.

**Bug fixes**

- Keep custom themes stable during plugin reloads [#153001](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153001).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Apply a theme's complete appearance">

Choosing a different theme now applies its default fonts and full palette, and eleven built-in themes gain locally bundled light and dark backgrounds. Existing preferences survive the upgrade and you can customize a theme after selecting it. Custom palettes use neutral artwork, increased contrast and forced colors hide artwork, and reduced transparency makes the composer opaque. Before downgrading to a Gateway that accepts only hex accents, remove `ui.prefs.accent=theme` or replace it with a hex color. [Appearance settings](/web/control-ui/settings#appearance-themes) covers these choices.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Apply the complete default appearance when switching themes [#152254](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152254) — thanks @vyctorbrzezowski, @fuller-stack-dev.
- Add theme-matched light and dark backgrounds with accessibility fallbacks [#153583](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153583).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Keep dashboard layouts and widgets in place">

An unchanged dashboard restores its selected, closed and focused panel arrangement after reload, and moving a loaded widget to an unopened tab preserves unsaved input and counters. The layout menu shows when the current view matches its shared default, while a lone full-width widget gets more unobstructed space with controls in the task menu. Explicit presentation changes still take precedence, and preserving a widget during a move does not make arbitrary widget state durable across reloads or deletion.

<Accordion title="Follow Activity recaps with less repeated work">

Activity recaps and people lists avoid preparing information they do not need, reducing repeated work during refreshes. Session assessments and recaps also recover when an implicit model choice selects unavailable Codex, while an explicitly chosen unavailable Codex route still reports failure. These utility requests remain tool-free.

Activity shows compact, uncropped HTTPS image previews that can expand without taking over the list, and plugin README and agent-file previews can load HTTPS images automatically. Those images are fetched directly by your browser, so their hosts receive its network address. HTTP images remain blocked, and Markdown attachments and Workshop previews retain click-to-open behavior.

<Accordion title="Review usage by date and conversation creator">

Usage opens with 30 days of history and a **Started by** breakdown, using saved conversation names and clearer treatment of known zero-cost usage. Whole conversations are attributed to their recorded creator, with older unknown creators kept as **Unattributed**, and displayed costs remain estimates rather than bills. Session exports respect selected conversations and active filters, but CSV exports and explicit row filters work from loaded rows while permitted report totals remain complete.

<Accordion title="Inspect CPU activity in a movable panel">

The System busyness panel separates host and Gateway CPU readings, uses clearer labels and can be moved with a mouse, touch or keyboard, with its position remembered in browser storage. Gateway 100% means one CPU, while host 100% represents the machine's total capacity, and child or remote processes are excluded. High ping receives a visible warning color, and unavailable counters stay absent rather than implying a measurement.

<Accordion title="Find machines and inspect recent resource use">

Systems offers a compact machine list with sorting and online or offline filters, plus recent load, memory and volume free-space graphs for the selected machine. History accumulates while Systems is open and resets after reconnects or gaps, so it is a recent view rather than a stored monitoring history. Connected nodes report their home volume, and load should not be read as CPU utilization.

On recognized iPhone and iPad Safari browsers, a single **Attach** entry opens the existing file chooser, with camera and source prompts still controlled by the operating system.

## Updates and Maintenance

Updates now explain more clearly when OpenClaw is ready, when maintenance is still running, and what needs your attention. Make and verify a [backup](/cli/backup) before updating, because rolling back the application does not restore your data.

<AccordionGroup>

<Accordion title="Automatic updates for idle headless nodes">

Packaged headless nodes on stable or beta releases now update automatically when their work and cleanup are idle, keeping their pairing, settings and launch options. They check hourly after connecting, leave at least 12 hours between activations, and fall back to the previous runtime if the replacement cannot start. Busy work and plugins without explicit idle reporting can defer activation indefinitely.

You can turn this off with `nodeHost.autoUpdate.enabled: false`, `update.checkOnStart: false`, or `OPENCLAW_NO_AUTO_UPDATE=1`; the Gateway's automatic-update switch is separate. Native app nodes, private workers, source installs and extended-stable pins keep their existing update paths. Releases needing database migration or repair still require `openclaw update`, and older co-located Gateways need one normal upgrade first. See [headless node updates](/install/updating/automatic-updates#headless-node-updates).

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Automatically update idle packaged headless nodes [#151545](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151545).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Bring prepared agents online during fleet startup">

In larger installations, prepared agents can take requests while the rest of the fleet finishes starting, with pending agents shown as still preparing. Startup and session-source reads also avoid repeated checks and unnecessary loading of saved prompts. A ready Gateway can therefore have agents that are not yet available; readiness does not mean the whole fleet has finished.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Reduce Gateway startup stalls and database worker conflicts [#151805](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151805) — @609nft.
- Keep prepared fleet agents available during remaining startup [#151074](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151074) — @609nft.

**Improvements**

- Reduce session-store discovery work across larger fleets [#153223](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153223).
- Reduce repeated filesystem checks for session sources [#153319](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153319).
- Batch model-catalog reads for large agent rosters [#152491](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152491).
- Reduce unnecessary worker prompt reads in large session stores [#149700](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/149700) — @vincentkoc.
- Avoid repeated filesystem lookups during session reads [#149838](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/149838).
- Skip unused saved prompts in worker-cleanup identity reads [#152447](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152447).
- Move session placement evidence reads off the Gateway thread [#152901](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152901).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Prioritize required repairs in large fleets">

Large-fleet updates reuse configuration and plugin inspections and prioritize repairs needed to start the replacement. Optional checks that are deferred have not passed yet, so run `openclaw doctor --fix` after activation to finish them. Older installed updaters keep their existing time limits until they have been replaced.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Reduce repeated configuration validation during fleet updates [#151678](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151678).
- Prioritize required Doctor repairs during large-fleet updates [#151683](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151683).
- Reduce large-fleet validation overhead and settle cleanup [#152378](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152378).

**Improvements**

- Reuse Doctor preparation across large fleets [#151366](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151366).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Finish backups while ordinary files change">

[Backups](/cli/backup) can finish when ordinary files disappear during capture, preserving the surviving files and reporting omitted paths. Completed database copies also return their result without waiting for unrelated activity, while archive creation and verification avoid repeated copying and searches. Required roots, unsafe file substitutions and capture or I/O failures still stop the backup, so review omissions before relying on an archive.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Resume completed SQLite backups in idle processes [#152302](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152302).
- Continue backups when ordinary files disappear, report omissions and filter transient names except explicitly selected roots [#153395](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153395) — @migelizaga-rgb.

**Improvements**

- Reduce buffer copying while creating backup archives [#152332](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152332).
- Reduce redundant archive searches during backup verification [#152156](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152156).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Verify retained launcher backups">

Updates no longer reject a retained launcher backup solely because the platform cannot preserve explicitly unsupported symlink metadata. Link targets, entry types and regular-file contents still have to match, and genuine mismatches leave the failed backup available for inspection. This belongs to the installed updater, so an older updater blocked before activation may need the documented [manual first upgrade](/install/updating/update-methods).

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Tolerate unsupported launcher symlink metadata during updates [#153081](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153081) — svenmy76.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Preserve chosen configuration during repairs">

[Doctor](/cli/doctor) preserves explicitly chosen workspace locations, leaves comments and formatting alone when no repair is needed, and keeps session-store ownership through unrelated settings changes. It can offer confirmed recovery of a removed owner setting from an eligible root-only, same-store backup, while conflicting tool lists are repaired only when permissions can be preserved. Ambiguous permission changes remain your decision, and a refused configuration write stops dependent service repair.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Preserve session-store ownership through unrelated edits and offer confirmed backup recovery, while actual store changes clear copied ownership [#151468](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151468) — @shadesurgeon, @sunnyshu0925, @masterswords1.
- Preserve explicitly configured workspaces during profile repairs [#145976](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145976) — @ericcaiwx-star, @fuller-stack-dev, @oshunter.
- Honor Doctor configuration refusals before repairing Gateway services [#147120](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147120) — @vincentkoc, @fuller-stack-dev.
- Avoid rewriting configuration when Doctor has no plugin repairs [6997e1d9](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/6997e1d920fbf54f0c24d8d9249c470966c69de6) — @romneyda.
- Repair safe tool-list conflicts without changing permissions [#151121](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151121) — @mertbasar0.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Run historical data repairs through Doctor">

Historical conversation titles, ACP metadata, transcript markers, worktree paths and task identifiers are repaired through [Doctor maintenance](/gateway/doctor/state-and-sessions), keeping ordinary startup and reads from rewriting that history. `openclaw update` already runs the required repair pass. After directly replacing a binary or container image, or using state touched by older writers, stop the Gateway and run `openclaw doctor --fix` against the same state and configuration before starting it again.

Successful imports can continue past archive-only or scratch-cleanup warnings, while changed originals, incomplete captured data and storage or ownership failures still need attention. Ambiguous task identifiers remain unchanged rather than risking a result reaching the wrong task; title repair also retains a limitation when several agents share one physical store.

- Move historical title and ACP-key repair into Doctor, with shared-store title coverage still limited [#151211](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151211).
- Repair historical task identifiers through Doctor, preserve completion bindings, pass active update context to recovery advice and defer Usage-only labels [#153509](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153509).

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Recover verified session imports">

If an old session index disappeared or a restored file changed identity after history was imported, `openclaw doctor --session-sqlite recover` can repair the import records using verified retained content. Current conversation settings and deletions remain authoritative, and changed or unverifiable files stay protected for inspection. Follow the [SQLite recovery guidance](/cli/doctor/sqlite-maintenance) and preserve files named in conflicts; a running Gateway does not by itself mean those files are ready for cleanup.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Recover verified session imports after missing or replaced source files [#153655](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153655) — @cpsleepy, @mmm7053455-tech, @blackeyes-boy, @albertyn87.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Understand and finish Doctor checks">

Doctor's final results now retain the original reason for a refused migration, identify plugins that failed to load, and give useful disk-space guidance. Completed checks survive cleanup-only delays instead of becoming false update failures, while unfinished checks still report a timeout. Standalone noninteractive Doctor exits with an error for plugin-load failures; updater-invoked Doctor can retain those findings as warnings when the update can otherwise proceed. See [how updates run](/cli/update/how-updates-run) for the remaining older-updater limits.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

- Move Crabbox diagnostic storage reads off the calling thread [3bbb9a3f](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/3bbb9a3f10a54e942a9ab6d58503be698433f28e).
- Move Skill Workshop diagnostic reads off the calling thread [45384f58](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/45384f58ab8a575c010c8663228cb8c7ab7950c0).

- Reuse prepared plugin metadata during repeated checks [#153452](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153452).

- Report plugin-load failures in Doctor's final outcome [#153640](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153640) — @th-m-vogel.
- Reduce unrelated work during Doctor's Matrix state repair [#152203](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152203).
