</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Repair plugin versions and follow actionable guidance">

After a manual core upgrade, `openclaw doctor --fix` can bring eligible official npm plugins back into line while respecting newer pins and nondefault tags. Repair guidance now distinguishes an attainable release from registry lag, reports unreadable required manifests, and avoids sending you around a circular migration fix. Third-party plugins stay with their own maintainers, unavailable packages remain warnings, and missing required manifests can still make post-upgrade checks fail.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Give achievable ClawHub update advice and explain registry lag [#145103](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145103) — @dasx, @vincentkoc, @fuller-stack-dev, @colton-harris, Jason Sy.
- Repair official plugin version drift after manual core upgrades [#153027](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153027) — Prince.
- Report missing or unreadable required plugin manifests after upgrades [baec505b](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/baec505bffcfc67b4e80429974bf705a39dc57fe).
- Avoid circular plugin migration recovery advice [#153419](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153419) — @romneyda.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Preserve local plugin overrides during updates">

Updates preserve local plugin overrides and the npm copies they shadow, with guidance about which copy is active. Check that your override supports the new release, or deliberately remove it to return to the managed copy. Preserving a local plugin does not grant it trust, and channels blocked by trust policy stop retrying until that policy is resolved. The [plugin sync guidance](/cli/update/how-updates-run#local-packaged-overrides) explains the update behavior.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Preserve local plugin overrides during update and explain blocked channels [#151851](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151851) — @whitenesstiger.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Honor explicit update work deadlines">

Plugin and hook updates can finish longer work when no work deadline was supplied, while explicitly short deadlines are honored. The same distinction carries through supported Doctor, plugin-convergence and finalization paths, without removing separate metadata, readiness, cleanup or enclosing updater limits. Git plugin clone and checkout still have a cancellation gap when running without a deadline, so this is not a promise that every stalled update can be canceled immediately.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Limits and compatibility**

- Honor plugin and hook work deadlines while retaining the Git clone and checkout cancellation limitation [#153043](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153043) — @fuller-stack-dev.

**Bug fixes**

- Let post-core plugin updates finish without an implicit work deadline [#153109](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153109) — @fuller-stack-dev.
- Avoid implicit overall deadlines during update finalization [#153085](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153085) — @fuller-stack-dev.
- Preserve omitted Doctor deadlines during update finalization [#152973](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152973) — @fuller-stack-dev.
- Honor omitted work deadlines in the Git updater API [#153036](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153036) — @fuller-stack-dev.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Keep update work from overlapping">

Long package updates retain exclusive control until their work has settled, and cancellation waits for cleanup before another update or repair can proceed. If cleanup cannot be confirmed, OpenClaw keeps recovery files and may leave the Gateway stopped. Inspect `openclaw update status` before retrying, and do not delete a lock merely because it is old or its parent process has exited.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Prevent overlapping installation work during long package updates [#153093](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153093) — @fuller-stack-dev.
- Wait for update command cleanup before releasing control [#150959](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/150959).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Let active work settle before restarting">

Managed restarts verify the serving Gateway and give admitted agent, subagent and scheduled work time to finish before storage and helpers close. Unknown activity no longer counts as idle, and forced restarts request cancellation before waiting for scheduled-work cleanup. Existing force and shutdown deadlines still apply, so work can be interrupted. These Unix recovery changes require the updated launcher; existing Linux services may also need same-profile `openclaw gateway install --force` to pick up `KillMode=mixed`. See [restart recovery](/gateway/restart-recovery).

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Use remaining Gateway shutdown time for sidecar cleanup [#151360](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151360).
- Preserve local Gateway restart cleanup and initial chat position [#153267](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153267).
- Defer restarts when active work cannot be inspected [#118053](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/118053) — @harjothkhara, @fuller-stack-dev, @dale-goes-fast.
- Let admitted work drain during recovered Unix Node restarts within existing service deadlines [#153435](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153435) — @ly85206559, @obviyus, @donniefi.
- Verify the serving Gateway before recovered restarts [#153510](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153510).
- Unblock forced restarts while cron cleanup is pending [#153575](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153575).
- Drain delivery maintenance before shared storage shuts down [#152379](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152379).
- Release agent database leases before update maintenance [#151435](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151435).
- Refuse pending database writes as shutdown begins [#152536](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152536).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Keep requested work through restarts">

Unfinished parent conversations can continue after a restart with their transcript, retained tool results and a restart notice. Interrupted subagents report back to the parent instead of automatically relaunching, and completion from the same child batch can be recognized when a different sibling retries. The parent must check whether earlier execution stopped and whether an interrupted action took effect before continuing or replacing work. Saved Stop decisions stay stopped, with current permissions and recovery eligibility still applying. See [what survives a restart](/gateway/restart-recovery).

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Preserve restart notices and requested session continuation recovery [#152929](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152929).
- Retain subagent progress across repeated restarts [#151450](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151450).
- Recover conversations stranded after Gateway restarts [#153433](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153433).
- Resume unfinished parent work after Gateway restart [#153243](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153243).
- Keep stopped parent conversations canceled across restart without guaranteeing that every child stopped [#153271](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153271).
- Recover parent tasks across sibling completion retries [#153850](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153850) — @vincentkoc.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Recognize slow but progressing startup">

Restart, update and diagnostic checks distinguish a Gateway that is still making startup progress from one that has failed, and local health checks recognize serving wildcard listeners on macOS. At the restart wait limit, exit code `2` means still starting, not healthy; `health` and `channels` JSON may likewise exit `0` with `status=starting`. Check `openclaw gateway status --deep` and inspect again before treating the service as ready. Existing wait budgets remain, and an older updater already running cannot inherit the new checks.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Recognize serving wildcard Gateways in local health checks [#153984](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153984).
- Avoid false restart failures during progressing startup [#151387](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151387).
- Preserve progressing Gateway startup during update verification [#151663](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151663) — @freshxiaoyao, @drtony1.
- Avoid false Gateway failures during slow startup [#153063](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153063) — @suidge.
- Reuse paired service credentials for trusted-proxy restart checks [#153540](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153540).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Change scripts that use the restart signal">

On supported Node installations on macOS and Linux, `SIGUSR1` now opens the debugger without restarting the Gateway. Change scripts that used it for restarts to `SIGUSR2`, or use `openclaw gateway restart`, which retains handling for verified older running Gateways. The [restart and supervision guide](/cli/gateway/restart-and-supervision) explains the signal change.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Limits and compatibility**

- Reserve SIGUSR1 for the debugger, move Gateway restarts to SIGUSR2 and QA diagnostics to SIGQUIT [#153462](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153462).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Reduce repeated startup preparation">

Eligible same-version clean restarts reuse completed database verification, and startup loads fewer unused dependencies. Memory, document and Codex workers also start with less overhead, while concurrent Windows UI preparation can reuse fully verified assets. Upgrades, unclean shutdowns, replaced files and migrations still require full checks; damage within the same file may be found after readiness, so a quick restart is not a fresh integrity certificate.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Reduce background worker startup time and memory use [#154293](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/154293).
- Reuse database checks after eligible clean restarts while refusing startup if the required dirty marker cannot be written [#153463](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153463).
- Skip unnecessary config-recovery lookups when no backup exists [#151801](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151801).
- Load workspace discovery dependencies only when needed [#154115](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/154115).
- Load Gateway startup recovery on demand with manual fallback [#151699](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151699).

**Bug fixes**

- Inherit the launcher compile cache in SQLite workers [#150715](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/150715).
- Reuse verified Control UI assets after Windows publication collisions [#151932](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151932).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Keep managed services on the selected installation">

Updates and Doctor can identify eligible managed Gateways pointing at a different installation. Updates refresh recognized stale service settings while preserving custom definitions, and supported Node installation changes may trigger a noninteractive installation of a private compatible Node runtime without changing system Node. Already-stopped services stay stopped, externally managed services keep their owner, and `--no-restart` does not rebind a service. Windows and some custom overrides retain the service-root fallback described in [update execution](/cli/update/how-updates-run).

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Restore Gateways after inconclusive systemd inspection [#153017](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153017) — @xilopaint.
- Repair managed Gateways left on a different OpenClaw installation [24445095](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/244450952c019eaffeced152f7c74ebaed1b8c6f) — @baiwei0427.
- Report outdated Gateway service settings in Doctor and update status [#151777](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151777) — @andapeng.
- Treat unset home literals consistently for Gateway services [#151277](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151277) — @masatohoshino.
- Refresh stale managed Gateway service definitions during updates [#152120](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152120) — @andapeng.
- Complete managed Gateway updates after Node installation paths change [97c7b359](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/97c7b35933924ff6db0a58fba255fef9e71f1e96) — @fuller-stack-dev, @romneyda.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Preserve and verify Mac Gateway services">

Mac service commands recognize native XML and binary definitions, preserve original LaunchAgent bytes and permissions during supported rollback, and verify that the service has unloaded before reporting a completed stop. Run maintenance from the service owner's logged-in session and use `openclaw gateway status --deep` to inspect it; a disabled but still-loaded service must be unloaded before runtime replacement. Affected older update helpers may need their first upgrade from an independent Terminal. Service inspection from an inaccessible working directory remains a known limitation.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Restore Mac service commands from external shells [#153403](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153403) — @romneyda.
- Preserve LaunchAgent bytes and permissions during rollback [#152195](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152195).
- Allow authorized update helpers to complete Gateway service shutdown [#153020](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153020) — @theangrypit, svenmy76.
- Verify macOS Gateway shutdown before reporting success [#153789](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153789) — @britrik.
- Read native XML and binary Mac service definitions, with inaccessible working directories still a limitation [#151432](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151432).
- Require the macOS Gateway service to be unloaded before runtime replacement [#153637](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153637).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Keep Windows shutdown and update paths verifiable">

Windows updates and snapshots retain exact directory identity, and managed Gateway shutdown keeps track of the verified process through its handoff. When native Windows Job support is available, cleanup can also stop and verify owned descendants after their parent exits. If that cleanup cannot be certified, locks and temporary files remain for recovery; foreign or reused processes are still rejected.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Preserve exact Windows directory identities for updates and snapshots [#152972](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152972).
- Preserve Windows Gateway shutdown ownership and handoff paths [#151157](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151157) — @romneyda, @fuller-stack-dev, Jason Sy.
- Verify Windows descendant cleanup after parent exit [#151443](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151443) — @andapeng.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Update source checkouts and preserve failed-build recovery">

Source-checkout updates handle larger Git packs and shallow partial repositories, show installed and target revisions, and preserve local edits when checking retained builds for rollback. Older installed updaters may still need missing Git objects fetched or a manual first upgrade, and staged packs need disk space. Doctor also gives selective recovery advice for source self-links without rewriting unrelated dependency edits.

The [source-server update script](/install/updating/update-methods#source-checkout-servers-reference-script) stops the Gateway before clean builds and restores generated output after ordinary build failures. Custom automatic service commands need paired, nonblank stop and restart settings; an exactly empty restart setting keeps manual lifecycle control. Recovery covers generated output only, and retained nested build work remains a limitation on automatic rollback and restart.

<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Preserve replaced clone directories during update validation [#147544](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147544) — @romneyda, @fuller-stack-dev.
- Verify retained npm-linked Git builds before rollback [#147562](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147562) — @romneyda, @fuller-stack-dev.
- Remove Git update pack limits and whole-pack buffering [#154042](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/154042) — @fuller-stack-dev, @romneyda, Mariano.
- Fix updates from shallow partial source checkouts [#154191](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/154191).
- Stop source Gateways before clean builds and restore ordinary failed-build output, with nested-build recovery still limited [#125896](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/125896) — @grynn, @fuller-stack-dev, @donniefi.
- Diagnose source self-links and preserve heartbeat and task-read recovery [#117114](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/117114) — @synthalorian, @fuller-stack-dev, @piotx, @vincentkoc.

**Improvements**

- Skip disposable build caches during Git runtime promotion [#153632](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153632) — @vincentkoc.
- Show development update revisions and GitHub comparisons [#153740](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153740) — @vincentkoc.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Use the Homebrew upgrade route">

Homebrew installations now receive the correct [formula upgrade guidance](/install/updating/update-methods#homebrew-formula-installs). Back up and stop the Gateway, run `brew upgrade openclaw-cli`, run `openclaw doctor --fix`, then restart. New or refreshed services use Homebrew's stable formula path; an existing service pointing into an old version directory may need same-profile `openclaw gateway install --force` from the upgraded CLI. OpenClaw does not run the Homebrew upgrade for you.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Guide Homebrew upgrades and preserve stable Gateway service paths [#141011](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/141011) — @maxsxu.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Follow the deployment owner's update path">

If an external supervisor owns your installation, the update screen explains that you need to use that server or deployment's update workflow. The refusal leaves packages and the Gateway untouched and removes irrelevant repair suggestions. If a later status read fails, Check status remains available without starting another update.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Explain external-supervisor update refusals and status recovery [#151942](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151942) — @vincentkoc, @fuller-stack-dev.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Update a foreground Gateway through a fresh process">

A Gateway running in a terminal now hands supported updates to a fresh process, avoiding missing-module failures after its runtime files are replaced. Candidate validation happens while it serves, followed by a brief activation interruption; Stop waits for owned update work and leaves it stopped. `OPENCLAW_NO_RESPAWN` refuses before changes and gives manual instructions.

Use the coordinated `openclaw update` path when possible. A Gateway already running the new detection code can also notice manual package replacement and drain toward managed restart or foreground relaunch with `openclaw gateway run`; an old running binary cannot gain that behavior from replacement files alone. If Stop remains unconfirmed, inspect the updater and retry Ctrl+C in the original terminal or SIGTERM to the original process, as described in [update execution](/cli/update/how-updates-run).

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Avoid missing-module failures during foreground Gateway updates [#152466](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152466).
- Recover the Gateway after manual package replacement [#153078](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153078) — @mattkanwisher.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Recover supported older-updater handoffs">

Supported older-updater handoffs can complete offline session repairs and verify the replacement Gateway, including supported Linux/npm upgrades from 2026.9.2. Missing inherited update history or backup coverage still stops maintenance, and backups an older updater already removed cannot be recreated. After a committed package change, recovery may require a compatible installed build, same-state `openclaw doctor --fix`, then `openclaw gateway start`. Keep a verified backup because package-only rollback can be unsafe after migration; [rollback and recovery](/install/updating/rollback-and-recovery) explains the boundary.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Restore working Gateways after legacy update and session-import failures [#151682](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151682) — @allen0373, @jjgao.
- Prevent false rollback by older Gateway updaters [#151977](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151977).
- Handle missing update history and temporary-path aliases on resume [#153784](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153784).
- Complete database upgrades started by OpenClaw 2026.9.2 [#153657](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153657) — @vincentkoc.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Restore missing packaged native filesystem support">

Packaged installs can restore a missing native filesystem addon from its exact declared prebuilt package after optional-dependency fallback. Healthy installations avoid a download, explicit native-off settings stay off, and source checkouts or existing broken addons are left untouched. Unsupported or offline recovery reports a warning instead of promising native support.

<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Restore missing native filesystem support after update fallback [#152349](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152349).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Keep managed worktrees visible through maintenance">

Restored or repaired managed worktrees remain visible when an older missing-path check finishes, and cleanup can proceed while the Gateway writes its database. Creation also avoids walking unrelated ignored dependency folders, while failed preparation preserves changed worktrees and committed reset actions. Already-removed records are not reconstructed, and historical repairs may still need Doctor.

- Avoid processing unrelated placements during worktree cleanup [#153309](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153309).
- Create managed worktrees beside large ignored dependency folders [3e013774](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/3e013774fc7677644cffb275192a3b45a28e5838) — @masatohoshino.

- Reduce waiting while capturing checkout baselines [#151548](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151548).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Reclaim eligible temporary data without repeated cleanup">

OpenClaw reclaims eligible abandoned managed plugin copies after an ownership check and a one-hour grace period, while session cleanup avoids repeatedly scanning data it cannot reclaim. Active and protected history remains protected, and `openclaw sessions cleanup --enforce` still requests immediate cleanup. Committed session resets finish their remaining actions even if later cleanup fails, with the original error kept visible.

- Reduce cold session reset and deletion loading [#153294](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153294).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Keep maintenance storage work from blocking other activity">

More maintenance, snapshot and shared-state database work runs in background workers, reducing work that competes with messages and status requests. Catalog refreshes avoid redundant copies, unused queued snapshots can be canceled, and update rehearsals can capture consistent individual databases while other processes write. Retention rules and uncertain or active owners remain protected; these individual copies do not create an atomic rollback across every database.

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="See what completed and what still needs recovery">

Update results distinguish the requested version becoming healthy from a rollback that restored the previous version, and can recognize eligible updates interrupted only during final verification. Successful repairs clear resolved guidance in recent history without changing the original failure into a success. When readiness or cleanup remains uncertain, the report keeps that uncertainty visible and directs you to inspect the current Gateway before continuing.

- Recognize completed updates after interrupted verification [#152202](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152202).

- Verify update recovery before triage reports success [#153443](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153443) — @johnnyjrizzo.
- Finish updates using the installed runtime and protect configuration rollback [1232d3f1](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/1232d3f13ec04a42491a3fb869bdcaf42dae78eb) — @vincentkoc, @fuller-stack-dev.
- Report current Gateway health after update finalization failures [#153178](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153178) — @0-danielviktorovich-0, @wlassalle724, @neangneatos, @zsmallx, @hxy8241.
- Reuse completed candidate checks before update repair [#153188](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153188) — @bodegaclaw, @theangrypit, @baumus, @hypocritepro, @bharatvasan, @jplavoiemtl, @guerilla47.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Get useful update failure reports">

Saved update reports retain redacted failure causes, identify the operation that failed and show recovery paths and observed rollback results. They also distinguish a completed check with slow process exit from a check that never finished, and avoid warning about rollback damage when preparation changed nothing. These reports help explain the failure rather than repair it, and older discarded details cannot be reconstructed. Review a report before submitting it.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Keep update warnings visible and provide saved outcome reports [#153147](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153147) — @davidchyi-beep, @bodegaclaw, @che10x, @jammyclaw, @servermacminim2ku, @hypocritepro, @lendersmark, @guerilla47, Patryk.
- Retain automatic-update failure causes in history and logs [#151568](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151568) — @masatohoshino.
- Explain update metadata failures and preserve installation mode [#151649](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151649) — @nsikak1.
- Identify failed update checks and retained recovery paths [#151667](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151667) — @soar-king, @baumus, @pfrederiksen, @zzs12345-web, @jplavoiemtl.
- Name the recorded failed update phase instead of an unknown reason [#148879](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/148879) — @navras98, @obviyus, @fuller-stack-dev, @nickfost, @wangzhongming-yh.
- Preserve useful causes and rollback outcomes in update failure reports [#152331](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152331) — @bobbygaerd.
- Avoid rollback warnings when update preparation changed nothing [#152749](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152749) — @fuller-stack-dev.
- Keep failed update operations identifiable in reports [#153425](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153425) — @marcio-absmartly, @bobbygaerd.
- Identify failed update progress writes [#153416](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153416) — @romneyda.
- Explain update health-check timeouts and unavailable repair [#153330](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153330) — @lendersmark, @davidchyi-beep, @soar-king, @bodegaclaw, @guerilla47.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Use supported update repair assistance">

Supported automatic update repair assistance now recognizes eligible older and hybrid Linux cgroup layouts, rechecking that it still belongs to the right service. Triage also explains when an agent CLI is missing from PATH and offers a saved debugging prompt when available. Assistance does not itself prove recovery or authorize a restart, and PATH changes remain yours to make.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Restore automatic update triage on older and hybrid Linux cgroups [#149596](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/149596) — @miorbnli, @fuller-stack-dev.

**Improvements**

- Clarify recovery when triage cannot find an agent CLI [#153408](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153408) — @romneyda.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Inspect logs, audits and worker resource use">

Diagnostic views retain complete structured Gateway logs, identify failed storage inspections and show background-worker memory use, including [Prometheus metrics](/gateway/prometheus) grouped by worker script. Memory-growth alerts distinguish sustained growth from recurring allocation peaks, while absolute pressure checks remain immediate. Samples can be partial, and these diagnostics identify where to investigate rather than fix a memory leak.

Routine model and voice logs are quieter, with detail available at debug level while errors, slow responses and secret-egress audits remain visible. Invalid directory log paths now show an error and keep the last records visibly stale. Routine structured log records can omit `_meta.path`, which matters if your log-processing tools expect that field.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Run audit-history queries outside the Gateway request thread [#152248](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152248).
- Move run-inspection database reads off the Gateway thread [#152561](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152561).
- Wait briefly for database writes during CLI snapshot preparation [#154025](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/154025).
- Reduce false Gateway memory-growth alerts [#151355](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151355).
- Retain complete JSON Gateway logs in status diagnostics [#151747](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151747) — @alix-007.
- Report invalid directory log paths without clearing visible logs [#151809](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151809).

**Improvements**

- Defer health collector loading until text output needs it [#152797](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152797).
- Reduce processing for long verbose log previews [#151593](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151593).
- Reduce routine log overhead while preserving error call sites [#152493](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152493).
- Worker memory visibility and quicker oversized Claude history imports [#153842](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153842).
- Reduce temporary memory in session-count status reads [#153855](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153855).
- Identify which SQLite inspection operation failed [#149516](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/149516) — @liuwqgit, @obviyus, @vyctorbrzezowski.
- Reduce repeated work in task-flow diagnostics [#152347](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152347).
- Read update history without caller-thread database work [#153660](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153660).
- Identify background workers using the most heap memory [#154295](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/154295).
- Stream transcript usage aggregation for raw traces [#150754](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/150754).
- Reduce routine Gateway and Discord voice log noise [#152080](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152080).

</details>

</Accordion>

</AccordionGroup>

## Messaging

Conversations keep more of the context needed to answer a follow-up, and accepted requests have clearer paths to a final reply. Changes across the messaging channels also keep progress, attachments, and delivery results attached to the work they describe.

<AccordionGroup>

<Accordion title="Retain Telegram group history">

[Telegram group and topic history](/channels/telegram/messaging#retained-group-history) now survives restarts and `/new` or `/reset`, so the agent can look back at earlier discussion when a recent message refers to it. The automatic context window stays bounded, normally at 50 messages, while explicit reads can page through older permitted messages. Mention requirements still apply, and reads stay within the authorized account, group, topic, and sender permissions. Only messages OpenClaw received and was allowed to record are retained, and older attachments may expire.

Retained group history grows with traffic. Setting `historyLimit` to `0` turns off automatic context but keeps recording and explicit reads enabled, while resetting a session does not delete that history. Make a compatible pre-update backup if you may need to downgrade, and restore it before running an older release against this data because older versions can damage expanded retained history.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Retain Telegram group discussion and read older messages on demand [#151911](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151911) — Thanks @obviyus.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Finish accepted group requests with an answer">

Accepted group and channel requests now require an answer by default, including unmentioned messages in groups configured to accept them. If tools finish and the agent ends silently, reply recovery can produce the missing answer without repeating completed actions. Confirmed or still-pending delivery prevents another recovery reply, and pending tool or delegated work stays pending while restart recovery waits for capacity.

Always-on groups may receive more replies after updating. To keep optional replies for unaddressed requests, set `agents.defaults.silentReply.group` to `"allow"` or use the corresponding surface override described under [silent replies](/concepts/messages#silent-replies). Mention and access rules still decide which requests reach the agent, while ambient events, heartbeats, and internal helper turns remain optional.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Limits and compatibility**

- Require replies to accepted group requests by default [#153800](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153800) — Thanks @obviyus.

**Bug fixes**

- Recover missing required replies after completed tools without replaying actions [#152786](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152786) — Thanks @obviyus.
- Respect deliberate conversational silence after tool failures [#151873](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151873) — Thanks @pash-openai.
- Keep messages pending during restart-recovery capacity waits [#152475](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152475).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Recover recent Slack and Discord discussion">

After a restart, addressed messages can pick up fresh recent discussion from [Slack](/channels/slack/threads-and-sessions) and [Discord](/channels/discord/threads-and-sessions) without turning quiet room messages into new agent turns. The default windows are 50 messages for Slack and 20 for Discord, with `0` disabling automatic history. Slack's room `historyLimit` also caps `thread.initialHistoryLimit`, including when set to zero. Reads respect current access, edits, deletions, retention, and bounded retrieval, so unavailable history is omitted rather than replaced with stale text. Discord replies also retain quoted bot responses and automation alerts as untrusted context, making follow-ups understandable without copying the original message.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Recover recent Slack and Discord discussion after restarts [#153075](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153075) — Thanks @obviyus.
- Preserve quoted bot text in Discord replies [#151821](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151821) — Thanks @obviyus, @haukeschnau.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Follow delegated work in the right conversation">

Interactive chats can show a waiting acknowledgement when delegated work would otherwise go quiet, and status checks remain visible without interrupting the running task or entering its later model context. Private child results return internally to the correct conversation, while [cross-session requests](/concepts/session-tool) from the Control UI receive their result without bouncing the human-facing response back to the other session. Thread-bound spawning is available again where channel settings and permissions enable it. The waiting acknowledgement is a single update, while independent agent peers keep their existing bounded exchanges.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Prevent Control UI reply exchanges from bouncing between sessions [#151297](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151297) — Thanks @romneyda.
- Resume private subagent results in channel conversations [#152207](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152207).
- Show a default waiting reply during delegated work [#151032](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151032) — Thanks @obviyus, @samiralibabic, @dankarization.
- Keep status exchanges visible without interrupting the running turn [#152512](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152512) — Thanks @obviyus, @samiralibabic.
- Restore enabled thread spawning in Gateway conversations [#153641](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153641) — Thanks @vincentkoc.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Keep Discord progress visible during delegated work">

With Discord `streaming.mode` set to `progress`, an accepted handoff to child agents keeps the existing progress checklist visible while their work continues. The final answer arrives separately, so the checklist remains a useful record of the work in progress. Declined handoffs and media or control-message paths keep their ordinary delivery behavior.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Keep Discord progress cards visible while subagents continue [#153028](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153028) — Thanks @obviyus, @samiralibabic, @dankarization.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Keep Telegram progress and questions in the chat">

Telegram keeps the same progress card and checklist while delegated work continues, including eligible restart recovery when the existing card's delivery record is available. The final answer remains separate and silent child work stays hidden. Interactive questions and their settled outcomes also survive progress cleanup, while temporary previews still retire; a missing delivery record does not create a replacement progress card.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Retain Telegram progress cards while delegated work continues [#152517](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152517) — Thanks @obviyus, @samiralibabic, @dankarization.
- Keep Telegram questions after progress cleanup [#151329](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151329) — Thanks @sercada, @obviyus.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Preserve delivery results and pending messages">

If a channel accepts a reply and saving its status later fails, OpenClaw keeps the known delivery result instead of treating the message as unsent. When only part of a reply arrives, the failure includes what reached the recipient. Pending messages and attachments remain available when the outcome is uncertain, and OpenClaw avoids speculative resends while recovery checks what happened.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Preserve known delivery results when annotation fails [#151217](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151217) — Thanks @shakkernerd.
- Preserve pending deliveries owned by another worker [#153083](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153083) — Thanks @chelsealong, @gabrielnkl.
- Stop repeated recovery retries for obsolete queued replies [#152967](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152967) — Thanks @hartmark, @obviyus.
- Preserve successful replies with opaque channel metadata [#151870](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151870).
- Keep Gateway requests responsive while storing outgoing messages [#153076](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153076).
- Refresh outbound senders when plugin state changes [#153105](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153105).

**Improvements**

- Move persistent channel replay checks into workers [#152642](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152642).
- Prepare cold channel metadata asynchronously before delivery [#149304](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/149304) — Thanks @vincentkoc.
- Reuse conversation-binding query templates [#151654](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151654) — Thanks @vincentkoc.
- Avoid redundant account listing for channel-default sends [#151812](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151812) — Thanks @vincentkoc.
- Reuse compiled conversation delivery lookups [#150627](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/150627).
- Stop duplicate-reply scans after the first match [#153248](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153248).
- Keep delivery claims and renewals off the Gateway thread [#152736](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152736).
- Move outbound delivery acknowledgements off the Gateway thread [#152840](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152840).
- Move pending delivery failure settlement off the Gateway thread [#152892](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152892).
- Reduce initialization during channel discovery [#154046](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/154046).
- Defer optional runtimes on cold text-only sends [#152784](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152784).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Keep accepted chats through compaction and cancellation">

An accepted message can continue once through verified conversation compaction, which condenses older context to make room for more work. When continuation cannot be established, Refresh preserves the draft without sending a second message. Reset, replacement, restart, and cancellation checks still apply, and clients waiting on a canceled chat now receive its completion result after the running dispatch settles instead of waiting until they time out.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Continue accepted chat messages through verified compaction without resending [#152958](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152958).
- Complete waiting clients after chat cancellation [#152597](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152597).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Stop later message requests after cancellation">

Canceled Mattermost sends stop before their next recipient lookup, upload, or posting request, and shared message actions stop later recipient lookups after cancellation. Teams polls also stop before submission when permission to send ends. Already accepted messages and polls keep their delivery results and vote tracking, so cancellation does not retract something already sent or guarantee that an in-flight request can be stopped.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Stop retired Mattermost sends before later provider requests [#151252](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151252) — Thanks @shakkernerd.
- Stop later recipient lookups after message cancellation [#151270](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151270) — Thanks @shakkernerd.

**Security and trust**

- Stop unsubmitted Teams polls after permission withdrawal [#151287](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151287) — Thanks @shakkernerd.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Preserve requested attachments and captions">

Repeated `--media` flags in the [message CLI](/cli/message) now preserve every attachment in command-line order, using each channel's existing sending behavior. Animated WebP files keep their animation within the existing size and model limits, with oversized animations rejected instead of flattened. Attachments also survive when a caption is stripped, without bringing the removed text back into chat. Older tool calls without a recorded publication no longer reconstruct a reply bubble, but their expanded diagnostics remain available.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Preserve animated WebP attachments within existing media limits [#129483](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/129483).
- Preserve all repeated CLI media attachments [#151043](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151043) — Thanks @krausefx, @obviyus, @dmnksss, @mirr0ch1.
- Keep attachments while preventing stripped-caption redisplay [#150694](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/150694) — Thanks @zhangguiping-xydt, @obviyus.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Keep long replies readable">

Long replies keep emoji, flags, and accented characters together when splitting them into messages, except when a single character cluster exceeds the channel's limit. Streamed code examples retain their fences and literal tags, and reply preparation preserves attachment details and the intended reply target through formatting and recovery. Several formatting paths also avoid repeated work on long text and code-heavy replies. Older third-party adapters need the prepared-operation interface to avoid reparsing replies in their own code; existing transcripts are not rewritten.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Keep emoji and accented characters intact when splitting replies [#151959](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151959) — Thanks @etzelm, @obviyus.
- Preserve reply metadata through outbound formatting and hooks [#152231](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152231).
- Preserve code, attachment details and reply intent through streaming and recovery [#146361](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146361).
- Preserve code blocks and tag boundaries in streamed replies [#151693](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151693) — Thanks @vincentkoc.

**Improvements**

- Reduce formatting work when splitting long messages [#151898](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151898) — Thanks @vincentkoc.
- Reduce processing for code-heavy Discord replies [#151828](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151828) — Thanks @vincentkoc.
- Reduce unnecessary processing for plain-text replies [#149829](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/149829).
- Reduce processing work for long plain-text replies [#150230](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/150230).
- Reduce repeated scanning inside long fenced replies [#151664](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151664) — Thanks @vincentkoc.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Use explicit channel names in scripts">

Login, logout, and directory commands now reject an explicitly blank `--channel`, preventing an empty shell variable from silently selecting a configured channel or affecting saved credentials. Omit the flag when you want automatic selection. Valid names, surrounding whitespace, and supported aliases keep their existing behavior.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Protect saved credentials from blank logout selectors [#150497](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/150497) — Thanks @masatohoshino, @fuller-stack-dev.

**Limits and compatibility**

- Reject blank directory channel selectors and require omission for automatic selection [#153731](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153731) — Thanks @masatohoshino, @obviyus.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Browse conversations after account removal">

Removing or disabling a Discord account, or removing a Matrix, Telegram, or Slack account, no longer blocks conversation discovery for the remaining active accounts. Retained history stays intact, while access to retired-account conversations is still restricted. An account that is configured but not yet ready can remain temporarily unavailable.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Conversation lists survive retired channel accounts [#153846](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153846) — Thanks @masatohoshino, @obviyus.
- Keep Discord conversations discoverable after account removal [#152662](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152662).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Identify credential and channel-status problems">

LINE send errors now identify an unreadable configured token file, and Nextcloud Talk distinguishes an unavailable configured bot-secret source from missing credentials. An unavailable QQ Bot secret blocks its own account while healthy accounts remain usable; failed explicit references do not silently fall back, and only unchanged accounts can retain previously working credentials during reload. Live channel status also remains readable from a reachable, authenticated Gateway while local state maintenance is underway.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Identify unavailable LINE token files in send errors [#152769](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152769) — Thanks @obviyus, @edenfunf.
- Keep live channel status available during state maintenance [#153445](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153445).
- Identify unavailable configured Nextcloud Talk bot secrets [#153751](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153751) — Thanks @masatohoshino, @obviyus.
- Isolate missing QQ Bot credentials to the affected account [#128320](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/128320).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Change the voice for the current Discord call">

Speakers already authorized to talk to the agent can ask to list or change the [current Discord call voice](/channels/discord/voice-channels) without also being configured as command owners. A change affects everyone in the shared call, while saved defaults and other owner-only permissions stay in place. That voice access ends with the call or turn, cancellation, or revoked access.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Let authorized Discord speakers change the current call voice [#152044](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152044).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Confirm the first message in a Discord thread">

Discord thread creation now reports an accepted first message separately from the thread's creation-time counters, which may still show zero or no value. Use the delivery result to check whether the initial message was accepted, and inspect uncertain sends before retrying. An empty standalone thread has no message-delivery receipt.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Confirm initial Discord thread messages without misleading creation counters [#150169](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/150169) — Thanks @alix-007, @obviyus, @ludovicfourrage.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Finish accepted Feishu work during shutdown">

Feishu account shutdown waits for accepted message handlers and duplicate-message bookkeeping to finish before releasing their resources. Reaching the five-minute ordering limit does not cancel an already accepted handler, allowing its work to settle during shutdown.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Finish accepted Feishu work before account shutdown [5f7904f1](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/5f7904f16f3c8815e896df1ee33d3afad6fc33ec).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Keep Matrix replies working after reload">

Delayed Matrix replies can obtain their connection after the original message handler finishes normally, and generated images or queued replies can reuse the matching live connection after a configuration-only reload. Startup and synchronization storage work also moves off the Gateway's main thread. Shutdown, account removal, and explicit cancellation still stop pending work.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Keep delayed Matrix replies alive after handler completion [#151063](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151063) — Thanks @synapse-homeroot.
- Restore Matrix image and queued-reply delivery after configuration reloads [#153645](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153645) — Thanks @vincentkoc.
- Move Matrix startup storage off the Gateway thread [#151994](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151994).
- Move Matrix sync-cache work off the Gateway thread [#151845](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151845).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Read the requested Matrix message">

Requesting a specific Matrix message through the [message CLI or agent tool](/cli/message) now returns that event or an error, instead of substituting unrelated recent room history. Existing permissions and encrypted-message limits still apply, while ordinary history reads keep their current behavior. Configured contact, room, and approver checks avoid unnecessary credential reads, and expired secret-storage requests fail promptly while transient network errors can still retry.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Honor exact Matrix message requests [#150554](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/150554) — Thanks @masatohoshino, @fuller-stack-dev.
- Stop retrying expired Matrix requests [#153434](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153434).

**Improvements**

- Check Matrix approvers without unnecessary credential reads [#152246](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152246).
- List configured Matrix contacts and rooms without credential reads [#152124](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152124).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Keep authored Signal links intact">

Signal preserves the authored destination when a link's path, query, or fragment uses different capitalization from its displayed label, keeping case-sensitive links intact. Signal and iMessage also do less formatting work on replies with many styled links while preserving their text and styling.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Preserve case-sensitive link destinations in Signal replies [#151754](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151754) — Thanks @alix-007, @obviyus.

**Improvements**

- Format link-heavy Signal and iMessage messages more efficiently [#152112](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152112).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Diagnose Slack status cleanup failures">

Normal logs now report when Slack fails to clear an accepted working status at the end of a turn, making that failure easier to diagnose. This exposes the cleanup problem without adding retries or repairing Slack Desktop's separate stale-indicator behavior.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Report failed Slack end-of-turn status cleanup [#145778](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145778) — Thanks @alix-007, @obviyus, @joncursi.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Keep context from unavailable Telegram stickers">

When Telegram sticker media is unavailable, its emoji and reply context remain in the conversation instead of leaving a blank turn that could revive an unrelated request. A sticker label fills in when no emoji is available, without adding visual analysis or animated and video sticker downloads.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Keep emoji and reply context for unavailable Telegram stickers [#151461](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151461) — Thanks @obviyus, @sloptop-the-terrible.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Prepare long Telegram replies with less repeated work">

Telegram avoids repeated escaping, link, and HTML-tag processing while preparing replies with the same supported formatting. With `richMessages` enabled, long and nested rich replies also avoid unnecessary repeated checks. The improvement is in local formatting work and varies with the message; it does not establish faster network delivery.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Avoid redundant escaping scans in Telegram prose [#151464](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151464).
- Skip unused link formatting in Telegram text and tables [#151630](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151630).
- Avoid duplicate HTML tag parsing in Telegram formatting [#151715](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151715).
- Reduce preparation work for long Telegram rich replies [#151827](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151827).
- Reduce repeated work in nested Telegram rich formatting [#151965](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151965) — Thanks @vincentkoc.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Restore affected Telegram replies under Bun">

Telegram now selects the complete installed HTTP client under Bun, repairing the affected reply path and `/models` provider picker that could fail with `dispatcher.compose is not a function`. The live verification used a custom Bun build, so this scoped fix does not establish general stock-Bun support.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Restore affected Telegram native replies under Bun [#152055](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152055).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="React to the current Teams message">

Teams can use the current inbound message when adding, removing, or listing reactions in the same conversation, without requiring its message ID to be supplied again. Reactions in another conversation and message deletion still require an explicit ID.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Use current-message context for Teams reactions [#151382](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151382) — Thanks @giodl73-repo.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Keep rapid WhatsApp messages in order">

With inbound batching configured, rapid WhatsApp messages from the same sender can reach the agent together instead of waiting through separate batching delays. Group conversations keep each participant's identity and message order, including when another person's quoted or buffered message interrupts a batch. This restores the existing setting's behavior without introducing a new default.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Batch rapid WhatsApp messages without reordering group participants [#112001](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/112001) — Thanks @mcaxtr, @fuller-stack-dev, @abacha.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Keep Zalo photo captions within limits">

Zalo applies the same photo-caption limit to ordinary and polling replies without splitting the two-part encoding of an emoji at the cutoff. Text beyond 2,000 UTF-16 units is truncated rather than sent as extra messages, and this does not guarantee that every multi-character emoji sequence stays together.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Bound Zalo photo captions consistently across reply paths [#125402](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/125402).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Keep queued iMessage replies attached to their questions">

When several iMessage questions arrive while an answer is being written, queued text and media replies can stay attached to the question that prompted each one. Explicit targets and the `first`, `all`, and `off` reply settings still apply. This requires both the updated iMessage plugin and a host that supports its inferred reply targets; updating the plugin alone is insufficient. The startup database read that establishes the latest seen message also moves off the Gateway's main thread.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Attach queued iMessage answers to their questions [#150626](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/150626) — Thanks @gennadyclaw, @fuller-stack-dev.
- Move iMessage startup database reads off the Gateway thread [#152837](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152837) — Thanks @summitoperations26.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Watching external conversations">

Looking up watched external conversations can now run alongside other OpenClaw requests. When monitoring stops, results that arrive late are discarded.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Move watched-conversation discovery off the Gateway thread [#151731](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151731).

</details>

</Accordion>

</AccordionGroup>

## Memory

Memory search and maintenance can recover from more interruptions, helping saved information stay useful as conversations continue.

<AccordionGroup>

<Accordion title="Find memories when embeddings are unavailable">

When an embedding failure interrupts a compatible index upgrade, you can still find previously indexed memories by keyword. This fallback searches the old index, so it may miss later edits and leaves meaning-based search and the rebuild unavailable until the embedding problem is resolved. Changes to the indexed sources or incompatible index history can still prevent fallback. Repeated empty searches also avoid rebuilding an unchanged empty index, and [memory status](/cli/memory#memory-status) shows the configured local embedding model before loading it.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Keep compatible keyword memory search available during failed embedding rebuilds [abbda386](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/abbda386ef9f5b9221b402bf131eed0db8630260) — thanks [@ruel225](https://github.com/ruel225).
- Avoid rebuilding unchanged empty memory indexes [#152025](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152025).
- Report the configured local model in memory status [#152618](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152618) — thanks [@zyz619963502zyz](https://github.com/zyz619963502zyz), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), and [@Yachiyo1680](https://github.com/Yachiyo1680).

**Improvements**

- Reduce repeated message processing during Active Memory recall [#151778](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151778) — thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Reduce repeated query processing in memory filename searches [#151758](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151758) — thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Reduce repeated project-aware memory ranking work [#151798](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151798) — thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Reuse query processes for repeated native memory searches on file-backed sqlite-vec indexes, retaining up to two processes for 30 idle minutes [#153832](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153832).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Keep memory indexing and saves moving">

History and search indexes can catch up while a conversation continues receiving messages, within a bounded amount of new history. Resets, branches, and larger backlogs still need a fresh rebuild. Completed memory saves keep their successful result if cleanup later fails, and the next sync can recover a lock left by completed local work instead of timing out. Recovery checks the original lock and database so it leaves active work and replacement locks alone.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Let session history and search indexing catch up during active conversations, up to 512 events and 256 KiB of new history [3c9a40aa](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/3c9a40aae8dafa280dd6373156cb0fcf5c466672) — thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa) and CanadaOrNaw, a name-only coauthor.
- Prevent maintenance overlap from leaving memory indexing locks behind [#152361](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152361).
- Recover memory-sync locks after cleanup contention [#152430](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152430).
- Bind memory-lock recovery to its original database and grant, including recovery after a lost acquisition reply without replaying work [#152480](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152480).
- Preserve completed Memory writes through cleanup failures [#153150](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153150).

**Improvements**

- Reduce preparation work for memory notes and session batches [#152339](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152339).
- Reduce vector encoding overhead during memory indexing [#152021](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152021).
- Reduce memory index publication startup overhead [#154155](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/154155).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Maintain conversation context between turns">

[Context engines](/concepts/context-engine) that opt into background maintenance can now start summarizing after a saved conversation turn, using idle time without holding up the completed reply. Engines using foreground maintenance, or leaving the mode unspecified, still wait for that work, and some compaction may still happen during later turns. New conversation summaries also receive independent text blocks with their boundaries preserved, avoiding text being joined together before summarization. Existing summaries remain unchanged.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Start opted-in context maintenance after accepted turns and keep its resources available until it finishes [#151936](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151936) — thanks [@jalehman](https://github.com/jalehman).
- Keep text blocks separated in conversation summary input [#151358](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151358) — thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).

**Improvements**

- Scan signed-turn attachments once during replay repair [#151398](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151398) — thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Avoid repeatedly encoding the same history when retrieving messages around a selected message [#150302](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/150302) — thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Recognizing preserved requests during compaction">

When the compaction quality guard checks a shortened conversation, it now recognizes short requests such as “How about now?” that the summary correctly retained. This avoids unnecessary retries or cancellation caused by that matching error when the guard is enabled.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Recognize short requests retained in compaction summaries [#153320](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153320) — @vacinc

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Save Dream Diary entries and respect paused scheduling">

New Dream Diary entries now save when their recent context was only shortened to fit, instead of being mistakenly discarded as stale. Actual context changes and Forget still prevent stale entries from being written, and previously discarded entries are not recreated. Dreaming also stops trying to reconcile its scheduled jobs while scheduling is disabled, preserving those jobs for later. Explicitly disabling dreaming removes its managed jobs, and resuming scheduling still requires an eligible owner.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Keep Dream Diary entries after unchanged context truncation [#153823](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153823) — thanks [@Psychnix](https://github.com/Psychnix) and [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Defer memory dreaming jobs while automatic scheduling is disabled through `cron.enabled` or `OPENCLAW_SKIP_CRON` [#151713](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151713).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Forget memories when session links change">

[Memory Forget](/cli/memory#memory-forget) keeps track of memories already selected for deletion and picks up new links to the selected sessions while preparing the operation, preserving unrelated content. A preview remains a view of the current state, so pause or coordinate direct agent edits and external writers during a sensitive purge. This repair covers changing session links without extending deletion to every possible copy of the information.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Keep memory forgetting complete when session links change, and reject cleanup through a retired database connection [#152902](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152902).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Use Memory files on a remote workspace host">

Workspace adapters can now provide access to Memory files on a remote host for searching, reading, and maintenance, while the computer running OpenClaw keeps the index and original session data. This optional [workspace capability](/plugins/sdk-entrypoints#workspace-access) requires a separately configured `memoryFiles` adapter and does not move existing storage automatically.

After changing which source files the adapter uses, run `openclaw memory index --force --agent <id>` before searching. Keep the workspace binding unchanged during maintenance. Revoking access does not retract results already read or retained, and embedding requests or multi-step diary updates may continue after their original access checks.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Limits and compatibility**

- Read and maintain remote workspace Memory files through an optional adapter; remote providers without maintenance support fail instead of editing local files [#153124](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153124) — thanks [@kimiyu-186](https://github.com/kimiyu-186).

</details>

</Accordion>

</AccordionGroup>

## Skills

Skills now refresh around actual content changes, while Workshop keeps a clearer record of the skills available to an agent and the reviews that shape them.

<AccordionGroup>

<Accordion title="Refresh Skills when their contents change">

Unchanged [Skills](/tools/skills) no longer trigger needless session rebuilds and chat metadata refreshes, and identical copies stop producing duplicate precedence warnings. Instruction edits and changes to which skill takes priority still refresh the session, while supporting scripts and assets continue updating in sandbox copies. Discovery also handles returning to an idle workspace and, on Windows, deleting and recreating a skill folder or its parent.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Reduce duplicate skill warnings and repeated parsing [#152062](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152062)
- Reduce repeated scans for missing skill folders [#152563](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152563)
- Avoid unrelated scans during Linux Node non-polling Skill discovery [#153436](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153436)
- Skip unnecessary filesystem work when loading local skills [#153777](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153777)
- Match local skill-file icons to the skill picker while keeping other link icons unchanged [#153908](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153908) — thanks @vyctorbrzezowski

**Bug fixes**

- Avoid skill reloads and metadata refreshes when content is unchanged [#151670](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151670)
- Bound retained skill subscriptions across execution workspaces and refresh file-backed skills when returning to an evicted workspace [#127204](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/127204)
- Reduce repeated skill refreshes after shared-directory changes [#154052](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/154052)
- Keep retired skill watchers from restarting folder scans [#152903](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152903)
- Keep Windows skills discoverable after folder recreation [#154273](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/154273)

**Limits and compatibility**

The subscription limit bounds retained workspace subscriptions, not the total number of operating-system file watches. Managed Library revisions remain pinned until explicitly refreshed.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Keep Workshop inventory and reviews current">

[Skill Workshop](/tools/skill-workshop) now lists current skill files and records successful tool-mediated use even when diagnostics are disabled. A rejected proposal remains a failed review when the reviewer sends no reply, and reviews delayed until idle time use the current plugins. Usage counts start with recorded activity rather than filling in older history, exclude native Codex skill activation, and do not follow files when they move.

Normal startup now leaves legacy Workshop data untouched. To repair it, run `openclaw doctor --fix` or `openclaw doctor --yes` with the same state directory and configuration as the affected installation. [Doctor during an update](/cli/doctor) continues to handle that repair.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Move skill usage and Curator status queries off the caller thread [#152319](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152319)
- Prepare only skill readiness counts for status summaries [#152799](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152799)

**Bug fixes**

- Restore recorded skill use and live Workshop inventory [#151048](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151048) — thanks @obviyus
- Record failed Skill Workshop reviews despite silent replies [#152018](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152018)
- Use current plugins for idle Skill Workshop reviews [#152235](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152235) — thanks @jalehman
- Read Workshop event history in background workers [#152583](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152583)

**Limits and compatibility**

- Move legacy Workshop repair out of normal startup [#151392](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151392)

Older clients show only current inventory entries whose dates are known. Usage shutdown draining covers writes already delivered and accepted, not diagnostic events still waiting upstream.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Install and finalize skill archives">

Bzip2 skill archives can now be extracted without installing a system `tar` command. Finalizing an uploaded archive also lets OpenClaw continue handling other work while it hashes and stores the archive, and uploads that expire before finalization or a repeated commit are rejected. Existing extraction permissions and protection for uploads already in use by an installation remain in place.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Install bzip2 skills without system tar and prevent false database snapshot or recovery comparison failures from unequal short reads [#153973](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153973)
- Keep skill archive commits responsive and reject expired uploads [#152816](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152816)

**Limits and compatibility**

Background processing covers archive finalization, not every upload or installation step. Stored formats and upload expiry durations are unchanged.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Read and manage Skills on their workspace host">

Supported remote workspace connections let agent tasks use Skills and supporting files on the machine where the workspace lives. Adapters that also supply the required host information and management operations can show that machine's installed skills, choose matching dependencies, and run approved dependency or source and ClawHub management operations there. Missing capabilities return an error without installing on the Gateway computer. The [workspace adapter documentation](/plugins/sdk-entrypoints) describes the requirements; a document bridge alone does not enable this management, and the paired-node adapter does not support remote source or ClawHub installation, updates or removal.

Keep the remote Skills connection in place until its tasks finish. Replacing it with a document-only connection while a task retains its remote skill list can make that task read supporting files from the Gateway computer instead.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Use Skills and supporting files from a remote workspace host [#153126](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153126) — thanks @kimiyu-186
