- Inspect host Skills and run supported dependency, source and ClawHub management through capable workspace adapters [#153438](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153438) — thanks @kimiyu-186

**Limits and compatibility**

Remote channel-native skill menus remain separate. Provider replacement during an active task remains a known read-ownership limitation.

</details>

</Accordion>

</AccordionGroup>

## Native Apps

The native apps make everyday conversations easier to write, read, and return to, with more room for replies and clearer controls when a connection or permission needs attention.

<AccordionGroup>

<Accordion title="Compose and inspect Android chat messages">

Android chat brings Gallery, File, and Location into the attachment menu, with a microphone tap for dictation and a long press for voice options. Location goes into a draft you can review before choosing Send, using only the foreground permission and precision you allowed. Failed tools and recovery instructions are easier to read, and tapping an eligible reply's timestamp opens its recorded model, token use, and estimated cost. Those details describe one model call, so they are not a whole-conversation total or a final bill.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Simplify Android chat controls and reviewable media and location sharing [#143099](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/143099) — thanks @ivankuznetsov and @iwhatsskill.
- Inspect Android reply usage and model details from its timestamp [#152880](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152880) — thanks @iwhatsskill, with proposal credit to @pestelence2k and Git author Cesura.

**Bug fixes**

- Keep Android attachment removal accessible with long filenames [#151337](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151337) — thanks @iwhatsskill.
- Keep Android chat bubbles consistent during delivery [#151336](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151336) — thanks @iwhatsskill.
- Expose Android tool failures and full recovery guidance [#151332](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151332) — thanks @iwhatsskill.

**Limits and compatibility**

Missing or zero usage details and synthetic reply rows do not supply inferred usage or billing values.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Zoom and pan Android chat images">

Open an Android chat image to pinch, pan, or use visible zoom controls without accidentally closing the preview. Zoom reaches 4×, a double tap switches between 2.5× and the fitted view, and rotating the device refits the image instead of keeping an outdated position.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Zoom and pan Android chat image previews [#152877](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152877) — thanks @iwhatsskill, with proposal credit to @pestelence2k and Git author Cesura.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Set Android text size and reorder navigation accessibly">

Settings → Appearance now offers five text sizes from 90% to 140%, combined with Android's own font scaling. The choice survives restart and stays on this device. Page headings and status text wrap more fully, while sidebar pages gain Move up and Move down accessibility actions so reordering does not depend on dragging. The normal sidebar moves visible pages; Edit pinned items includes all pages.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Choose a device-local text size in Android Appearance [#152878](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152878) — thanks @iwhatsskill, with proposal context from @pestelence2k and Git author Cesura.
- Reorder Android sidebar pages with accessibility actions [#152881](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152881) — thanks @iwhatsskill, with proposal context from @pestelence2k and Git author Cesura.

**Bug fixes**

- Keep Android page headings readable with large text [#151341](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151341) — thanks @iwhatsskill.
- Make Android status text and accessibility labels complete [#151342](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151342) — thanks @iwhatsskill.

**Limits and compatibility**

Text size defaults to 100% and does not sync with Gateway profiles. Some provider metrics can still clip at narrow widths and very large system text sizes.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Read Android connection and setup status">

Android connection forms keep their labels visible after you type and put setup errors beside the relevant fields. Overview gives Chat a clearer entry point and makes connection states and destination rows easier to read, while saved credentials remain masked and replacing them still requires confirmation.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Clarify Android Overview actions and connection status [#151335](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151335) — thanks @iwhatsskill.

**Bug fixes**

- Keep Android Gateway labels and setup errors visible [#151338](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151338) — thanks @iwhatsskill.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Use Android realtime Talk without repeated speech">

Android [Talk](/nodes/talk) can use supported GPT-Live realtime routes advertised by the Gateway and shows which route is active or why device speech was selected. The Codex route tries ChatGPT OAuth first and can fall back to a Platform key; the public API route requires a Platform key. Platform-key use is billed per use, including fallback. An advertised route still needs valid credentials and account access, and changing an active route requires stopping and restarting Talk. Longer replies can use the existing audio buffer more fully, and a stopped session now shows its failure reason in chat.

During realtime Talk, the phone leaves automatic local read-aloud silent, including replies started from another client, so consultation answers are not spoken twice. Text remains visible, and native Talk and explicit read-aloud remain available. Reconnecting to the same Gateway also keeps the selected agent in memory, while new Android voice messages send the recognized words without the app's former added instructions.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Use supported GPT-Live relay routes in Android Talk and explain fallback [#153741](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153741) — thanks @iwhatsskill.
- Keep long Android Talk replies playing and explain failures [#152834](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152834) — thanks @saariuslystoned and @iwhatsskill.
- Stop duplicate Android speech during realtime Talk [#153087](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153087) — thanks @saariuslystoned.
- Preserve Android Talk's agent across Gateway reconnects [#152593](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152593) — thanks @saariuslystoned, @obviyus, and @destinedenergy.
- Send Android Talk transcripts without app-added instructions [#153021](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153021) — thanks @iwhatsskill, @vacinc, and @guarismo.

**Improvements**

- Translate Android Talk status guidance [#153967](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153967).

**Limits and compatibility**

Explicit native mode, forced consultation, and configured Azure endpoints retain their native or restricted behavior. Updating only the Gateway can change an older Android app's route; that mixed-version audio path and independent subscription-audio verification remain pending. The audio buffer remains bounded at 12 MiB. Agent selection does not survive an app restart or Gateway switch, and historical voice messages are unchanged.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Keep Talk consultations visible and complete">

Talk keeps valid consultation answers when speech transcription finishes late and shows each new answer once in Chat. Your spoken request remains readable while generated instructions for handing work to the agent stay out of the visible conversation. On supported interruptible relay calls, interrupting a reply also lets you continue speaking without immediately losing the call when the provider is slow to confirm the stop.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Preserve Talk answers across late speech transcripts [#150536](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/150536) — thanks @marvinthebored, @peetiegonzalez, @shakkernerd, @dimonnld, @saariuslystoned, and @goldenrodscript.
- Keep Talk consultation startup valid as finalized speech enters history [#150283](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/150283) — thanks @mertbasar0, @shakkernerd, @dimonnld, and @saariuslystoned.
- Prevent duplicate Talk consultation answers [#153140](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153140) — thanks @shakkernerd.
- Hide generated Talk steering instructions from visible chat [#153698](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153698) — thanks @shakkernerd.
- Keep Talk calls connected after interrupted replies [#152427](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152427) — thanks @saariuslystoned, @obviyus, and @dimonnld.

**Limits and compatibility**

Newer tasks, cancellation, and completed answers still retire older requests. Existing history is not rewritten. After an interrupted relay reply, microphone input resumes after one second while the old output stays suppressed; another 30 seconds without a provider response boundary can end the session. Non-interruptible providers and explicit session closure retain their existing behavior.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Read full replies on Wear OS">

Long watch replies now offer Read full reply, with scrolling, part navigation, and retry controls beyond the compact preview. Update both the watch app and phone companion; Chat also needs a Gateway that supports full-message retrieval. Voice status and microphone guidance fit small watches more comfortably, and context selections expose their selected state to accessibility services. Live Chat stays a preview until a stored reply is available, and older Talk text can become unavailable after its session data is retired.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Read complete assistant replies on Wear OS [#152041](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152041) — thanks @iwhatsskill.

**Bug fixes**

- Make Wear context selections accessible and easier to read [#151296](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151296) — thanks @iwhatsskill.
- Keep Wear Voice text and controls usable on small watches [#151868](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151868) — thanks @iwhatsskill.
- Keep Wear update guidance accurate after Refresh [#151779](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151779) — thanks @iwhatsskill.

**Limits and compatibility**

Full replies have a one-million-character limit and show explicit messages for unsupported, changed, unavailable, or oversized text.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Keep typing after sending photos on iOS">

Sending a photo in native iOS chat no longer triggers the reported composer freeze, so you can send the attachment and continue typing without restarting the app. This repairs the send-time focus problem; keeping sent photos visible after history refresh remains separate work.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Prevent native iOS photo-send composer freezes [#154082](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/154082) — thanks @solvely-colin, @eragim, @kittitys, @rwoverdijk, and @hhkwoong.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Keep one Mac Quick Chat composer">

Native Mac [Quick Chat](/platforms/mac/webchat#quick-chat-bar) keeps one composer beneath the conversation, preserving your draft and streamed reply when you collapse and reopen it. Separate model and Effort controls make thinking levels and supported Fast mode easier to choose, while the context ring offers Compact Thread. Model pickers keep usable choices visible without unrelated discovery warnings, and capture actions sit in the plus menu. Screenshot capture still sends after selection.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Use one Quick Chat composer with preserved conversation state [#153383](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153383) — thanks @benediktweber for the related proposal.

**Bug fixes**

- Remove unrelated discovery warnings from native model pickers [#154015](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/154015) — thanks @romneyda.

**Limits and compatibility**

Model discovery failures remain visible in Models settings and the CLI. The Quick Chat layout here belongs to the native Mac app.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Draft beneath the latest Tauri Quick Chat exchange">

The Tauri desktop app puts your latest exchange above a separate bottom composer, letting you prepare the next message while a reply streams. Collapsing the reply preserves the draft, live text, and widget interactions; sending and switching agents wait until the current turn finishes. Cmd/Ctrl+Shift+O now reaches the foreground app for New Session instead of summoning a background dashboard. The separate configurable Quick Chat shortcut remains available, as described in the [desktop app guide](/platforms/linux).

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Redesign Tauri Quick Chat and simplify Apple thinking labels [#153564](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153564).

**Bug fixes**

- Return the New Session shortcut to the foreground app [#154118](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/154118).

**Limits and compatibility**

The retired background dashboard shortcut also removes its Enable Global Shortcut tray toggle. The Apple changes simplify effective thinking-level labels and retain the reset-to-default action; they do not apply the Tauri layout to Apple apps.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Restore saved Gateways in the Tauri app">

The Tauri app on macOS can reopen saved local or remote Gateways without the reported startup crash. If the launch environment prevents keychain access, its warning now gives appropriate recovery guidance, including reopening from Finder when relevant.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Prevent saved-Gateway startup crashes in the Tauri app [#154028](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/154028) — thanks @vacinc for credited prerequisite work.
- Correct saved-Gateway keychain recovery warnings [#154151](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/154151).

**Limits and compatibility**

Reopening from Finder is recovery guidance, not a guarantee that every keychain problem is repaired.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Keep Mac Gateway ownership and connection status clear">

Opening the native Mac app or changing connection modes leaves independently managed Gateways and other services running. Saved Gateways show the current connection status from their Dashboard windows, even when they are not the primary connection, and About can copy version and build details while offline. Resolve a port conflict through the service that owns it; a green connection indicator describes a connection, not the health of every service behind it.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Keep independently managed Gateways running when the Mac app opens [#153474](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153474).
- Show live connection status for saved macOS Gateways [#152638](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152638).

**Improvements**

- View and copy Mac build information offline [#153184](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153184).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Start Windows background helpers without a blank console">

Updated Windows startup and recovery helpers avoid opening an extra blank console during background launches. Interactive commands keep their terminal, and the change takes effect when the replacement helpers run.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Hide replacement consoles during Windows background startup [#152452](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152452).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Request Mac access and read recent app activity">

Mac permission controls can request missing Screen Recording or Accessibility access through Grant, with System Settings nearby when more help is needed. After updating both the native app and Gateway, interacting with OpenClaw also gives agents basic [recent-computer activity](/nodes/presence) without Accessibility permission. The renamed System-wide presence detection option stays off by default and needs Accessibility for activity in other apps. Turning it off now disables only system-wide detection, including for previously disabled preferences; app-local activity remains eligible and does not prove who is physically at the computer or which device sent a message.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Restore Mac screen and accessibility permission requests [#153263](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153263).
- Make Mac inspection menus follow the Developer setting [#153273](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153273).
- Release memory used to read audio-input names and IDs [#151455](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151455) — thanks @vincentkoc.

**Improvements**

- Restore Mac activity context and Canvas media playback [#153381](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153381).

**Limits and compatibility**

Merely opening or foregrounding the app does not count as recent input. Removing the unused Terminal automation row does not revoke macOS grants, and hiding inspection menus is a presentation setting rather than a security boundary.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Play HTTPS audio and video in Mac Canvas widgets">

Canvas widgets can play direct HTTPS audio and video in inline previews, dashboards, and native panels. Update both the Mac app and Gateway, then update or recreate older saved widgets to receive the new media policy. Browser format support and autoplay rules still apply, so playback controls remain useful. Media requests can reach HTTPS hosts beyond a widget's separately granted API connections; the [widget media guide](/tools/show-widget#audio-and-video) explains the distinction.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

The shared source and complete accounting for this media change appear in Request Mac access and read recent app activity above.

**Limits and compatibility**

Media hosting servers receive the client's request. Keep private data and credentials out of media URLs. API requests, scripts, images, and navigation retain their separate restrictions.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Recognize Chrome setup across Mac app versions">

Mac settings distinguish an installed Chrome extension from one that needs enabling, and provide separate guidance when its helper needs repair. Older Mac apps keep a usable setup path with a newer Dashboard, including an unknown status when the older app cannot establish whether the extension is installed. Installation status remains separate from a live browser connection.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Recognize installed Chrome extensions in Mac settings [#153266](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153266).
- Preserve Chrome setup across Mac app and dashboard versions [#153289](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153289).

**Improvements**

- Refresh translated Chrome setup guidance [#153411](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153411).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Keep the desktop awake for local work">

The Tauri tray menu adds an optional Keep computer awake setting beside Start at Login for long-running local work. It is off by default, remembers the choice on this device, and stays active when windows close to the tray until you disable it or quit. On Linux it can also delay automatic dimming and locking; manual lock, explicit sleep, and lid behavior remain under operating-system control. The native Mac app adopts the clearer name for its existing setting, which still requires a connected, hosting, unlocked Mac.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Add the optional Keep computer awake desktop setting [#152420](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152420).

**Limits and compatibility**

Linux needs a working session manager or idle-inhibition portal. A saved preference that cannot activate is marked inactive and can still be turned off. Windows and macOS Tauri requests prevent system idle sleep without requesting that the display stay on.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Finish hosted worker cleanup across restarts">

Hosted workers track unfinished commands through cancellation and node restarts, freeing occupied slots after cleanup is confirmed. Update the Gateway and the node hosts or Mac app together, and restart the updated hosts; older nodes may show Update required. Linux and macOS gain stronger cleanup, while Windows keeps a slot reserved when cleanup is uncertain. Let active workers finish before downgrading to an older version.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Track hosted worker cleanup across cancellation and node restarts [8277325](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/8277325ccf1b616d3ff911b4a4102133319e8564).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Reduce Mac app size while retaining required helpers">

Mac packaging trims files the app's private worker does not need, reducing the universal app's size while retaining Apple silicon and Intel support. The packaging correction also keeps nested helpers needed by bundled subprocess support, including configured stdio MCP servers. Normal npm installations keep their CLI and Gateway functionality.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Reduce universal macOS app package size [#150773](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/150773) — thanks @romneyda.

**Bug fixes**

- Retain nested worker helpers in Mac app packaging [#154291](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/154291).

**Limits and compatibility**

Recorded size reductions come from controlled package comparisons, not a final signed release download. The later helper repair has native arm64 staging evidence; that staging does not establish publication of a new app asset.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Keep the Mac Dashboard visually consistent">

The Mac Dashboard follows the native window's appearance while loading, avoiding a white flash before the selected theme appears. The collapsed-sidebar Inbox also matches the other titlebar actions and leaves room for its unread badge.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Prevent the macOS Dashboard's white loading flash [#153837](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153837).
- Align the Mac titlebar Inbox and unread badge [#153241](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153241).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Read permitted attachments through Mac folder aliases">

Audio and document attachments can be read through equivalent macOS paths such as `/tmp` and `/private/tmp`. The opened file must still be inside a permitted folder, so this fixes path handling without extending access to neighboring folders or shared workspaces.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Read authorized media through macOS directory aliases [#136218](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/136218).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Read updated native controls in existing languages">

Existing native-app languages receive refreshed labels for Android controls, Wear reply reading, Apple chat and progress, and Mac consent screens. The translations help explain the current controls and permissions without introducing new languages or changing what those permissions allow.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Refresh native app labels and reply-reading translations [#152164](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152164).
- Refresh native chat and progress translations [#153464](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153464).
- Refresh translated macOS Automation permission explanations [#153578](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153578).
- Translate Android controls and macOS desktop-sharing consent [#153746](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153746).
- Refresh native tool-activity translations [#151702](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151702).

</details>

</Accordion>

</AccordionGroup>

## Models and Providers

Models settings brings provider connections and model choices together, while installed coding agents gain more ways to participate in ordinary chat. This release also adds optional models for structured decisions and repairs how provider failures, usage, and Codex conversations are handled.

<AccordionGroup>

<Accordion title="Models settings and runtime choices">

[Models settings](/web/control-ui/settings) now brings global defaults and the selected agent's provider connections into one workflow, preserving unfinished setup input during rescans. Saving credentials leaves model activation as a separate choice, and changing a model keeps your selected runtime unless you explicitly change or reset it. Incompatible combinations are rejected without changing either selection. Model and effort controls remain usable in narrow chats, with more accurate refresh feedback, supported thinking choices, and Fast preferences for delegated work.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Unify model defaults, provider connections and Gateway discovery in Models settings [#147794](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147794).

**Bug fixes**

- Preserve runtime choices when selecting a model [#152394](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152394) — @obviyus.
- Prevent Gateway hangs for agents without runtime pins [#153386](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153386) — @stackingrockss.
- Offer thinking levels only when declared labels or explicit mappings support them [#145248](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145248) — @MasterSwords1, @obviyus, @itanyplus.
- Scope inherited Fast mode to the same model [#153251](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153251) — @MertBasar0, @holny, @obviyus, @Oldrich333.
- Keep model refresh progress compact and accurate [#153932](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153932).
- Keep model and effort pickers separate in narrow chat panes [#153616](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153616).
- Give the provider-loading dialog its screen-reader name [#152733](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152733) — @obviyus.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Installed coding agents in chat">

You can select installed OpenCode, Qwen Code, Pi ACP, and Kilo Code agents from Models settings and use their streamed replies in ordinary chat. Install and sign in to the app on the computer running OpenClaw, then use the discovery feedback and Check again control to resolve unavailable models. Detection alone does not confirm sign-in. [Native-agent setup](/tools/acp-agents-setup) explains how an administrator's **Continue for this chat** grants Full Access, turns off optional sandboxing for that chat, and consents to the native app's permissions. Required sandbox and workspace boundaries still apply.

An explicitly selected CLI that is unavailable now reports the failure instead of silently switching to API access. Restore that CLI or explicitly choose an API route. Claude's picker labels help distinguish those routes, but an API-key account still incurs API charges through Claude CLI. Native consent must be renewed after a reset, runtime change, or stronger settings and does not carry into forks; deleting the OpenClaw session leaves the native app's history intact.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Select and manage supported installed coding agents from Models and chat [#150224](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/150224) — @obviyus.
- Clarify Claude CLI, API and Default routes before model selection [#153707](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153707) — @obviyus.

**Bug fixes**

- Show installed-agent failures and retry model discovery [#153693](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153693) — @obviyus.
- Avoid false sign-in prompts while native models are discovered [#153796](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153796) — @obviyus.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="GitHub Copilot CLI models in chat">

An installed, signed-in GitHub Copilot CLI can now supply models for ordinary web and channel conversations. [Copilot setup](/tools/acp-agents-setup#github-copilot-cli-in-native-chat) uses the account running OpenClaw on that computer, with access and billing owned by Copilot; explicit bring-your-own-key settings and environment tokens can affect which account is used. Discovery is enabled by default, and disabling it stops new discovery and turns while admitted work continues. These native turns use the app's host permissions, without OpenClaw sandbox or workspace-only confinement.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Select installed GitHub Copilot CLI models for ordinary chat [#153799](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153799) — @obviyus.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Claude CLI launches and long-running commands">

Claude CLI keeps follow-up tools and the final answer in the original turn when a foreground Bash command takes long enough to be moved into the background automatically. Commands intentionally started in the background still end the turn promptly and do not gain independent later delivery. Supported npm-installed wrappers now launch on Windows, long reasoning streams require less repeated parsing, and recovery timing stays within its existing budget when the system clock changes.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Reduce CPU work for long CLI reasoning streams [#152191](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152191).

**Bug fixes**

- Preserve Claude CLI follow-up tools and answers after foreground Bash timeouts [#149967](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/149967) — @marvinthebored, @peetiegonzalez, @shakkernerd.
- Launch supported npm-installed Claude CLI wrappers on Windows [#138504](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/138504) — @LiuwqGit, @obviyus, @Vasanthdev2004.
- Keep CLI recovery timing stable across clock changes [#150817](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/150817) — @tzy-17, @altaywtf.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Anthropic OAuth client compatibility">

Direct [Anthropic OAuth requests](/providers/anthropic) now advertise a maintained Claude client version to address newer models rejecting an outdated client identity. OpenClaw uses at least version 2.1.278 or a newer installed stable Claude CLI, with the result cached until restart. This updates request compatibility while leaving account entitlement, billing policy, and local model restrictions unchanged.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Update Anthropic OAuth client identity for newer models [#154098](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/154098) — @KrasimirKralev, @SPRIC76, @Cyb3rb1ade, @raghidtawil-lab, @davidcittadini, @Kiiatkin, @fray-ai, @Oldrich333.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Decision Models and TypeSafe Jev">

[Decision Models](/concepts/decision-models) give supporting plugins a separate model for choosing among options, scoring supplied evidence, or estimating a yes/no probability. The role is off by default and can be selected globally, inherited, overridden, or disabled for individual agents. It requires both a supporting provider and a feature that uses it; selecting a model does not start background work, replace chat, or grant permission to act on its answers.

[TypeSafe Jev](/plugins/typesafe) provides a hosted option through the separately packaged, optional TypeSafe plugin. Enable it, configure an API-key SecretRef, and select Jev or Jev 1.13.0 to send evidence to TypeSafe with its normal API charges. The evaluation tool has its own controls. Registry publication is pending, so the guide includes source-checkout setup; packaged use requires OpenClaw and plugin API 2026.9.6 or newer. If replacing an external prototype, save and reapply its settings, credentials, and policy, because `uninstall --keep-files` does not preserve the configuration.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Choose separate Decision Models for structured plugin decisions [#152237](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152237) — @jalehman.
- Add opt-in TypeSafe AI Jev decision models [#152298](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152298) — @jalehman.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Local ONNX decision models">

The optional [ONNX plugin](/plugins/onnx) runs structured decisions locally on the CPU, without sending the decision evidence to a hosted inference service. Its seven presets include five with explicit pinned downloads and two requiring local exports. Installation, enablement, model preparation, and a Decision Model selection are all explicit; registry publication is pending, with source-checkout and locally built package setup documented. Packaged installs require OpenClaw and plugin API 2026.9.6 or newer.

Decision evaluations can now be allowed up to ten seconds, with shorter caller or provider deadlines still respected. Large cold models may need preloading or a smaller model. This provides a local backend for supporting consumers, without automatically routing conversations or adding a chat fallback.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Add optional local ONNX decision models and setup guides [#153476](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153476) — @jb510.

**Limits and compatibility**

- Allow decision evaluations up to ten seconds [#153817](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153817).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Local TypeSafe Kev decisions">

The [TypeSafe plugin](/plugins/typesafe) can also use a separately running local Kev server for the same [Decision Model](/concepts/decision-models) choices, scores, and probabilities. Configure its loopback endpoint and select Kev after starting the server yourself. The server owns the model weights, and that endpoint applies to every model selected through the plugin. Local mode uses no hosted key and has no automatic fallback to hosted Jev. This uses the separate optional plugin and its source-checkout setup while registry publication is pending, with OpenClaw and plugin API 2026.9.6 required for packaged installs.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Run TypeSafe decisions through a local Kev server [#154059](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/154059).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Model discovery and retry feedback">

Model discovery can use saved credentials in custom agent directories, refresh eligible empty catalogs after their cache expires, and retain existing choices when a catalog refresh fails. Failures and Retry remain visible even while other providers are still loading, so you can act on the problem without losing a working selection. OAuth diagnostics also identify the affected agent and profile with the appropriate sign-in command.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Restore model discovery from custom agent directories [#151458](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151458) — @obviyus.
- Refresh successful empty provider catalogs after expiry [#151580](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151580) — @obviyus.
- Retain failed-refresh status after model-catalog worker recovery [#151597](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151597) — @obviyus.
- Keep model discovery failures, retries and save errors visible [#153703](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153703) — @obviyus.
- Explain OAuth failures during model discovery with scoped recovery guidance [#139079](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/139079) — @StrajnarFilip, @obviyus, @jzakirov.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Model catalog resource use">

Repeated model and authentication refreshes avoid whole-database copies and limit temporary plugin copies created by current discovery workers. Catalog preparation also reuses more existing work and stops recurring retirement checks while workers are idle. The repair takes effect in newly started workers; temporary copies left by older killed processes are not removed automatically.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Stop recurring retirement polling in idle model-catalog workers [#152800](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152800).
- Index configured models for repeated selection [#151442](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151442) — @vincentkoc.
- Reduce repeated provider catalog copying [#151849](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151849) — @vincentkoc.

**Bug fixes**

- Bound model-catalog temporary copies and stop closed-client retry loops [#153038](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153038) — @mrzeepek, @Conan-Scott, @ge0el, @cnflwzh, @lishewen, @zl0nline, @shakkernerd, @flyto168, @msobrosa, @islandpreneur007, @JoPaMu, @safrano9999.
- Reuse plugin source copies during model catalog refresh [#153041](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153041) — @shakkernerd, @mrzeepek, @zl0nline.
- Prepare model catalogs asynchronously with consistent configuration [#152142](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152142).
- Avoid repeated database copies during model refreshes [#153130](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153130) — @dmlau76, @mirasrael, @vyctorbrzezowski.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Credential preparation during model requests">

OpenClaw avoids more repeated storage reads, process starts, filesystem checks, and copying while preparing model credentials, allowing other Gateway work to progress during asynchronous reads. Reuse is invalidated when relevant credentials or ownership change. Credentials changed by another process can take up to 100 milliseconds to become visible through the cached path, while changes made within the running process invalidate it immediately.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Yield during stored model-credential reads [#149309](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/149309).
- Reduce Gateway pauses during concurrent credential reads [#152140](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152140).
- Reuse unchanged authentication reads across Gateway turns [#152266](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152266).
- Reduce copying during cached model-authentication reads [#152504](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152504).
- Reuse authentication readers during model fallback [#152016](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152016).
- Reduce filesystem work on cached credential reads [#152719](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152719).
- Avoid redundant header copying before model requests [#152722](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152722).
- Reuse module-loading work during model selection [#152732](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152732).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Provider retries and configured fallbacks">

Conversations and scheduled work can reach configured alternatives sooner when a provider asks for a rate-limit wait longer than the saved [retry cap](/concepts/retry). This requires a configured model fallback, a positive cap, and a request that can be replayed safely; zero disables the cap. Switching accounts or models sooner can change costs and answers. Long tasks also regain their transient-outage recovery window after a completed successful model response, while overall retry and run limits remain in force.

Eligible background text requests can try prepared backup accounts while respecting explicit account pins, and canceled requests stop waiting for a shared credential refresh. Saved cloud failures retain their cause, partial output, and usage. Check what already completed before retrying a failed run, because earlier tool actions may have taken effect.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Use configured fallbacks when safe rate-limit waits exceed the saved cap [#148580](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/148580) — @azuretek, @altaywtf, @cookywook, @oldrich333, @binchenutrecht.
- Renew transient retry windows after successful model responses [#151610](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151610).
- Retry eligible background completions with prepared backup accounts [#151133](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151133) — @anyech, @obviyus.
- Retry model selection after an overlapping credential refresh [#152426](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152426).
- Finish canceled requests during shared OAuth refresh [#152664](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152664).
- Preserve model failure causes and cloud-worker fallback runtime [#152028](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152028).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Provider error messages">

Budget errors no longer trigger futile retries merely because a billing-help URL contains limit-related wording. Upstream server failures also report as server errors instead of misleading timeouts, while genuine rate limits, timing failures, and overload retain their existing handling.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Avoid futile budget-error retries caused by help URLs [#151377](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151377) — @obviyus.
- Distinguish upstream server errors from genuine timeouts [#141843](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/141843) — @amittell, @obviyus, @jnikolaidis.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Responses and Chat Completions tool continuity">

Compatible custom Responses endpoints preserve optional tool inputs, and managed Chat Completions conversations retain the encrypted provider state needed to continue after tools. Eligible Responses stream conflicts can recover within the existing retry allowance without repeating earlier completed tools. Recovery is limited to qualifying completed responses that have not already exposed output from the failing response, and excludes provider-hosted tools.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Preserve optional tool arguments on custom Responses routes that declare support for the strict field, allowing `strict` to be false [#151416](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151416).
- Normalize malformed Responses tool-call results [#151044](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151044) — @RomneyDa.
- Preserve matching provider state through tool continuations [#150633](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/150633) — @zhangguiping-xydt, @obviyus.
- Recover eligible Responses stream conflicts without repeating completed actions [#152947](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152947).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Tool schema compatibility">

Gemini tool requests no longer include the internal optional-field metadata that could cause rejection. Plugin authors also get deeper finite-schema support in the Gemini normalizer and unsupported-keyword cleaner, avoiding recursive stack overflow in those two paths while still rejecting circular objects.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Prevent Gemini rejection of optional-field metadata [#151327](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151327) — @zesming, @Takhoffman.
- Clean deeply nested tool schemas without recursive stack overflow [#152990](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152990) — @obviyus, @hpyhandsome, @wangmiao0668000666.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Long-conversation context preparation">

Configured cache-TTL pruning now removes expired tool output on eligible OpenAI and ChatGPT/Codex routes. It remains opt-in, and custom proxies must explicitly support prompt-cache keys. Preparing long conversations also avoids some repeated request-copying and Unicode cleanup work while preserving valid multilingual text and emoji.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Reduce temporary allocations when fingerprinting long provider requests [#151603](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151603).
- Reduce Unicode text-preparation work [#151950](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151950).

**Bug fixes**

- Restore configured cache-TTL pruning on eligible OpenAI routes [e0e6ce7b](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/e0e6ce7bab919a9e37079841b6ee399cb90631b8) — @original4422.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Model costs and Luna Reserve usage">

Unknown model costs are now left unavailable instead of appearing as free usage, while explicitly free rates and provider-billed zero totals remain zero. Recorded charges retain their request-time pricing. Plugin consumers should handle the optional `usage.costUsd` field being absent.

Codex usage views now label [Luna Reserve](/plugins/codex-harness#luna-reserve-and-credit-usage) as a separate route. Unused Reserve quota does not cover ordinary Luna requests, including with Fast off, and ordinary Luna may still consume credits. This clarifies the display without enabling Reserve routing; account counters are not per-request billing receipts.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Distinguish unavailable model prices from genuinely free usage [#111175](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/111175) — @xydigit-zt, @obviyus, @dltlaos11.

**Limits and compatibility**

- Explain Luna Reserve separately in Codex usage displays [#152383](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152383) — @fuller-stack-dev, @saariuslystoned.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Search provider choices in coding agents">

Claude Code, Codex CLI, and Gemini CLI now honor explicit search-provider and disabled-search settings. Eligible native OpenAI search also keeps its selected route through Tool Search and Code Mode. Choosing a managed provider suppresses native fallback even when that provider's connection fails, so a failed connection does not silently change your search choice.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Honor search provider and disable settings in coding CLIs [#154143](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/154143).
- Keep native search on its chosen provider route [#154084](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/154084).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Deepgram multichannel transcription">

Deepgram prerecorded transcription now retains speech from later audio channels, including recordings whose first track is silent. It uses the best alternative from each channel in the provider's order; it does not interleave the tracks chronologically or add speaker labels.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Retain Deepgram transcripts from later audio channels [#151343](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151343) — @leon-sk668, @obviyus.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Local transcription setup checks">

Doctor can identify missing commands or arguments in CLI media-tool setup, and transcription rejects those incomplete commands before launching them while retaining model fallback. It provides guidance without rewriting your settings. Audio discovery also skips Whisper model scans when `whisper-cli` is not installed.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Skip Whisper model discovery without its executable [#152570](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152570).

**Bug fixes**

- Diagnose incomplete CLI media tools before transcription [#151969](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151969) — @fede-kamel.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="ChatGPT image-generation errors">

When image generation through ChatGPT sign-in fails, OpenClaw now shows the provider's available explanation or refusal. The bounded, sanitized diagnostics help explain the result while leaving the provider's policies and failure rules unchanged.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Explain failed ChatGPT image requests with provider diagnostics [26c8cd22](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/26c8cd22713ff4318b4567245d5897d56dc20e77) — @rondavis007, Ayaan Zaidi (coauthor).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="llmman local and hybrid setup">

Expanded [llmman guidance](/providers/llmman) explains existing local and hybrid setup, model names, credentials, and troubleshooting. It documents the existing integration rather than adding a new provider or routing feature.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Documentation**

- Expand llmman local and hybrid inference setup guidance [#139606](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/139606) — @ericcurtin, @sallyom.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="xAI device sign-in validation">

xAI device sign-in now rejects malformed token-response bytes before they can be saved as corrupted credentials. This check applies during device-token polling and leaves existing credentials and refresh handling unchanged.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Reject malformed xAI device-login credentials before saving [#111737](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/111737) — @zengwen-dt, @fuller-stack-dev.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Codex accounts and migration">

Codex account status now identifies the selected connection and reports a deleted profile's own errors instead of showing another subscription as active. Credential import asks for consent before inspection, and upgrade guidance distinguishes missing local profiles from provider authentication failures. Follow the agent- and profile-specific recovery guidance and verify the result with an unfiltered auth list; credentials are not imported automatically. [Migration guidance](/cli/migrate) also explains when secret inspection can still prompt for Keychain access.

Supervised turns retain their [local Codex configuration and login](/plugins/codex-harness/native-features). A shared Codex daemon still shares its login across chats, without per-chat account isolation or fallback to OpenClaw credentials. Changed policy may require a reconnect or retry.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Correct Codex control permissions, node cleanup, and active-account reporting [#151728](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151728).
- Show errors for the selected Codex account [#151831](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151831).
- Preserve Codex authentication choices and migration consent, with direct `allowKeychainPrompt=false` imports skipping all credential inspection, including files [#151761](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151761).
- Explain missing Codex profiles after upgrades without false HTTP 401 errors [#153051](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153051) — @msobrosa, @kpwillis.
- Preserve local Codex setup across supervised turns [#151001](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151001) — @galiniliev, @iXandru.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Codex app approvals and plugin availability">

Scheduled Codex app calls retain the connected-account approval requirements captured when the schedule was created, alongside current policy. Plugin installation refreshes shared information without treating another conversation's readiness as proof that an app is usable in this one, and prompts dismiss when another connected client actually answers them. Missing configured plugins no longer restrict otherwise eligible connected apps, while a present plugin disabled by an administrator retains its restrictions. Older custom runtimes may need `/new` or `/reset` after installation.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Preserve scheduled Codex app approvals and refresh plugin state [#151757](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151757).
- Keep connected Codex apps available when configured plugins are missing [#150255](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/150255) — @sjf-oa, @sjf.
- Dismiss Codex requests resolved by another client [#151381](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151381).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Codex launch and restart recovery">

Codex turns can start after slow process-registration reads and recover from stale records for exited processes after an OpenClaw restart. Valid native configuration forms and managed Bun launch checks also receive the intended handling. Verified setup requires a local native executable or official npm launcher, so an app-server proxy is not a substitute on that setup path. Persistent process-recovery failures still need inspection rather than manual deletion of records.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Correct Codex TOML parsing and managed Bun launch verification [#151686](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151686).
- Start Codex turns after slow registration reads [#153282](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153282).
- Recover Codex turns blocked by stale process records [#153454](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153454).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Codex background text completions">

Eligible local, agent-scoped Codex background titles and narratives can run with administrator hooks in a verified isolated environment. Model-callable tools, apps, and MCP remain unavailable there, and unsupported launchers may need a directly executable wrapper. Optional finalization can also choose silence without generating a missing-summary fallback, while required replies and failure reporting remain intact.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Restore isolated Codex background completions with administrator hooks [#151658](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151658).
- Respect optional Codex finalization silence [#151951](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151951) — @vincentkoc.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Codex workspace instructions and side questions">

Resumed Codex sessions retain their captured inherited `AGENTS.md` instructions even if the source file changes or disappears; start a new session to load edited instructions. Eligible [`/btw` side questions](/tools/btw) regain inherited shell tools, and cancellation settles the side question's native background terminals or reports when stopping cannot be confirmed. Main-conversation terminals and OpenClaw background jobs keep their existing lifetimes.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Preserve Codex workspace snapshots, completion during delayed prompt saves, and side-question cleanup, while honoring explicit reasoning and permitted empty background replies [#151753](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151753).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Codex child results and reply delivery">

Delegated Codex work now completes from native completion events or recovered history, preventing parent commentary from being mistaken for a child's finished result. Parents can resume after yielding to deliver completed work, and eligible saved results can restore a missing final summary without repeating completed actions. Reply handling also distinguishes delivered, queued, and failed output while preserving unsent images and finalized text.

Automatic approval remains limited to one execution, with persistent trust requiring an explicit choice and empty approval forms requiring Allow or Decline. Recovery retains evidence of prior tool effects, but uncertain partial media delivery can still require checking what arrived. Updated Codex and Copilot plugins need a matching core SDK for the delegated-result repair.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Copy only matching task history during harness recovery [#152790](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152790) — @fuller-stack-dev.

**Bug fixes**

- Use native Codex events for child completion [#151680](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151680).
- Recover Codex summaries after background work finishes [#153538](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153538).
- Preserve Codex delivery evidence, image replies and approval scope [#151863](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151863).
- Preserve multiline Codex output without repeated parsing stalls [#151399](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151399).
- Reject malformed and nonterminal Codex completion events [#151373](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151373).
- Preserve background tool effects in Codex retry decisions [#151371](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151371).
- Deliver delegated results after a Codex parent yields [#152172](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152172).
- Restore plugin worker history access and avoid update cleanup loops [#153772](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153772) — @lidashi1025.
- Avoid Gateway pauses during Codex process registration and cleanup, with updated plugins requiring a host that supports asynchronous process storage [#152820](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152820) — @fuller-stack-dev.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Codex conversation history and branches">

[Codex conversations](/plugins/codex-supervision) keep their selected physical source, with stop and steer reaching the active turn and completed forks retaining their outcome. Compaction waits for confirmation, valid retained text spans can be forked, and oversized tool results can be replaced with explicit omission notices when reopening a conversation without changing its full transcript. New sender-attributed messages also preserve the submitted text needed for branching; older mismatched transcripts are not rewritten.

Older adopted chats without a saved source keep their history but need fresh adoption into a new chat. Pinned chats need their original source restored, or the replacement adopted separately. Older nodes require an update and refreshed capability approval to continue, and native terminal or paired-node CLI resume requires a local stdio source. Archiving a parent protects active descendants, while required requests and tool arguments must still fit the history limits.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Preserve Codex history and wait for confirmed completion [#151700](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151700).
- Fix Codex forks after sender-attributed messages [#153552](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153552).
- Recover Codex context from oversized tool outputs [#153854](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153854) — @vincentkoc.

**Limits and compatibility**

- Keep Codex chats and controls attached to their selected source [#151854](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151854).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Inspecting full Codex tool output">

Long available Codex tool responses now remain saved after a reload, with **Show full output**, Copy, and Download providing plain-text inspection and export. The [output guide](/plugins/codex-harness/runtime-behavior#inspecting-tool-output) explains the distinction between execution output and provider responses. Older discarded text cannot be recovered, and redaction, provider truncation, and transport limits still apply, including a requested maximum of two million characters per text field. Neither view proves exactly what Codex later sends to its model.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Preserve long Codex tool responses for inspection and export [#153542](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153542).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Codex sandbox commands and cancellation">

Interactive Codex sandbox commands now receive real terminals, Ctrl-C reaches interactive and noninteractive commands, and exit results identify the requested process. If command execution is allowed but process management is denied, permitted commands can finish in the foreground while background continuation and explicitly excluded shell tools remain unavailable. POSIX installations running Bun need a real Node runtime for terminal helpers. Cleanup retains owned resources until work settles, including canceled readiness probes.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Fix Codex sandbox terminals, cancellation, and readiness cleanup [#151764](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151764).
- Keep permitted Codex sandbox commands without process management [#152600](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152600).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Codex catalog and connection overhead">

Small Codex catalog replies avoid waiting for a decoder worker to start, while large session lists reduce repeated scans and database work. Catalogs refresh incrementally, with full reconciliation at startup and reconnect; quiet edits or removals in older sessions may wait for a successful fifteen-minute safety cycle. Large catalog parsing moves off the Gateway's main thread, and recreated connections suppress unused notification traffic, reducing repeated processing without changing model generation.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Reduce database work while listing Codex sessions [#151979](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151979).
- Reuse eligible Codex session rows during catalog polling [#152712](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152712).
- Reduce Codex app-server framing overhead [#152035](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152035).
- Reduce unused Codex notification traffic [#151998](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151998).
- Refresh Codex session catalogs incrementally [#152257](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152257).
- Parse large Codex catalogs outside the Gateway main thread [#152352](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152352).

**Bug fixes**
