</details>

</Accordion>

</AccordionGroup>

## Automations and Scheduling

Scheduled work is easier to run, follow, and return to, with clearer distinctions between work that is executing, work that has finished, and results still waiting to reach you.

<AccordionGroup>

<Accordion title="Run paused automations without losing their schedule">

You can use **Run now** on a paused automation in the Agents panel without enabling its schedule. A future **Once** job keeps its saved occurrence even with **Delete after run** enabled, including when a manual run waits past that date or OpenClaw restarts. Re-enabling makes the preserved occurrence available again, while genuine schedule edits still take effect and already-deleted jobs are not restored. [Manage automations](/automation/cron-jobs/managing-jobs) explains the controls and run history.

Accepted manual runs can start after the calling tool finishes, and acknowledgement now follows a saved run receipt. If OpenClaw crashes before dispatch, the interrupted receipt remains for diagnosis without automatically replaying the run.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Run paused automations once from the Agents panel [#129310](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/129310).
- Keep paused future one-shot automations after Run now; downgrading to older code can lose this protection [#152126](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152126).
- Let queued manual automations start after tool completion [#153865](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153865) — thanks @vincentkoc.
- Record manual automation runs before acknowledging them; pre-dispatch interruptions remain in database receipts and may not appear in task-backed run history [#153360](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153360) — thanks @obviyus.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Let scheduled work outlive the creating conversation turn">

[Scheduled jobs](/automation/cron-jobs/how-it-works) keep their own execution and result-saving lifetime after the conversation turn that created them ends. Their command launcher remains available after background handoff, and parent conversations stay available while delegated work or its delivery is pending. Canceling a job or withdrawing message permission stops later message actions while preserving records of deliveries already accepted.

Scheduled Gateway tools must run on the Gateway that owns the job. Remove `gatewayUrl` and `gatewayToken` from same-host templates before rerunning them. Cancellation cannot recall effects already submitted to a provider.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Keep scheduled work and delegated results alive after the creating turn [#151919](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151919).
- Keep complete scheduled-job lifetimes independent of creating requests [#152051](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152051).
- Preserve the Gateway launcher for scheduled commands [#152451](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152451).
- Cancel pending callback work while preserving required completion [#151214](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151214).

**Security and trust**

- Stop new scheduled message actions after cancellation or permission withdrawal [10169f04](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/10169f0489e4f31e3af6ae4e3a48252f3c739095) — thanks @shakkernerd.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Create automations from the current conversation">

Remote administrators can create recurring automations in a fresh chat turn under their existing permissions, including jobs attached to the current conversation. WebChat can also create announcements back to that conversation using `announce` or omitted delivery settings. Fresh creation rights do not carry over to management continuations or grant access to the host, providers, or additional MCP tools, and an explicit `delivery.channel=webchat` remains invalid.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Restore scoped automation creation for remote administrators [#151291](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151291) — thanks @jalehman.
- Create current-session announcements from WebChat [#152960](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152960).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Preserve commands and clarify automation settings">

Creating or editing an automation preserves meaningful whitespace in shell commands, and invalid cron stagger values are rejected before they can partially change a job. Keyboard focus stays visible above the editor’s sticky save controls, delayed action feedback names the job you acted on, and timeout errors correctly explain that zero disables that particular timeout. Other execution limits still apply.

If an older save trimmed a command, reapply the original command to repair it. When creating jobs from scripts, omit working-directory flags to use defaults instead of passing empty strings.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Avoid redundant reads for explicit-revision cron scratch writes [#153941](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153941).

**Bug fixes**

- Preserve meaningful whitespace in automation shell commands [#150071](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/150071) — thanks @zhangguiping-xydt, @obviyus.
- Reject invalid cron stagger values before changing jobs [#151740](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151740) — thanks @alix-007, @aniruddhaadak80, @obviyus.
- Keep automation form keyboard focus clear of sticky actions [#152135](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152135).
- Explain the existing zero-timeout option in automation errors [#152814](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152814) — thanks @kriswuli006, @aniruddhaadak80, @obviyus.
- Identify the automation behind delayed action feedback [#150203](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/150203).

**Limits and compatibility**

- Reject blank working-directory arguments when creating automations [#129714](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/129714).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="See whether scheduled work and delivery finished">

Run history now shows **OK · Error** or **OK · Unknown** when execution succeeded but overall completion failed or remains uncertain. These runs still appear under the **OK** execution filter, so the delivery details matter when checking whether a report reached you. Confirmed final delivery can prevent a later tool error from turning a completed report into a false failure, while progress-only messages do not count as final delivery. Repeated failed runs in a persistent conversation also retain their original errors for diagnosis.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Avoid duplicate retained-history reads for deleted automations [#152492](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152492).
- Move read-only Cron inspection into background workers [#152500](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152500).
- Preserve Cron recovery history during stop and restart [#153158](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153158).
- Avoid unrelated session rebuilds after cron events [#151279](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151279) — thanks @jalehman.
- Reduce task-payload copying in cron cleanup [#154174](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/154174).
- Skip redundant schedule checks on idle automation ticks [#150277](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/150277) — thanks @vincentkoc.
- Reduce timer allocations for busy cron streams [#151956](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151956).

**Bug fixes**

- Expose failed or uncertain completion in scheduled-job history [#129396](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/129396).
- Avoid false failures after scheduled reports are delivered [#152165](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152165) — thanks @pash-openai.
- Preserve errors from repeated automation runs in persistent sessions [#150315](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/150315) — thanks @jalehman, @dazlarus.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Keep heartbeat checks responsive and outcomes clear">

Overdue [heartbeat checks](/gateway/heartbeat) no longer hold up scheduler startup or reload, and their results can wait for busy storage without blocking unrelated work. A successful or skipped agent no longer hides another agent’s failure in a combined heartbeat result. Background command results can also reach their idle originating conversation while unrelated sessions are busy, when automatic wake is enabled. Activity in the destination conversation or pending recovery can still defer delivery, and subagent sessions remain excluded from automatic wake.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Move heartbeat outcome queries into the agent database worker [#147463](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147463).
- Avoid unrelated session scans during heartbeat admission [#151400](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151400) — thanks @vincentkoc.
- Reduce heartbeat membership work in large agent setups [#151867](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151867) — thanks @vincentkoc.

**Bug fixes**

- Prevent heartbeat catch-up from blocking cron startup; a handler-unavailable skipped result calls for checking startup and sidecar errors [#151319](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151319).
- Avoid blocked heartbeat writes behind busy SQLite workers [#152763](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152763) — thanks @fuller-stack-dev, @smfworks.
- Preserve individual agent failures in heartbeat broadcast results [#129648](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/129648).
- Show a copyable heartbeat command to keep results internal while leaving checks scheduled [#153448](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153448) — thanks @alix-007, @obviyus, @tridefender.
- Deliver background command results despite unrelated busy sessions [#152287](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152287).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Resume parents and finish delegated results">

Parents waiting on delegated work can resume across provider retries, and old pending result delivery no longer blocks new launches within the existing active-run limits. Eligible native children paused with `sessions_yield` can continue under their original task when their controlling parent sends an ordinary message, allowing the task and its chat activity to finish together. Explicit follow-ups remain separate work, and interrupted children after a restart report back for [parent-directed continuation](/tools/subagents/operations).

Queued children are recorded before startup, and native follow-ups before acceptance, while completion notices remain owed until the parent turn finishes. If follow-up acceptance is still pending, inspect the returned run before retrying. Results remain accessible through conversation compaction and cleanup, with existing access checks preserved; pending or failed saves remain visible rather than being treated as delivered results.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Reduce subagent capability-check work for large histories [#152263](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152263).

**Bug fixes**

- Resume waiting parent tasks across provider retries [#151879](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151879) — thanks @jalehman.
- Preserve sub-agent cleanup during overlapping result delivery [#151907](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151907).
- Unblock subagent launches after confirmed late result delivery [#151949](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151949) — thanks @vacinc.
- Keep undelivered results from blocking new delegated work [#152537](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152537) — thanks @damiansmazurek.
- Acknowledge queued subagent records before launch [#151303](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151303).
- Track child follow-ups before acknowledging acceptance [#153358](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153358).
- Preserve permitted session reads across background handoffs and resumes [#141865](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/141865) — thanks @takhoffman, @brokemac79, @vacinc.
- Complete eligible native paused child tasks after ordinary parent continuations; automatic continuation requires recorded task and store ownership, while older records retain explicit controls [#153047](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153047).
- Keep agent results accessible through session changes [#151935](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151935).
- Avoid false drain failures after private subagent timeouts [#153461](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153461).
- Retain subagent completion notifications while the parent is still running [#152761](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152761) — thanks @pollybot13, @shakkernerd, @bsniznd, @borclaw.
- Show direct-announcement failures after successful fallback delivery [#125130](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/125130) — thanks @vincentkoc, @alix-007, @shakkernerd, @alexey-pelykh.
- Deliver current subagent results past old pending cleanup [#153157](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153157).
- Suppress stale tool-error replies during successful agent pauses [#153779](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153779) — thanks @vacinc.
- Isolate pending system notifications by agent and conversation, with bounded transient queues; ambiguous multi-agent SDK calls must supply a resolved `agentId` or qualified session key [#151652](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151652).
- Settle cancelled subagent notifications and quiet repeated backlog warnings [#151569](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151569) — thanks @laurenceputra.
- Suppress obsolete subagent timing warnings [#151275](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151275) — thanks @jalehman.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Inspect live tasks and complete command details">

[Task details](/automation/tasks) show complete command inputs with sensitive values redacted, and distinguish live **Running**, **Queued**, waiting, and **Finished** execution from a saved task record. Quiet background commands can still show as running, while delayed activity no longer makes finished work look active. **Finished** describes execution, so success and pending result delivery remain separate facts, and unavailable activity does not by itself mean failure.

Failure notices offer concise previews with full diagnostics available through `tasks show`, and unreadable task metadata produces an explicit error instead of a falsely empty list. Previously truncated command inputs cannot be reconstructed, and command output remains bounded.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Reduce repeated work in background-job status reads [#150299](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/150299) — thanks @vincentkoc.
- Avoid execution lookups for queued and finished task summaries [#151585](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151585) — thanks @vincentkoc.
- Avoid task-store coordination for transient streaming events [#152088](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152088).
- Skip excluded task copies in filtered JSON lists [#152440](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152440).
- Reduce copying when inspecting a session's latest task [#152731](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152731).
- Copy only matching records when listing an agent's tasks [#150297](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/150297) — thanks @vincentkoc.

**Bug fixes**

- Distinguish live subagents from retained task records [#152514](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152514) — thanks @obviyus, @samiralibabic, @dankarization.
- Show live CLI tasks as Running or Queued [#152329](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152329).
- Show complete commands and live background-process status [#152333](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152333).
- Show completed tasks correctly after delayed activity [#152367](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152367).
- Keep finished task execution from briefly appearing unknown [#152951](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152951).
- Bound background-task failure previews without losing diagnostics [#151120](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151120) — thanks @bartok9, @obviyus, @jackten.

- Keep media task context available during unrelated task updates [#153612](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153612).

- Return completed tasks despite overlapping metadata updates [#153371](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153371).
- Avoid unnecessary pauses when task lists retry [#153328](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153328).
- Avoid full-history scans for individual task reads [#153394](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153394).
- Keep task-status reads from blocking other Gateway requests [#149738](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/149738).
- Keep task artifact requests from blocking the Gateway [#149852](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/149852).
- Keep task run lookups consistent after updates [#153397](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153397).
- Keep task lists readable during completion updates [#153939](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153939).
- Respect refreshed task retention during cleanup [#151578](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151578).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Keep task tracking responsive as work changes">

Task tracking, maintenance, and flow reports do less repeated storage work while preserving newer progress, deletions, delivery details, and late results. Maintenance can continue around unsettled tasks, and restored or retried workflows show their committed status even when a background worker’s reply is delayed. Individual requests can still wait for storage, and queued events become durable only when saved.

Historical task-identifier repair belongs to [Doctor](/gateway/doctor/state-and-sessions) before startup. The normal `openclaw update` path already runs it; after directly replacing a binary or container image, or using an older writer, run `openclaw doctor --fix` against the same state before starting the new Gateway. Ambiguous identifiers produce a warning and leave the repair unchanged.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Find background tasks by run or child session without scanning history [#153467](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153467).

- Batch task-list refreshes during concurrent updates [#153737](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153737).
- Avoid redundant task-state reads for accepted agent events [#153786](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153786).
- Move execution-tracking writes off the task startup thread [#153649](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153649).
- Limit pending task-flow refreshes to selected records [#153806](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153806).
- Avoid duplicate task reads in flow reports [#152857](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152857).
- Move active task completion persistence off the Gateway thread [#151685](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151685).

- Keep task maintenance responsive while updates settle [#153634](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153634).
- Keep delayed workflow synchronization current through cleanup [#151309](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151309).
- Move initial task persistence off the Gateway thread [#151634](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151634).
- Preserve current task metadata during lifecycle callbacks [#151454](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151454).
- Keep concurrent managed-task updates current and correctly ordered [#151590](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151590).
- Keep restored task-flow status current [#153852](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153852).

</details>

</Accordion>

</AccordionGroup>

## Browser and Computer Use

OpenClaw can keep apps beside your conversation, show more kinds of cloud desktops, and make the browser work easier to inspect as it happens.

<AccordionGroup>

<Accordion title="Open Crabbox apps beside the conversation">

Ask the agent to open an app in Crabbox and show it beside your chat, then keep working with the same machine on later turns. [Attached apps](/gateway/cloud-workers/desktop#ask-the-agent-to-open-an-app) need a configured Crabbox profile and a persistent conversation outside a sandbox. Native Linux apps also need a desktop-enabled profile and the Cloud Worker Desktop lab, while web apps open in a Portal. Files must be copied or prepared on that machine explicitly. Closing the panel leaves apps running, so save the outputs you need and stop the Crabbox when finished.

[Remote Portals](/gateway/portals#remote-access) now use their published HTTPS addresses. Managed Tailscale Portals stay private to your tailnet even when the Gateway uses Funnel, and their separate ports need to be allowed. An optional wildcard proxy needs your own DNS, TLS, access controls, and a Gateway restart. Forwarding the Gateway alone does not expose the apps. Upgrade OpenClaw and its bundled providers together, and stop attachments before downgrading.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Open Linux and web apps beside the current conversation and reuse their attached Crabbox across turns. [#152094](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152094)

**Bug fixes**

- Open remote Portal apps through their published HTTPS routes, preserving app paths, queries, and owned route cleanup. [#150279](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/150279) Thanks @jalehman.

**Limits and compatibility**

- Manual desktop takeover pauses agent input. Handing control back requires a fresh screenshot before input resumes, and a Portal may need reopening after a Gateway restart even when its app survives.
- Browser framing and cookie rules can still prevent an app from embedding. Portal and remote-desktop browser cookies remain separate.
- Attached machines are disposable and follow their configured lifetime. Closing a view does not release the machine or stop its background apps.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="View native macOS and Windows cloud desktops">

[Cloud desktops](/gateway/cloud-workers/desktop#native-desktops) now support native macOS and Windows profiles, with Fit and Actual viewing sizes. Enable the Cloud Worker Desktop lab and a desktop-enabled profile first. Native machines start cold and do not have the Linux Browser and Terminal launchers or remote display resizing. WSL2 desktops remain unsupported, and Mac provisioning requires existing EC2 Mac Dedicated Host capacity. Live Mac desktop validation remains incomplete.

Before downgrading to a build without native desktop support, stop and release every native Mac or Windows environment and conversation attachment, and wait for confirmed teardown. Closing the viewer or suspending a worker is insufficient. Linux cloud sessions also regain resized screenshots and browser reopening; an already affected browser needs a restart or a newly provisioned worker.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Provision and view native macOS and Windows cloud desktops with platform-specific authentication. [#152273](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152273)

**Bug fixes**

- Deliver resized screenshots in cloud computer sessions with the coordinate mapping needed for interaction. [#151573](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151573)
- Reopen Linux cloud browsers after the first launch without an inherited startup-lock timeout. [#153169](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153169)

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Control paired desktops and enable host access">

Persistent paired Mac, Windows, and Linux computers now offer [desktop sharing](/gateway/config-browser-ui-desktop#paired-node-desktops) by default, with native Mac and Tauri switches and connection status. Pairing approval and an authenticated local Screen Sharing or VNC service are still required, and the switch does not turn on that operating-system service. Explicit disables and Gateway denials remain respected. Some existing computers need approval again in Devices after updating. Tauri requires a local OpenClaw CLI, and closing it to the tray leaves sharing active.

Access to the computer running the Gateway remains a separate opt-in. An administrator can enable [Host Desktop from Systems](/gateway/config-browser-ui-desktop#desktop), with setup guidance for missing services and macOS permissions. Labs changes, including Systems enablement, apply without restarting the Gateway when automatic configuration reload is enabled. Turning access off closes viewers but leaves system screen-sharing services and cloud workers running. Code Mode and Tool Search changes apply to future runs, and disabling Custom plugin UI requires a browser-tab reload to clear code that already ran.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Enable paired desktop sharing by default with native controls, Tauri connection status, and reapproval for affected older grants. [#153359](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153359)
- Offer administrator host-desktop setup in Systems with guidance for macOS Screen Sharing and Remote Management permissions. [#154008](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/154008)
- Apply Labs and desktop settings on existing connections without a Gateway restart when automatic reload is enabled. [#153975](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153975)

**Bug fixes**

- Save Host Desktop disablement from Labs while preserving its other settings. [#147741](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147741) Thanks @TriDefender and @obviyus.

**Limits and compatibility**

- Desktop sharing, agent Computer Control, and Keep Awake remain separate controls. macOS Remote Management accounts need Observe and Control rights.
- Turning Cloud Worker Desktop back on restores access to already capable workers. It does not provision a desktop or replace a worker that lacks one.
- Use the app switch, the node's explicit disabled setting, or a Gateway deny rule to disable paired sharing. Removing a Gateway allow entry is no longer the ongoing disable mechanism after reapproval.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Start computer control and diagnose unavailable access">

Ordinary [Codex Computer Use](/plugins/codex-computer-use) turns using the default non-strict readiness setting no longer wait for optional live desktop probes before starting. Installation, available tools, and permissions still matter, and a later desktop call can still fail; strict readiness and explicit status or install commands keep their live checks. Computer-control helpers also find a suitable Node runtime when a Bun shim shadows it, though Bun users still need Node installed. Browser refusals give recovery guidance that matches the actual policy or plugin problem, and Linux X11 apps that previously missed brief key taps can detect them.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Skip optional live desktop probes during non-strict Codex startup while retaining installation and tool checks. [#151666](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151666) Thanks @jalehman.
- Start desktop computer-control helpers with a suitable Node runtime despite a shadowing Bun shim. [#151933](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151933) Thanks @wangmiao0668000666 and @altaywtf.
- Explain browser-control refusals with recovery steps for the current policy or plugin cause. [#154091](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/154091) Thanks @mmkerrigan.
- Make computer-control key taps detectable by Linux X11 apps that poll keyboard state. [#153830](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153830)

**Limits and compatibility**

- The key-tap fix does not add sustained keyboard holds or establish the same input improvement on macOS and Windows.
- Better refusal messages do not repair the separate plugin-activation mismatch or allowlist-persistence issue, and enabling access remains explicit.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Keep browser actions tied to the captured controls">

Browser actions and selected-area screenshots stay tied to the controls that were captured, including pages with repeated labels or frames. If a captured control disappears, the action fails instead of choosing another matching control. Browser input keeps its click and key order, healthy tabs avoid waits on stalled siblings, and form failures explain problems such as covered or disabled controls. Exact storage keys, including surrounding spaces, are preserved.

When using an [existing Chrome session](/tools/browser/existing-session), a snapshot refresh retires the previous references even if it fails or runs inside a condition wait. Take a fresh snapshot before another action that uses those references. Pages with ambiguous element IDs across frames reject that capture; use a managed profile or a screenshot without references. Native coordinate input supports left and double clicks; right or middle clicks and nonzero click delays require a managed profile. A missing target is never silently replaced with another tab.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Preserve exact browser snapshot and screenshot targets, allow action deadlines to finish, and return useful input errors. Also preserve dialog and option values, upload timeouts, pending cookie changes, and bridge connection details. [#153420](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153420)
- Keep actions tied to captured controls, prevent stale captures replacing newer references, clean up frame labels, preserve sibling connections, and recheck current node access without changing the selected profile. [#153597](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153597)
- Preserve the order of browser-extension mouse and keyboard events within each tab. [#151650](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151650)
- Recover browser inspection without sequential delays from stalled sibling tabs while preserving newer connections and blocked-tab rules. [#153283](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153283)
- Preserve spaces in quoted browser-storage keys. [#151300](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151300) Thanks @zhangguiping-xydt and @obviyus.

**Limits and compatibility**

- Selected-area snapshot formatting and reference numbers can change. Custom launchers need `click_at` and its required feature flag for coordinate clicks.
- Blank storage keys remain invalid for writes. Previously overwritten values are not recovered.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="See browser pages and retained computer screenshots in Chat">

Browser cards can show public page titles, favicons, and social images when a live screenshot is unavailable, making closed or historical pages easier to recognize. Repeated opens of the same page share a card within an activity group, while actual tabs and distinct routes stay separate. New Browser side panels use more available space without overriding your saved width. Retained computer screenshots also appear during runs and after reopening chat, with image-viewer, copy, and download controls. Discarded pixels cannot be recovered, and some saved-image layouts can still show duplicate tiles.

Public metadata fetching follows the existing default-on `automaticallyFetchFavicons` setting. Turning it off disables those previews while keeping live screenshots. These requests carry no browser credentials, but destination sites and image hosts can see the requested URLs and the addresses making the requests. Computer screenshots shown as observations are not automatically sent as outgoing attachments.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Add public favicons, page titles, and social images to browser cards without live screenshots. [#152324](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152324)
- Give new Browser side panels more available width while preserving saved widths and manual resizing. [#152575](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152575)
- Move browser-dashboard discovery off the Gateway thread and finish accepted events during shutdown. [#151952](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151952)

**Bug fixes**

- Consolidate repeated previews of the same browser page within an activity group while keeping tool history expandable. [#152281](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152281)
- Display retained computer screenshots inline during active runs and after reopening chat. [#153249](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153249)
- Release obsolete browser activity after its final tab owner retires, preserving activity still owned by another tab or retained dashboard. [#127096](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/127096)

**Limits and compatibility**

- Public page metadata may be unavailable. Disabling automatic favicon fetching also hides cached metadata previews.
- Recovered images stored only as artifacts can still appear twice when matching saved media uses an inline layout.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Recover retained cloud sessions">

Restarting with several retained cloud sessions can finish recovery sooner because independent provider checks run together within a fixed limit. A submission that has not yet started can also recover after its worker reconnects, update the worker on the same machine when needed, and retry admission once. This requires a compatible worker installer and a successful reconnect. Work already handed to a worker, including interrupted tool calls, is never replayed by that retry. [Cleanup guidance](/gateway/cloud-workers/session-lifecycle) now reports the latest cleanup failure, so an old session error does not hide what still needs attention.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Reduce restart waits for multiple retained cloud sessions through bounded concurrent provider checks. [#154171](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/154171)
- Show the current conversation-worker cleanup error with bounded, redacted diagnostics. [#153869](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153869) Thanks @vincentkoc and @skorfmann.
- Resume unstarted cloud submissions after worker reconnection and a required in-place worker update. [#154154](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/154154)

**Limits and compatibility**

- Checks that have already started must finish before recovery proceeds, and ownership errors still prevent adopting a session. Recovery has no fixed speedup.
- A cleanup warning does not mean the provider problem is repaired or the machine is deleted. Check the current state and follow its recovery guidance.

</details>

</Accordion>

</AccordionGroup>

## Plugins and Integrations

Connected tools bring more of the work into OpenClaw, with clearer setup and access to the workspace where your files live. Plugin installation, reloads, and storage also receive fixes for interruptions that could stop otherwise usable tools.

<AccordionGroup>

<Accordion title="Meeting notes during live capture">

Captured voice and [Google Meet, Microsoft Teams, and Zoom meetings](/plugins/meeting-plugins) can save updated notes about every five minutes when new speech arrives, then produce final notes when capture ends. The Meetings page follows saved speech automatically and separates Summary from Transcript, with complete transcript access and missing-note generation for users who have write access. Updates can lag the conversation while speech is transcribed, and model-generated notes use your configured model and its normal usage charges.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Follow meeting notes and saved speech during live capture [#151957](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151957).
- Save meeting captions off-thread and drain accepted speech before final notes [#151760](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151760).
- Move stored meeting transcript lookups off the Gateway main thread [135f934d](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/135f934db69da6d08e2e77443161ea65e9528af2).
- Move meeting-note database work off the caller thread [#154070](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/154070).
- Move transcript list and show queries off the caller thread; retry relevant older-date reads if the timezone changes during the read [#154178](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/154178).

**Bug fixes**

- Load complete meeting transcripts and generate missing notes with write access, preserving existing notes and bounded summary input [#152463](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152463).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Experimental FaceTime Audio">

The optional [FaceTime plugin](/plugins/facetime) adds one-to-one audio conversations with your configured agent, including spoken answers to requests that need its tools or memory. It is experimental, disabled by default, and needs a dedicated Apple Silicon Mac running macOS 14.4 or later, OpenClaw 2026.9.6, matching signed native helpers, configured audio devices, and realtime-provider credentials. Matching plugin and native distribution availability remains unconfirmed.

Setup also requires participant consent, Developer Tools access, and manually reduced macOS SIP debugging protections. Every allowed handle has owner authority, only one managed call can run at a time, and agent-initiated calls require one-shot approval. Follow the setup guide before enabling it.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Add an experimental FaceTime Audio bridge for authorized agent owners [#119291](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/119291) — thanks @omarshahine, @romneyda.

**Limits and compatibility**

- Align FaceTime compatibility and plugin versions with 2026.9.6 [0764ed75](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/0764ed759a0bedd64c3aac6768aacf6fe039a23d) — thanks @vincentkoc.

**Documentation**

- Clarify experimental FaceTime setup and troubleshooting [#153450](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153450) — thanks @omarshahine.

**Bug fixes**

- Repair FaceTime call tracking and realtime voice routing [#154225](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/154225).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="MCP connector sign-in from Settings">

Administrators can now sign in to eligible [MCP connectors](/tools/mcp) directly from Settings, approve access in the browser, and return to an Authentication saved result. This works for enabled HTTP OAuth connectors using shared native credentials when you open the Gateway through its own loopback or published Tailscale address. Other account modes keep their existing sign-in paths, and saving credentials still leaves connector reachability and tool access to be checked.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Sign in to MCP connectors from Settings [#151023](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151023) — thanks @obviyus.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="MCP tool connections and plugin replacement">

New CLI-backend turns keep access to their permitted OpenClaw tools after a plugin replacement, without needing a Gateway restart. Long-running MCP calls also keep their local connection alive while awaiting a result, and Bun cleanup handles a supervisor that outlives its command. Each new turn still needs an active grant, and connection keepalives neither extend deadlines nor indicate progress.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Keep CLI bridge tools available after plugin replacement [#153673](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153673) — thanks @zhangguiping-xydt, @obviyus, @kiranvk-2011.
- Keep local MCP connections alive during long tool waits [#153879](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153879) — thanks @vacinc.
- Close Bun MCP cleanup control channels correctly [#153459](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153459).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="ACP model choices and task tracking">

ACP-connected coding agents resolve model choices against the currently connected harness, including after reconnecting, and newly spawned child work appears once in the task list. Explicit model choices fail if they are unknown or ambiguous, so an unsupported selection cannot silently become another model. Failed ACPX starts also release unused session state while preserving queued retries.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Resolve ACP model choices against the current connected harness [#152850](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152850) — thanks @saariuslystoned, @shakkernerd, @jhgaylor.
- Show each newly spawned ACP task once [#153071](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153071) — thanks @obviyus, @saariuslystoned, @markgenhealth.
- Release unused ACPX state after failed session starts [a3f9d754](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/a3f9d754ac7284d70498dc66f0539e47e7088eae).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="A2A answers after model fallback">

When an A2A task switches models, its completed result now contains the fallback model’s actual answer. The task no longer finishes early with only the notice that a different model will be used.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Return the actual A2A answer after model fallback [#152168](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152168) — thanks @chelsealong, @obviyus, @edgarstool.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="On-demand Tool Search by default">

Embedded and Copilot agents now use [Tool Search](/tools/tool-search) when the setting is omitted, discovering relevant tools as needed instead of receiving every eligible tool’s full description upfront. You can turn it off in Labs or set `tools.toolSearch` to `false` to restore direct tool descriptions. Extra discovery turns can cost more, particularly with a small tool catalog.

Existing explicit settings keep their meaning. `true` selects the legacy Node code bridge, an empty object leaves Tool Search disabled, and Code Mode takes precedence. Codex’s native tool discovery is unchanged.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Enable structured Tool Search by default for embedded and Copilot runs [#154068](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/154068).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Code Mode moves to JavaScript">

[Code Mode](/tools/code-mode/configuration) now executes plain JavaScript only, while its tool discovery still provides typed documentation. This is a breaking change for existing TypeScript cells. Rewrite those cells without TypeScript-only syntax and remove both `language` and `typecheck` arguments, even when their values are `"javascript"` or `false`. Eligible startup migrations remove the old `tools.codeMode.languages` setting; otherwise run `openclaw doctor --fix`. Configuration migration does not rewrite your code.

Tool inputs and results are checked at runtime, so a later error can occur after an earlier action has already completed. Structured results also reject BigInts, cycles, and failing serializers with catchable errors; convert those values explicitly to JSON-compatible data and keep your configured output limits in mind.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Limits and compatibility**

- Move Code Mode execution to JavaScript with migration guidance [#154001](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/154001).

**Improvements**

- Reduce Code Mode JavaScript preparation overhead [#154209](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/154209).

**Bug fixes**

- Preserve Code Mode completion work and structured results, including redacted configuration results within the 12,000-character limit [#151372](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151372).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Reading GitHub pull requests and documents">

The [GitHub reader](/plugins/github) opens public issues, pull requests, commits, discussion, and expandable diffs in tabs beside chat. It also shows supported screenshots, coauthor portraits, and a snapshot of pull-request checks, with clearer guidance when access, timeouts, or rate limits prevent loading. Full documents now use the same configured GitHub identity as previews, avoiding unnecessary anonymous-quota failures while remaining public-only and read-only. Private content and edits stay on GitHub.

Attachment images still load anonymously without cookies or credentials, with PNG, JPEG, GIF, and WebP support up to 2 MiB. Check results are bounded snapshots, not a merge-readiness verdict. If you use a restrictive plugin allowlist, add and enable `github` while preserving its other entries to use the reader and previews. Native credentials come from the operating-system account running the Gateway.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Use configured GitHub credentials for full public documents [#153981](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153981).
- Display public GitHub attachments and hide reader comment metadata [#153004](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153004).
- Explain GitHub preview and status failures [#153181](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153181).
- Restore compact PR previews and coauthor portraits [#153193](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153193).
- Preserve complete characters in shortened GitHub text [#153344](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153344) — thanks @xydt-juyaohui, @obviyus.
- Resolve named-project PR links to the correct repository [#151057](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151057) — thanks @brokemac79, @vyctorbrzezowski.
- Clarify GitHub authorization progress and completion [#150669](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/150669) — thanks @romneyda.
- Prevent malformed coauthor trailers from stalling GitHub previews [#152115](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152115).

**Improvements**

- Inspect head-commit PR checks and navigate reader sections, with up to 100 displayed check items and a 30-second snapshot cache [#153274](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153274) — thanks Vyctor (reporter).
- Read public GitHub discussions and diffs beside chat in up to ten memory-only tabs, with bounded comment and file collections [#148464](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/148464).

**Documentation**

- Explain which computer account supplies native GitHub credentials [#153754](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153754) — thanks @vincentkoc.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Publishing session work to GitHub">

Publishing a session’s work accepts valid recreated branches even when their reflog has expired, while retaining ancestry checks and non-force push protections. Publication errors now distinguish another operation holding the lock from unavailable storage or cancellation, making the next step clearer. A storage failure can be retried with the original request or checkpoint; cancellation before the operation acquires its lock is not automatically retryable. Canceled Doctor inspections are reported as not performed, rather than as a health result.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Publish valid recreated branches without relying on reflogs [#152334](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152334).
- Improve checkpoint publication admission during database contention, with the final outcome policy supplied by the following repair [#152387](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152387).
- Distinguish held publication locks, retryable storage failures, and pre-grant cancellation; replace the earlier separate retry policy and label canceled Doctor inspections as not performed [#152437](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152437).
- Keep GitHub publication options responsive with large pending histories [#152851](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152851).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Authorized tools after delegated work">

An agent that explicitly waits for delegated work can resume using supported owner-only plugin tools without requiring another message from you. Eligible sessions also retain their GitHub tools across supported harness continuations, with the same current permissions and publication approvals. Plugin authors must opt in to the [owner-authorized continuation contract](/plugins/tool-plugins) and check that authority immediately before an action; unrelated sessions and scheduled jobs gain no owner privileges.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Preserve opted-in owner-only plugin access after a parent resumes [#150295](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/150295) — thanks @vacinc.
- Retain eligible GitHub tools after subagent continuations; remove the deprecated, ignored githubPublicationAvailable SDK option [#151651](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151651).
- Preserve host-selected GitHub tools and active plugin work across harnesses while retaining tool profiles and live authorization [#152073](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152073) — thanks @vacinc.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Choosing cloud sessions from an agent">

Agents can discover configured cloud profiles and request an advertised operating system and machine size when starting a separate visible worktree session. The [cloud-session tool path](/tools/subagents/tool-reference) requires a live hosted Gateway, a supported provider, and the existing placement permissions. Failed or uncertain starts retain the session for inspection without silently running locally, so inspect that state before retrying. Work already assigned remotely also avoids provisioning an unrelated local Docker sandbox.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Let agents choose configured cloud profiles, operating systems and machine sizes [#148034](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/148034) — thanks @vincentkoc, @vyctorbrzezowski.

**Bug fixes**

- Start remote turns without requiring a local container [#153037](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153037).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Worker startup diagnostics and shutdown">

Worker launch failures now explain rejected commands and unconfirmed cancellation more clearly. Stopping a node environment attempts the remaining cleanup even if one part fails, and replacement work waits while cleanup is unresolved. This can leave capacity occupied until the old work is confirmed stopped, rather than starting another worker over it.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Explain rejected worker launches and cancellation failures [#151985](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151985).
- Complete independent node cleanup attempts despite individual failures [#152896](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152896).

**Improvements**

- Move node-host configuration reads off the caller [#151411](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151411).
- Avoid repeated device scans after unrelated state writes [#153567](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153567).
- Reduce CPU work when choosing node targets [#151687](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151687) — thanks @vincentkoc.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Returning cloud workspace changes">

Cloud workspaces can return larger rebases within increased transfer limits, and transfers work on POSIX hosts whose defaults create group-writable directories. The permission repair applies only to OpenClaw-owned directories, preserving transferred file modes and the host’s defaults. Large returns remain bounded at 500,000 before-and-after records and 768 MiB of changed content or patch data, with the existing 64 MiB per-file and 256 MiB compressed rollback limits.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Restore workspace transfers on group-writable hosts [#152717](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152717).
- Return cloud workspaces after large rebases [#152566](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152566).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Agent documents on paired-node workspaces">

Supported host integrations can point the existing agent document editor and instruction readers at the workspace on a paired node, without maintaining a local copy. This requires an explicit workspace mapping, a compatible connected node, and separate file grants, with a 16 MiB document limit. If that workspace becomes unavailable, reads fail instead of using stale local files. Owner writes remain limited to the root `AGENTS`, `SOUL`, `IDENTITY`, and `USER` documents, and an already-dispatched write cannot be undone by disconnecting.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Use host-owned agent documents and bootstrap context [#150584](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/150584) — thanks @kimiyu-186.
- Read and edit agent documents on paired nodes [#150734](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/150734) — thanks @kimiyu-186.
- Expose shared workspace document rules and unavailable errors [#152635](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152635) — thanks @kimiyu-186.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Files, Memory and Skills on remote workspaces">

Configured remote-workspace integrations can receive uploaded attachments before a turn starts and return generated files with replies. Paired-node adapters also connect Memory files and workspace Skill discovery, reading, watching, and dependency installation to that workspace. These paths require host support, explicit configuration, current command and file grants, and matching Gateway and node versions. Remote Skill source installation, updates, removal, and ClawHub lifecycle operations remain outside this support.

The default transfer limit is 50 MiB per file, and uploads buffer the admitted file in memory. A failed enabled upload stops dispatch, while denied or missing output files do not fall back to stale local copies. Remove saved `file.create` grants before downgrading to an older version.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Add service-owned binary file transfers through paired nodes [#152640](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152640) — thanks @kimiyu-186.
- Deliver uploaded attachments to enabled remote workspaces [#152652](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152652) — thanks @kimiyu-186.
- Use paired-node workspace attachments, Memory and Skills [#154087](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/154087) — thanks @kimiyu-186.
- Reduce repeated encoding in general directory listing and fetch summaries while retaining filenames and the 8,192-byte output limit [#151987](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151987) — thanks @vincentkoc.

**Bug fixes**

- Send files generated in remote workspaces with replies [#152633](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152633) — thanks @kimiyu-186.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Fleet status and registry reads">

Fleet status shows whether a cell is recorded as using Docker or Podman, and ordinary registry reads see newly committed cell information. The recorded engine helps identify the configuration; it does not confirm that the engine is currently running.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Run Fleet registry operations asynchronously with current committed reads [a7d62b62](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/a7d62b621ab3ce462a349f644ec4bac2f5be8002).
- Show the recorded Docker or Podman engine in Fleet status [#144691](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144691) — thanks @alix-007, @shakkernerd.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Finding, installing and repairing plugins">

Chat plugin cards update to Installed after installation, and agent guidance favors tools you already have before suggesting more from ClawHub. Plugin archives avoid development-only dependency failures, while incomplete npm installations name missing required dependencies and can be repaired by Doctor when eligible. Repairs deferred during an update may require another Doctor run afterward. Installation fixes also cover stale paths after moving OpenClaw, equivalent Windows directory names, and native dependency asset paths.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Prefer current bundled plugins over stale installation paths [#144843](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144843) — thanks @romneyda, @fuller-stack-dev.
- Install plugin archives without development-only dependency failures [#143350](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/143350) — thanks @giodl73-repo, @fuller-stack-dev.
- Reject incomplete npm plugins and repair missing required dependencies [c6bb12b0](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/c6bb12b061bc7980b9992b52f62d572af5a455e1) — thanks @nxmxbbd, @fuller-stack-dev.
- Preserve native dependency asset paths in staged plugins [#153546](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153546) — thanks @richsomeday222, @maxwell-code07.
- Recognize equivalent Windows plugin directory aliases [#151245](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151245) — thanks @romneyda.
- Refresh chat plugin cards after installation [#152017](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152017) — thanks @hannesrudolph.
- Reduce unnecessary ClawHub installation suggestions [#153913](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153913).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Plugin loading and failed setup">

Plugin loading follows the current selected copy, profile, and configuration, including valid structured secret references. A failed setup keeps its original error without repeatedly initializing the same plugin, and repeated failures no longer bury distinct diagnostics in duplicates or remove healthy sibling tools. Packaged installations also skip unnecessary source parsing during plugin startup.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Skip unnecessary plugin parsing during packaged startup [#153694](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153694) — thanks @vincentkoc.
- Reduce redundant configuration work when toggling plugins [#152794](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152794).

**Bug fixes**

- Keep plugin validation and full loading separate [#151927](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151927).
- Use current profile and Gateway state when loading plugins [#152340](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152340).
- Use current plugin selection for extraction and web setup [#152661](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152661).
- Validate authored plugin secret references alongside prepared values [#152236](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152236) — thanks @jalehman.
- Clean failed plugin setup without repeated initialization [#152026](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152026).
- Stop retaining duplicate plugin tool diagnostics [#151897](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151897).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Plugin reloads during active work">

A busy [plugin reload](/plugins/architecture) can now refuse before stopping the serving runtime, keeping chat available while an active turn or its cleanup still needs that plugin. Active search and fetch calls retain their selected plugin files until the work actually finishes. Finish the work and retry a refused replacement; it is not queued automatically.

Reloads already waiting for active calls can restore the previous runtime when the replacement cannot finish, including recovery after delayed cleanup succeeds within the existing 60-second window. A retirement timeout does not mean plugin files have been deleted, and cleanup that never finishes can still need operator attention.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Prevent plugin reload stalls after alternate-provider turns [#151588](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151588) — thanks @jalehman.
- Keep cached plugins available during model-runtime refresh [#151692](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151692).
- Keep the Gateway serving when a busy plugin reload is refused [#151738](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151738) — thanks @jalehman.
- Retain plugin files through active calls and shutdown cleanup [#153662](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153662) — thanks @sagebhardt, @novalux12, @gorkem2020, @cjn119-ui, @zanechen76.
- Recover channels after delayed plugin cleanup [#151216](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151216) — thanks @vincentkoc.
- Roll back plugin reloads blocked by active calls [#151273](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151273).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Plugin storage capacity and persistence">

Growing plugin caches no longer consume a shared row quota that can block replies, execution records, or background jobs. Individual namespace, expiry, value, and blob limits still apply, but total storage can grow. Keep a verified pre-update backup if you may downgrade, because older versions restore the aggregate quota. Do not delete durable ownership records to make the data fit that older limit.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Move plugin artifact storage work off the Gateway calling thread [#151175](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151175).

**Bug fixes**

- Fix plugin state writes after database identity reuse [#154176](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/154176).
- Prevent growing plugin caches from blocking replies and jobs [#152363](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152363).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Abandoned plugin catalog files">

Managed model-catalog temporary files can now be reclaimed after a crash while live workers retain the files they need. Doctor also identifies older unmanaged directories and provides a manual cleanup command. Stop every Gateway, CLI process, and container sharing that state directory before removing those legacy files; `doctor --fix` deliberately leaves them intact, and reported file sizes can overcount hardlinks.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Clean abandoned model-catalog files and identify legacy storage [#153468](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153468) — thanks @zanechen76, @navras98, @sagebhardt.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Plugin execution callbacks and cleanup">

Plugin-requested answer revisions wait for session history to become available, and required execution-start callbacks finish before the run continues. For plugin authors, a returned startup promise now delays startup and can fail it, so do not return unrelated background work. Worker-pool closure also waits for delayed preparation and cleanup and can report their errors. Cancel preparation waits before closing their own pool, since factories that never settle can keep closure waiting.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Limits and compatibility**

- Wait for execution-start callbacks before continuing [#150396](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/150396).
- Preserve deprecated channel-message exports and their existing deadline; use openclaw/plugin-sdk/channel-outbound for new helpers [#151816](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151816).

**Bug fixes**

- Retain worker inputs through canceled preparation and retirement [#152544](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152544).
- Wait for session history before plugin-requested answer revisions [#140743](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/140743) — thanks @petroff, @obviyus, @rmyers64.

**Improvements**

- Add captureProviderApiKey and persistProviderApiKey helpers while preserving source inputs and profile credentials [#151973](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151973).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Local speech command arguments">

Local speech commands preserve intentionally empty quoted arguments, preventing later options from shifting into the wrong position. Ordinary replies also skip unnecessary Markdown parsing when OpenClaw checks whether they should be spoken, without changing speech policy or synthesis limits.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Preserve empty quoted local speech arguments [#151322](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151322) — thanks @leon-sk668, @obviyus.

**Improvements**

- Skip needless parsing when checking ordinary text for speech [#152728](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152728).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Background media completion">

Background image, video, and music generation can complete independently of the request that started it, avoiding a wait cycle that could trap the originating request in completion work. Managed-image metadata reads also move off the Gateway’s main thread.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Keep background media completion independent of its requester [#151056](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151056) — thanks @romneyda.

**Improvements**

- Move managed-image metadata reads off the Gateway thread [#150628](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/150628).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Visual pages in mixed PDFs">

[PDF extraction](/tools/pdf) keeps images of low-text or visual-only pages alongside selectable text, so a text-rich page no longer hides a neighboring diagram or scanned page. This affects extraction fallback; native Anthropic and Google PDF input is unchanged. More rendered pages can increase image-token charges, while the existing 20-page default selection, 200,000-character text cap, and shared four-million-pixel budget remain.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Preserve visual pages in mixed-text PDF extraction [#152614](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152614) — thanks @zhangguiping-xydt, @obviyus.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Logbook frame-range reads">

Logbook avoids fetching unused metadata when reading a range of frames, while returning the same public results.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Reduce unnecessary Logbook frame-range metadata [#153162](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153162).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Work conversations in Team Reports">

[Team Reports](/plugins/team-reports) adds a Work sessions directory and overview preview with links directly to current conversations. Member reports also link to conversations their members currently own. These are current work views, not a reconstruction of ownership during an older reporting period, and existing viewer permissions and exclusions for archived, incognito, automated, system, and hidden sessions still apply. Stored reports, transcripts, and full downloads are unchanged.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Open current work conversations from Reports [#154020](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/154020).
- Link member reports to current owned conversations [#154192](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/154192).
- Avoid loading unused Markdown for Team Reports reads [#153111](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153111).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Workboard context and board management">

Workboard loads the cards relevant to the selected work or conversation, so an unrelated damaged card no longer blocks those operations. Deleting an eligible empty board is also isolated from malformed notification subscriptions on other boards. Errors in selected cards still surface, and default or nonempty boards remain protected from deletion.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Load only relevant Workboard cards for context and capture [#153958](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153958).

**Bug fixes**

- Isolate empty-board deletion from other boards' subscriptions [#152102](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152102).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Browsing Session Share lists">

Session Share reads transcript-derived titles for the page you are browsing and batches creator lookups, reducing unnecessary reads while keeping the same discovery permissions and labels. Update the plugin on the source node to receive the page-scoped behavior; updating only the receiving side is insufficient. Searching still resolves titles before filtering.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Avoid off-page transcript reads in Session Share lists [#154249](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/154249).

**Improvements**

- Reduce repeated creator lookups in shared-session lists [#151681](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151681).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="DuckDuckGo result preparation">

DuckDuckGo search stops parsing additional results once it has the requested valid entries, preserving their returned text and snippets. The full response is still received and checked for bot challenges.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Avoid parsing surplus DuckDuckGo results [#152344](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152344).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="ClickClack discussion shutdown">

Stopping or restarting ClickClack waits for accepted room creation and persistence to finish, helping those operations complete before the integration closes.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Finish accepted ClickClack operations before shutdown [#151978](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151978).

</details>

</Accordion>

</AccordionGroup>

## Security and Privacy

Permission checks now cover more of the moments when a chat starts, a file is read, or background work continues after its original request. These changes also make credential repair and access changes clearer for people operating OpenClaw.

<AccordionGroup>

<Accordion title="Native-agent consent before sending">

A native-agent chat now asks for the required confirmation before saving or running its first message. An eligible administrator can choose **Continue for this chat**, which grants Full Access and disables optional sandboxing for that chat, then retries the original message once while keeping an inherited model selection. Declining leaves the message unsent, although an empty chat may remain. Consent applies only to that chat and runtime, and cannot waive mandatory sandbox, workspace, or remote-execution restrictions. The [native-agent permission guide](/tools/acp-agents-setup#permissions-for-native-chat-runtimes) explains this choice.

Native hook responses arriving after their deadline also return a timeout instead of a late success. Default Codex permission checks deny on that timeout; this does not cancel work already running on the server.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Confirm native-agent permissions before the first message [#153805](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153805) — thanks @obviyus.
- Reject late native hook responses [#150712](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/150712) — thanks @anyech and @fuller-stack-dev.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Operator commands and agent attribution">

Standalone tool calls now follow an authenticated operator's agent and sandbox restrictions even when no saved session exists. Automation that relied on that exception may now be refused; callers required to use a sandbox must first create and target a recorded sandboxed session. Agent shell scripts also need to send reports through available attributed session tools or normal subagent completion, so their messages retain their agent origin. The shell marker used for this check is not authentication or isolation from other processes owned by the same user.

Command errors now distinguish an unsupported command form from a human denial and explain how to retry through the approval flow. Unsupported forms remain blocked. The [approvals guide](/cli/approvals) also clarifies the existing `approvals get --node` and `approvals set --node` commands, with `--file` or `--stdin` supplying a replacement policy.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Security and trust**

- Apply operator limits to tools invoked without stored sessions [#153881](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153881) — thanks @shakkernerd.

**Bug fixes**

- Distinguish unsupported command preparation from a user-denied approval [#144869](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144869) — thanks @Alix-007, @obviyus, and @cipp-ashe.

**Documentation**

- Correct node approval inspection and replacement examples [#153716](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153716) — thanks @harshitgupta31415 and @yetval.

**Limits and compatibility**

- Require attributed delivery for agent shell messages [#150499](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/150499) — thanks @RomneyDa.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Sandboxed projects and file access">

Sandboxed sessions can work on an authorized registered project with `worktree: true` in a private Docker or Podman checkout, with accepted changes returned to the managed worktree. Host Git credentials and ignored provisioning stay outside that checkout. Explicit read-only access remains read-only, and unsupported backends refuse this workflow. Later files created only on the host need `git add` before entering an existing private checkout. Let pending changes finish before downgrading, and use a supporting version to recover unfinished work, as described in [managed project workspaces](/gateway/sandboxing/workspace-access#managed-project-workspaces).

File patches also recheck permission before changing files, and operator logs explain which workspace restriction rejected a patch without relaxing required roots. Automatic cleanup now follows each sandbox owner's retention settings, so one agent's shorter retention window does not prematurely remove another's working environment.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Work on managed projects inside private Docker and Podman sandboxes [#152641](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152641).
- Explain the workspace restriction behind rejected patches [#136126](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/136126) — thanks @Marvinthebored, @Peetiegonzalez, @shakkernerd, @Haderach-Ram, and @BottaniCals.

**Security and trust**

- Correct literal-tilde and same-file alias patches, recheck file permissions before mutation, reject OpenShell local access failures before remote creation, and stream ONNX verification within a memory bound [#154201](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/154201).

**Bug fixes**

- Preserve Linux sandbox transport priority under memory pressure without changing the backend's workload policy [#142764](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/142764) — thanks @ayoakouh, @obviyus, and @mrunalp.
- Honor each sandbox owner's retention policy during cleanup, with shared runtimes following global settings [#153752](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153752) — thanks @vincentkoc.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Credential repair and background command access">

Saving settings that display hidden secrets now preserves their real values, and OpenClaw rejects known redaction placeholders as credentials. If a stored Gateway token was already replaced by a placeholder, install this fix and run `openclaw doctor --fix` or `openclaw doctor --generate-gateway-token`. [Doctor verifies a backup before repairing the token](/cli/doctor/recovery#invalid-gateway-tokens); then restart the Gateway and reconnect or re-pair devices with the replacement. Other corrupted secrets need a real replacement at their source.

Background commands using the optional secret proxy on the Gateway host now keep HTTPS access after the originating turn ends. Each command retains its original secrets and allowed destinations until it stops. After changing credentials or bindings, stop older commands to revoke their access immediately and start a new run and command to use the changes. Exit, cancellation, timeout, and Gateway shutdown also revoke access. This proxy does not cover sandbox, remote-node, or provider-native shell execution.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Security and trust**

- Preserve credentials across redacted saves and repair placeholder tokens [#151250](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151250) — thanks @wrgrant.

**Bug fixes**

- Keep background command proxy access until the command stops, with independent access for sibling commands [#152557](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152557).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Chat media and reset permissions">

Chat images, documents, and trusted audio now retain the requesting sender's file restrictions throughout preparation.
