Pending reads and copies stop when access is revoked, the workspace or execution location changes, or the turn is canceled, and rejected preparation removes its newly staged files. Already-produced text remains available, while previously retained attachments keep their existing retention rules. These boundaries are covered in the [Control UI security model](/web/control-ui/security-model#assistant-media-route-auth).

In Discord, `/new` and `/reset` in unbound channels now respect channel disablement, allowlists, group policy, and server command authorization before changing a session. Previously bypassed commands may now be refused, while existing configured-binding recovery exceptions remain available.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Security and trust**

- Stop pending chat media reads when access changes [#152219](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152219).
- Restore Discord access checks for unbound reset commands [#152991](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152991) — thanks @yetval.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Account identity and Visitor Access">

[Cloudflare Access OIDC sign-in](/gateway/cloudflare-access#oidc-sign-in-and-existing-people) can reuse an existing OpenClaw profile, role, and history when the verified email matches a linked alias. Cloudflare admission and identity-provider email verification remain required; an unmatched email gets a separate profile with the configured default role. Tailscale profile-picture adoption also moves its database work off the main Gateway thread while preserving a picture you have explicitly chosen.

For the private, source-built Team plugin's Visitor Access feature, failed revocations are now kept for startup or hourly cleanup to retry. This plugin is excluded from npm, and access can remain active until removal succeeds. The repair does not invalidate existing logins or cancel running work.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Reuse matching profiles for Cloudflare OIDC sign-ins [#153556](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153556).

**Improvements**

- Save adopted Tailscale avatars in a worker without overwriting explicit choices, including an explicitly empty image [#152670](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152670).

**Security and trust**

- Retry failed Visitor Access revocations [#153878](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153878) — thanks @shakkernerd.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Session isolation">

Switching session stores now stops delayed child-agent activity from bringing the old store's context into a new conversation. Changing stores does not migrate conversations, and suspended child-result delivery needs explicit recovery even if you later select the old store again. Completed task results remain available; old watches whose store ownership is unknown need fresh registration. Separately, creating an incognito session with an explicit key checks for a collision without scanning unrelated durable history, while preserving existing isolation and authorization rules.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Security and trust**

- Keep child notifications and context in their original session store [#152828](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152828).

**Improvements**

- Use exact-key checks for requested incognito sessions [#152617](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152617).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Redaction and audit processing">

Secret masking avoids repeated scans of unchanged tool results and skips AWS credential checks that cannot match, while changes to output or redaction policy still trigger fresh checks. Transcript saving keeps its separate sanitization. Queued [audit history](/gateway/audit) writes and retention cleanup also move off the main Gateway thread. Audit collection remains best effort, so queue saturation, storage failures, or shutdown deadlines can still drop waiting metadata.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Skip impossible AWS credential matches during redaction without changing masking coverage [#152484](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152484).
- Reuse sanitized tool results without skipping changes to output or policy [#151638](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151638).

**Bug fixes**

- Move queued audit writes and cleanup off the Gateway thread [#152745](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152745).

</details>

</Accordion>

</AccordionGroup>

## Quality-of-Life Improvements

Everyday work gets clearer progress and less repeated preparation around tools, replies, and stored conversations.

<AccordionGroup>

<Accordion title="Following through on instructions">

Shared agent instructions more explicitly connect your corrections and an agent's promise to check something with continuing the authorized work. They also tell an agent receiving delegated results to address fixable failed checks before stopping, while preserving approval requirements, pauses, and cancellations. These are guidance changes, so individual model behavior can still vary.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Strengthen agent guidance to act on corrections and progress promises [#152833](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152833) — @vacinc

**Bug fixes**

- Preserve follow-through guidance after delegated batches [#153441](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153441) — @vacinc

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Workspace files and prompt inclusion">

Configuration help now explains that `skipBootstrap` controls creating workspace files, while existing files can still enter the prompt. The [workspace settings reference](/gateway/config-agents/workspace-and-bootstrap) clarifies the existing `contextInjection` choices for the embedded runtime, including `never`. Those choices do not control ordinary CLI-backed prompt preparation or prevent an agent from reading files with tools.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Documentation**

- Clarify workspace-file creation and existing prompt-injection controls [#118755](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/118755) — @camerono

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Progress and tool outcomes">

Progress and conversation history suppress repetitive checks while keeping useful updates and distinct failed, blocked, completed, or unknown tool outcomes recognizable. Your channel's visibility settings still apply, so Telegram quiet mode can hide intermediate tool failures unless tool progress is enabled.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Quiet repetitive polling and show accurate tool outcomes [#151456](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151456) — @obviyus

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Command help and read-only listings">

Help and read-only commands for hooks, nodes, sandboxes, and worktrees load less unused code. JSON agent and session listings also avoid repeated lookups or terminal-only display preparation while preserving their output.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Batch creation-history lookups for JSON agent lists [#151471](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151471)
- Defer installer loading for read-only hook reports [#152433](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152433)
- Defer unused hosting code in node commands [#152448](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152448)
- Defer unused sandbox command loading [#152688](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152688)
- Load worktree operations only when needed [#152730](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152730)
- Reduce presentation work in JSON session listings [#152109](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152109)

**Limits and compatibility**

Reduced loading does not make every command faster. Sandbox explain was slower in recorded comparisons, and worktree cleanup has no established speed improvement.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Terminal input and rendering">

Remote terminal typing and paste require less preparation, while tool-output redraws and scrollback reading avoid repeated work. On macOS and Linux, a terminal launched through Bun can also find a real Node installation when a shim hides it, keeping input and cursor keys working. A real Node installation is still required.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Reduce remote terminal input and paste processing [#151982](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151982)
- Reuse prepared tool output during terminal activity updates [#152008](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152008)
- Reduce temporary allocations when reading terminal scrollback [#152007](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152007)
- Reduce redraw work for tool output without right-to-left text [#152867](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152867)

**Bug fixes**

- Keep Bun-launched terminals interactive when Node is shadowed [#151750](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151750)

**Limits and compatibility**

Rendering measurements vary by content, with some right-to-left text and link cases taking longer. These changes do not establish a uniform terminal speed or memory improvement.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Tool previews and unknown-tool feedback">

Compact tool previews omit extreme array nesting instead of overflowing the formatter, without changing the original arguments used to run the tool. Plugin authors using the [display metadata helper](/plugins/sdk-agent-harness/sessions-and-results) should expect values beyond 64 array levels to be omitted and a possible `undefined` result. Suggestions after an unknown-tool request also avoid repeated lookup preparation.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Reduce repeated work when suggesting alternatives to unknown tools [#152345](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152345)

**Limits and compatibility**

- Bound deeply nested arrays in compact tool previews [#152923](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152923) — @wangmiao0668000666, @obviyus, @hpyhandsome; name-only coauthor nebulacoder-v8.0

The display helper is intentionally lossy and is not a general limit on argument traversal. Keep the original arguments for validation and execution.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Preparing tool context">

When messaging tools offer only sending and broadcasting, their definitions take up less model context while keeping supported options. Automation descriptions also remove repeated argument lists, and large tool outputs reuse valid text counts during context checks and trimming. Context limits and trimming rules stay the same.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Reuse unchanged tool-output text estimates [#152501](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152501)
- Avoid repeated scans when trimming large tool results [#153551](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153551)
- Reduce messaging and automation tool-definition overhead [#153841](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153841) — @obviyus

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Preparing agent and media requests">

Agent requests skip unused diagnostics when debug logging is off, and media progress sections share a fresh task read within each turn. Large-request diagnostics and shell setup also avoid some repeated preparation before execution; these changes concern local setup work rather than media generation speed.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Skip unused agent setup diagnostics [#150361](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/150361) — @vincentkoc
- Reduce repeated allocation when measuring large model payloads [#153821](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153821)
- Share fresh task reads across media prompt sections [#153656](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153656)
- Launch shell snapshot helpers through the spawn broker [#151711](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151711)

**Limits and compatibility**

Small diagnostic inputs can cost slightly more to process. Shell preparation retains its capture deadline, cleanup, authorization checks, and native fallback.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Processing long streamed replies">

OpenClaw avoids some repeated local cleanup and rendering while receiving long replies containing comparison text or final-answer tags. The benefit depends on the reply, and the final-answer path can use more peak memory while reducing repeated processing.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Reduce filtering work for streamed comparison text [#153944](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153944)
- Reduce repeated processing of streamed final replies [#154292](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/154292)

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Reusing database workers and connections">

Repeated conversation and state reads reuse eligible database connections and workers instead of repeatedly opening them. Healthy reusable resources can stay available for 30 idle minutes, reducing setup during intermittent activity while reads continue to see current data. Larger active installations can consequently retain more memory and open file handles during that window.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Reuse idle SQLite connections for repeated state operations [#153601](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153601)
- Reuse host verification when native workers open databases [#153581](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153581)
- Retain healthy shared-state workers across short idle periods [#152341](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152341)
- Reuse prepared queries in retained session readers [#152610](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152610)
- Skip redundant maintenance planning after warm writes [#152621](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152621)
- Reuse bounded Node workers for fresh state reads [#151929](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151929)
- Offload cached snapshot ownership bookkeeping [#153112](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153112)

**Limits and compatibility**

Incognito connections require explicit disposal because they hold the only copy of their data. Bun keeps separate worker lifetimes, unhealthy workers can retire earlier, and maintenance still runs when resources are cold, expired, or under capacity pressure. Stored-data retention rules are unchanged.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Serving other work during database activity">

Bursts of conversation writes leave more opportunities for other work to proceed, and saving large replies or tool results shortens the time other writers can be held up. Small requests arriving during startup or reconnect can wait up to 10 seconds for capacity instead of failing immediately, while memory and oversized-request limits still apply. Lookups through large task and conversation histories also inspect fewer unrelated records.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Shorten database writer holds when saving large replies and tool results [#153818](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153818) — @todddickerson
- Reduce database work for large task and session histories [#153548](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153548)

**Bug fixes**

- Reduce I/O stalls during concurrent agent database writes [#151909](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151909)
- Wait briefly for SQLite capacity during request bursts [#153437](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153437)

**Limits and compatibility**

New indexes install automatically when databases open for writing and require construction time, temporary disk space, and additional work on future writes. Large-message preparation can still be synchronous, and sustained overload can still fail when the waiting deadline expires.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Publishing live session updates">

Live conversation updates avoid preparing duplicate message data that would be replaced before delivery. Each recipient still receives the version allowed by their permissions, with the existing per-client preparation intact.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Avoid duplicate encoding of live session messages [#153122](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153122)
- Avoid redundant session snapshot encoding [#153006](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153006)

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Home awareness of new sessions">

Creating an eligible session now queues a notice for its agent's [Home conversation](/concepts/main-session), including the session key and available title, creator, and creation source without copying messages. This is enabled by default, and you can opt out with `session.notifyOnCreate: false` in [session settings](/gateway/config-agents/sessions). Home receives the notice on its next turn or scheduled heartbeat, without an immediate wake-up or ongoing monitoring of that conversation.

Drafts, incognito sessions, hidden internal sessions, scheduled cron runs, and Home itself are excluded. Reopening or resetting an existing session does not send another notice, and pending notices do not survive a restart.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Give Home awareness of newly created sessions [#152068](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152068)

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Illustrations in older release documentation">

The [2026.9.1 release guide](/releases/2026.9.1) gains interface illustrations and labeled conceptual examples to explain that older release. The figures document existing capabilities, with fictional and conceptual examples identified as such.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Documentation**

- Illustrate the historical 2026.9.1 release guide [#152988](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152988)

</details>

</Accordion>

</AccordionGroup>

## Other Bug Fixes

These fixes address interrupted work, misleading command failures, and database waits that could get in the way of everyday use.

<AccordionGroup>

<Accordion title="Canceling work and finishing cleanup">

Stopping a chat turn now interrupts optional idle waits and affected cleanup reads that were waiting on a stalled history rebuild. Required saves and cleanup still run, saved answers and attachment references remain intact, and an interrupted delivery check stays pending until its outcome is known.

Manually canceled [background commands](/gateway/background-process) no longer trigger an unwanted follow-up reply just because they produced some output. Their logs remain available through `process poll` and `process log`, and cleanup failures still notify. Confirmed process cleanup is retained accurately, while an unconfirmed stop remains uncertain.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Interrupt optional cleanup waits when an agent turn is canceled [#149755](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/149755). Thanks @romneyda.
- Avoid unsolicited replies after canceling background commands [#153795](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153795). Thanks @vacinc.
- Authorize local shared-credential session cancellation for implicit-scope CLI calls, retaining explicit scope restrictions and remote authorization [#153332](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153332). Thanks @vacinc.
- Let canceled chat turns finish cleanup during history rebuilds while preserving saved answers and pending delivery [#153778](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153778). Thanks @vincentkoc.
- Preserve confirmed process cleanup after cancellation without treating canceled commands as successful execution [#152107](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152107).
- Prevent canceled worker exchanges from hanging shutdown while retaining resources until cleanup and worker exit [#152994](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152994).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Command results and edit input">

Commands that stop reading their input early now return their actual exit status and error output, avoiding misleading broken-pipe failures. File edits also recover from limited newline and backslash encoding mistakes in replacement lists sent as strings, while preserving the intended literal text. Incomplete lists and malformed outer requests still fail validation without applying a partial edit.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Preserve command results when children close input early, while keeping input-admission rejection and other input faults as failures [#153644](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153644). Thanks @stackingrockss.
- Recover limited string-encoding mistakes in edit replacement lists without changing valid literal text or whole-set validation [#153692](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153692). Thanks @wangmiao0668000666, @obviyus, and @aniruddhaadak80.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Database activity and session reads">

Optional archive cleanup now waits for a later opportunity when the database is busy, instead of holding up OpenClaw. Work already accepted can finish before a database worker closes, and saving device tokens or task information avoids additional waits on the main process. State directories reached through filesystem aliases, such as macOS `/var` and `/private/var`, also avoid a timeout caused by treating those paths as different locations. Fully received results survive cleanup failures, while writes with an unknown outcome are not automatically repeated.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Avoid archive-cleanup lock stalls and preserve known subagent completion outcomes through transient save failures in the live process [#151594](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151594). Thanks @laurenceputra.
- Drain accepted database work before retiring shared workers and allow fresh calls after cleanup, retaining failed cleanup for explicit retry [#152873](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152873).
- Tolerate changes limited to `lastReadAt` in prepared session metadata and model-catalog reads [#152827](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152827).
- Prevent worker write timeouts through state-directory aliases, including macOS `/var` and `/private/var`, while retaining write authorization [#152190](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152190).
- Avoid host-thread database waits during asynchronous state operations and retain completely received outcomes through cleanup failures [#152377](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152377).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Intact shortened diagnostics">

Shortened update Doctor warnings and Skill Workshop error records avoid leaving half of an emoji at the cutoff. An affected emoji is omitted whole, so shortening the message does not introduce a broken-character symbol.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Preserve valid Unicode surrogate pairs when shortening update warnings and Skill Workshop errors, without changing the existing text limits [#150837](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/150837). Thanks @xialonglee.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Command validation and diagnostics">

Commands give more useful feedback for invalid agent selections and empty node invocation keys, and plugin and skill listings preserve version labels without adding duplicate prefixes. If a request times out after being sent, the message now advises checking its current state before retrying, because the operation may already have completed.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Display plugin and skill version labels without duplicate prefixes [#145975](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145975)
- Explain uncertain outcomes after Gateway request timeouts [#153370](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153370) — @liuwqgit, @obviyus, @deregtx
- Remove C1 control characters from agent console diagnostics [#149075](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/149075) — @vincentkoc
- Report empty node invocation keys before Gateway lookup [#145032](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145032) — @wangmiao0668000666, @obviyus

**Limits and compatibility**

- Reject invalid agent selectors before routing [#153387](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153387)

Strict callers must supply a valid agent selector or omit it. Deprecated SDK aliases retain their blank-as-omitted behavior through the documented November 29, 2026 compatibility window. A timeout alone establishes neither success nor failure.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Returning to the intended terminal conversation">

The [terminal interface](/web/tui) restores your remembered conversation even when newer conversations have similar names, and can show its model details without relying on a short search-results list. After reconnecting, old connection responses can no longer replace the current details. Existing agent scope and hidden-conversation rules still apply.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Restore the intended TUI conversation despite similar names [#153596](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153596)

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Portable terminal upload names">

When a long terminal-upload filename needs shortening, the resulting name still follows the portable rules for reserved names and trailing characters. This applies to newly staged uploads and leaves existing filenames alone.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Preserve portable terminal-upload names after truncation [#153023](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153023) — @goutamadwant, @gabrielnkl, @obviyus

</details>

</Accordion>

</AccordionGroup>

<Accordion title="Reverted changes and retained contributor credit">

The temporary extension of tool and model duration histograms was reverted, leaving the existing finite buckets ending at ten seconds. Anyone who deployed the brief main-branch extension should compare common bucket boundaries or keep observations separate across the rollback. The source list also retains credit for a codec-import repair whose merge added no further changes.

<details class="release-source-toggle">

Swift and Android tests reuse preparation and wait for observable readiness, while Apple catalog checks follow the current app text. Windows lifecycle fixtures retain their compiled files until cleanup completes.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Build, typecheck and source-checkout tooling">

Fresh source dependency installs use cloned or copied package files so an install in another checkout cannot falsely invalidate active compiler inputs. Existing hardlinked installs need a fresh checkout and install, and copy fallback can use more disk space, as explained in the [local testing guide](/reference/test/local). Build and typecheck fixes also cover Windows paths with spaces, selected compiler runtimes and custom Control UI output directories.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Reuse the build wrapper's option scanner [#152293](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152293). Thanks @vincentkoc.
- Run compiler shards through the native loader [#153446](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153446).
- Extend type-aware lint to CommonJS test support [#151385](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151385).
- Keep UI stylesheet validation scoped to relevant test graphs [#153630](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153630).

**Bug fixes**

- Correct configuration include validator imports [#153007](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153007). Thanks @fuller-stack-dev.
- Preserve the selected compiler runtime in Bun-launched builds [#151735](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151735).
- Keep Node-specific development checks on Node under Bun [#151772](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151772).
- Finalize Control UI assets in custom build destinations [#153571](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153571).
- Honor configured Mac worker-packaging timeouts [#153380](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153380).
- Record the temporary message-helper export allowance later narrowed to the pinned surface [#151881](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151881).

- Isolate fresh dependency installs across source checkouts [#154096](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/154096).
- Run Windows developer checks from paths containing spaces [#151701](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151701).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Subprocess, signal and cleanup tests">

Process tests wait for the intended child to start and finish, use the required runtime and retain diagnostics when cleanup is incomplete. Controlled clocks remove fixture delays while preserving production timeout settings.

<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Await node-worker recovery completion in tests [#153398](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153398).

- Capture operating-system evidence for stalled CLI tests [#151946](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151946).
- Record test-worker exit entry for the current stop request [#153050](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153050).
- Advance watchdog test deadlines with a controlled clock [#152922](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152922).
- Isolate shell fixture logging dependencies [#152431](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152431).
- Skip impossible renderer waits in Crabbox tests [#154012](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/154012).

<Accordion title="PR preparation, review and merge tools">

PR tools provide clearer checkout, review and merge diagnostics and avoid repeated GitHub metadata requests. Supported REST paths can continue selected operations after GraphQL quota exhaustion, with reviewed-revision and merge-approval requirements still enforced.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Read source files concurrently in coercion-helper checks [#151722](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151722).
- Reuse plugin-boundary source scans [#151426](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151426). Thanks @RomneyDa.
- Share grouped rendering for developer test reports [#149078](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/149078). Thanks @vincentkoc.
- Share test-report comparison pairing [#149142](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/149142). Thanks @vincentkoc.
- Reduce commit-hook formatter resource demand [#152297](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152297).
- Reduce GitHub API work during PR landing [#154040](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/154040).
- Explain merge aborts and invalid review fields [#151996](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151996).
- Use REST metadata for CI-only PR polling [#152697](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152697).
- Show rejected merge observations [#150647](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/150647).
- Reduce GitHub reads during PR preparation and polling [#153424](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153424).
- Keep PR workflows working across host tooling differences [#152078](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152078).
- Reuse REST metadata when attaching the CI watcher [#152893](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152893).
- Synchronize AutoReview Git startup safeguards [#152039](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152039).

**Bug fixes**

- Reject incomplete CI success and trim maintainer API reads [#153880](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153880).
- Report unsupported Python before workflow-check bootstrap [#151222](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151222). Thanks @wangmiao0668000666, @altaywtf, @vincentkoc.
- Run line-cap checks inside ignored temporary directories [#151748](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151748).
- Permit ordinary reviewed PR merges while main advances [d01d83b7](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/d01d83b79b18fd7700648e97e54db9e133bca46d). Thanks Peter Steinberger.

- Correct failed-request quota diagnostics in PR tooling [#152723](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152723).
- Use REST for PR identity and reviewer assignment [#153296](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153296).
- Continue CI monitoring and eligible squash landing after GraphQL quota exhaustion [#153563](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153563).

</details>

</Accordion>

- Run Node recovery fixtures with Node [#151976](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151976).
- Select Node explicitly for built recovery tests [#152162](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152162).
- Repair and speed up service-update replacement tests [#154263](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/154263).
- Preserve workspace aliases in release-metadata fixtures [#152486](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152486).
- Restore Doctor fixture coverage under Bun [#152587](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152587).
- Use supported Bash in macOS workflow fixtures [#152619](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152619).
- Prevent inode reuse from destabilizing backup tests [#152795](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152795).
- Make service drop-in fixtures independent of permissive umasks [#152807](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152807).
- Use Node for upgrade diagnostic test children [#151286](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151286).
- Protect update-aware plugin migration guidance [#153951](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153951). Thanks @vincentkoc.
- Allow fixture companion reinstall after Doctor preparation [#153382](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153382).
- Recognize compiled plugins in upgrade validation [#153410](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153410).
- Allow one required baseline restart in upgrade validation [#151607](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151607).
- Separate title repair from worktree upgrade verification [#153350](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153350).
- Isolate installer PATH tests from installed Node [#154186](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/154186).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Local test preparation and planning">

Local test tools reuse verified compiled workers where supported and keep large test inventories complete. An optional offline Gateway test path is available for trusted, keyless tests on Linux with rootless Podman and a matching prepared image and toolchain; it does not support SELinux, external network access or automatic fallback to host execution.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Add opt-in offline Gateway tests for trusted keyless fixtures on prepared Linux rootless Podman hosts [#152318](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152318).
- Reuse verified compiled workers across local test runs [#153558](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153558).
- Check directory exclusions first during test planning [#151669](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151669).
- Remove duplicate tooling-routing test coverage [#151592](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151592).
- Consolidate captured CI watcher fixtures [#152930](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152930).
- Remove duplicate build-cache test assertions [#153101](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153101).
- Reduce Git fixture copying overhead [#154222](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/154222).
- Reuse selection patterns when planning shared-helper tests [#153650](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153650).
- Reduce repeated tooling-test compilation and readiness waits [#153685](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153685).
- Reuse compiled worker fixtures in lifecycle tests [#151389](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151389).

**Bug fixes**

- Restore guest-command transport for qualified Parallels test hosts [#153469](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153469).
- Restore test cache reuse and consistent UI dependencies [#154121](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/154121).
- Preserve complete test discovery for large Git inventories [#151991](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151991).
- Restore compile-cache access in Bun worker test mocks [#152956](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152956).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Localization tooling and catalog cleanup">

Localization maintenance removes entries whose interface text has already been retired and shares Android quote-validation logic. Active translations remain unchanged.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Synchronize locale metadata after workspace-label removal [#152977](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152977).
- Remove retired native translation entries [#153626](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153626).
- Share quote handling in Android translation checks [#149090](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/149090). Thanks @vincentkoc.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Memory indexing and result internals">

Memory file watching is separated from indexing, and diagnostics and search-result preparation reuse existing information. This is maintenance of the current Memory implementation.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Extract Memory file watching from indexing [#152631](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152631). Thanks @Kimiyu-186.
- Avoid repeated workspace lookups in memory diagnostics [#152040](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152040).
- Consolidate memory search result preparation [#152858](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152858).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Model catalog and provider processing internals">

Model and provider processing avoid repeated catalog searches, metadata preparation and response parsing. Selection precedence, permission checks, provider output and fallback rules remain intact.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Prepare shared authentication labels once per catalog publication [#152859](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152859).
- Reuse prepared model metadata before ambient state reads [#152119](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152119).
- Stop model catalog scans at the first literal match [#152128](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152128).
- Skip impossible provider ownership scans [#152158](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152158).
- Reuse prepared provider-authentication declarations [#151570](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151570).
- Stop catalog template searches after the preferred match [#151616](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151616). Thanks @vincentkoc.
- Reduce work in pending model-catalog status checks [#151819](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151819).
- Reduce routine Codex request-cleanup overhead [#152479](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152479).
- Index repeated provider and alias lookups [#152495](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152495).
- Simplify xAI response citation selection [#152686](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152686).
- Simplify Kimi citation collection [#152692](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152692).
- Reuse provider-option locale comparison [#151066](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151066).
- Defer provider sign-in metadata construction until selection [#151943](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151943).
- Avoid reparsing complete streamed tool arguments [#152904](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152904).
- Simplify compatible-provider stream readability checks [#152401](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152401).
- Skip redundant scans of ordinary completion chunks [#152549](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152549).
- Select Parallel MCP responses without an intermediate array [#152685](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152685).
- Derive provider request types from resolver results [#150413](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/150413). Thanks @RomneyDa.
- Simplify model retry and recovery decisions [#151995](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151995).
- Normalize only selected Firecrawl search results [#152348](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152348).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Shared native-client code and contracts">

Shared native clients gain interfaces and cross-language contract checks for continued development, alongside consolidated Apple Watch status formatting. The shared-client groundwork does not activate a new macOS sidecar.

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Release preparation, publication and recovery tools">

Release tools improve package checks, publication readback and recovery of interrupted work while preserving exact-package verification and explicit approvals. Optional early activation remains off by default and is limited to direct core publication; using it can leave the release page public while Docker distribution is still incomplete.

<Accordion title="Performance benchmarks and measurement controls">

Benchmarks distinguish startup preparation from completed work and retain more useful installed-package diagnostics. These changes improve how performance is measured and compared without establishing an application speedup.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Verify benchmark work and separate warmup and CPU placement [#149702](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/149702). Thanks @vincentkoc.
- Measure installed Gateway startup and shutdown on Windows [#151635](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151635).
- Compare installed Gateway startup packages on one Windows runner [#151697](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151697).
- Add optional CPU profiling for installed Gateway startup [#151825](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151825).
- Cover Gateway admission in catalog benchmarks [#151846](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151846).

**Bug fixes**

- Calibrate catalog performance checks to host CPU speed [#151732](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151732).
- Isolate catalog performance measurements from background work [#151832](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151832).
- Retain foreground scope during catalog benchmarks [#151840](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151840).
- Remove redundant catalog benchmark priority hold [#151892](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151892).
- Preserve valid installed-startup CPU diagnostic captures [#152888](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152888).
- Start installed Gateway benchmark fixtures under Bun [#152704](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152704).

</details>

</Accordion>

Plugin tests cover asset lookup across installation layouts, cleanup after failed preparation and package lifecycle behavior. Their fixtures isolate database state and temporary dependencies while retaining existing assertions.

Plugin loading and catalogs avoid repeated path resolution, unused metadata reads and temporary allocations. Existing tool behavior, migration history and execution ownership are preserved.

<Accordion title="Remote source validation and recovery">

Crabbox reports classified fetch failures without exposing raw private Git output and waits for cancellation cleanup. It can inspect and recover verified abandoned local staging, while keeping copies whose ownership or cleanup remains uncertain.

- Classify Crabbox fetch failures without raw Git output [#152138](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152138). Thanks @vincentkoc.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Skill installation and Workshop internals">

Skill installation separates file operations from policy, and status checks avoid unnecessary installer loading. Workshop review status and skill-filter checks reuse their existing inputs without changing installation or review behavior.
